id,english,transliteration,assamese
"0","I wake up early.","mai xonkāle xār pāoṃ.","মই সোনকালে সাৰ পাওঁ।"
"1","You wake up late.","tumi derikai xār pāy.","তুমি দেৰিকৈ সাৰ পায়।"
"2","He wakes up at six.","chay bajāt xār pāy.","ছয় বজাত সাৰ পায়।"
"3","She wakes up at seven.","xāt bajāt tāi xār pāy.","সাত বজাত তাই সাৰ পায়।"
"4","We wake up together.","āmi ekelge xār pāoṃ.","আমি একেলগে সাৰ পাওঁ।"
"5","They wake up at dawn.","bhorbhorāi xār pāy.","ভোৰভোৰাই সাৰ পায়।"
"6","I brush my teeth every morning.","pratidine rātipuwā dāṃt brāch karo.","প্ৰতিদিনে ৰাতিপুৱা দাঁত ব্ৰাছ কৰো।"
"7","She brushes her teeth after breakfast.","brekphāxṭa kari tāi dāṃt brāch kare.","ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট কৰি তাই দাঁত ব্ৰাছ কৰে।"
"8","He takes a shower before work.","kāmar āgte gā dhui lay.","কামৰ আগতে গা ধুই লয়।"
"9","We eat breakfast together.","āmi ekelge brekphāxṭa khāoṃ.","আমি একেলগে ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাওঁ।"
"10","I go to work every day.","pratidine kāmlai yāoṃ.","প্ৰতিদিনে কামলৈ যাওঁ।"
"11","She goes to school by bus.","tāi bāchere xkullai yāy.","তাই বাছেৰে স্কুললৈ যায়।"
"12","He walks to the office.","xi aphiclai khoj kāṛhi yāy.","সি অফিচলৈ খোজ কাঢ়ি যায়।"
"13","They drive to work.","gāṛī calāi kāmlai yāy.","গাড়ী চলাই কামলৈ যায়।"
"14","We take the train to the city.","āmi ṭreinat uṭhi caharlai yāoṃ.","আমি ট্ৰেইনত উঠি চহৰলৈ যাওঁ।"
"15","I come home at five.","pāṃc bajāt gharlai āhoṃ.","পাঁচ বজাত ঘৰলৈ আহোঁ।"
"16","She comes home late.","tāi derikai gharlai āhe.","তাই দেৰিকৈ ঘৰলৈ আহে।"
"17","He arrives at the office at eight.","āṭh bajāt aphicat upaxthit hay.","আঠ বজাত অফিচত উপস্থিত হয়।"
"18","I leave the house at seven.","xāt bajāt gharar parā olāi yāoṃ.","সাত বজাত ঘৰৰ পৰা ওলাই যাওঁ।"
"19","We leave for work together.","āmi ekelge kāmar bābe olāi yāoṃ.","আমি একেলগে কামৰ বাবে ওলাই যাওঁ।"
"20","I do not wake up early.","mai xonkāle xār nāpāoṃ.","মই সোনকালে সাৰ নাপাওঁ।"
"21","She does not take the bus.","tāi bāchat nāyāy.","তাই বাছত নাযায়।"
"22","He does not eat breakfast.","teoṃ brekphāxṭa nākhāy.","তেওঁ ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট নাখায়।"
"23","We do not drive to work.","āmi kāmlai gāṛī calāi nāyāoṃ.","আমি কামলৈ গাড়ী চলাই নাযাওঁ।"
"24","They do not walk to school.","xkullai khojkāṛhi nāyāy.","স্কুললৈ খোজকাঢ়ি নাযায়।"
"25","Do you wake up early?","xonkāle xār pāy neki?","সোনকালে সাৰ পায় নেকি?"
"26","Does he take a shower?","gā dhui neki?","গা ধুই নেকি?"
"27","Does she eat breakfast?","tāi brekphāxṭa khāy neki?","তাই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খায় নেকি?"
"28","What time do you wake up?","kimān bajāt xār pāy?","কিমান বজাত সাৰ পায়?"
"29","Where do you work?","āpuni ka't kām kare?","আপুনি ক'ত কাম কৰে?"
"30","How do you get to work?","kenekai kāmat xomāb?","কেনেকৈ কামত সোমাব?"
"31","When does he come home?","gharlai ketiyā āhe?","ঘৰলৈ কেতিয়া আহে?"
"32","Why do they leave early?","kiy xonkāle guci yāy?","কিয় সোনকালে গুচি যায়?"
"33","I woke up early yesterday.","kāli xonkāle xār pāichiloṃ.","কালি সোনকালে সাৰ পাইছিলোঁ।"
"34","She ate breakfast this morning.","āji rātipuwā tāi brekphāxṭa khāle.","আজি ৰাতিপুৱা তাই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খালে।"
"35","He walked to work yesterday.","kāli xi kāmlai khoj kāṛhi ga’l.","কালি সি কামলৈ খোজ কাঢ়ি গ’ল।"
"36","We took the bus last week.","āmi yowā xaptāhat bāchat uṭhiloṃ.","আমি যোৱা সপ্তাহত বাছত উঠিলোঁ।"
"37","They drove to the city.","gāṛīre caharlai ga’l.","গাড়ীৰে চহৰলৈ গ’ল।"
"38","I did not eat breakfast.","mai brekphāxṭa khowā nāchilo.","মই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খোৱা নাছিলো।"
"39","She did not go to work.","tāi kāmlai yowā nāchil.","তাই কামলৈ যোৱা নাছিল।"
"40","Did you wake up early?","xonkāle xār pāichilā neki?","সোনকালে সাৰ পাইছিলা নেকি?"
"41","Did he come home late?","derikai gharlai āhichil neki?","দেৰিকৈ ঘৰলৈ আহিছিল নেকি?"
"42","What did you eat for breakfast?","brekphāxṭat ki khāle?","ব্ৰেকফাষ্টত কি খালে?"
"43","I will wake up early tomorrow.","kāilai xonkāle xār pām.","কাইলৈ সোনকালে সাৰ পাম।"
"44","She will take the bus.","tāi bāchat uṭhib.","তাই বাছত উঠিব।"
"45","He will walk to work.","khojkāṛhi kāmlai yāb.","খোজকাঢ়ি কামলৈ যাব।"
"46","We will eat dinner together.","āmi ekelge rātir āhār khām.","আমি একেলগে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খাম।"
"47","They will come home late.","derikai gharlai āhib.","দেৰিকৈ ঘৰলৈ আহিব।"
"48","I will not go to work tomorrow.","kāilai mai kāmlai nāyāoṃ.","কাইলৈ মই কামলৈ নাযাওঁ।"
"49","Will you eat breakfast?","brekphāxṭa khābne?","ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাবনে?"
"50","When will she come home?","tāi ketiyā gharlai āhib?","তাই কেতিয়া ঘৰলৈ আহিব?"
"51","I am eating breakfast.","mai brekphāxṭa khāi ācho.","মই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাই আছো।"
"52","She is walking to school.","tāi khojkāṛhi xkullai gai āche.","তাই খোজকাঢ়ি স্কুললৈ গৈ আছে।"
"53","He is taking a shower.","gā dhui āche xi.","গা ধুই আছে সি।"
"54","We are getting ready.","āmi xāju hai ācho.","আমি সাজু হৈ আছো।"
"55","They are leaving now.","etiyā guci gaiche.","এতিয়া গুচি গৈছে।"
"56","I was eating when you called.","tumi phon karār xamyat mai khāi āchilo.","তুমি ফোন কৰাৰ সময়ত মই খাই আছিলো।"
"57","She was sleeping when I arrived.","mai āhi powār xamyat tāi xui āchil.","মই আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত তাই শুই আছিল।"
"58","I have already eaten.","itimadhye khāichoṃ.","ইতিমধ্যে খাইছোঁ।"
"59","She has already left.","tāi itimadhye guci gaiche.","তাই ইতিমধ্যে গুচি গৈছে।"
"60","He has not eaten yet.","etiyāo khowā nāi.","এতিয়াও খোৱা নাই।"
"61","We have been here since morning.","āmi rātipuwār parāi iyāt ācho.","আমি ৰাতিপুৱাৰ পৰাই ইয়াত আছো।"
"62","They had already left when I arrived.","mai āhi powār xamyat xihaṃt itimadhye guci gaichil.","মই আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত সিহঁত ইতিমধ্যে গুচি গৈছিল।"
"63","Wake up!","xār powā!","সাৰ পোৱা!"
"64","Do not be late!","deri nakribā!","দেৰি নকৰিবা!"
"65","Eat your breakfast.","āponār brekphāxṭa khāok.","আপোনাৰ ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাওক।"
"66","Go to bed early.","xonkāle xui yāok.","সোনকালে শুই যাওক।"
"67","Let us leave now.","etiyāi guci yāoṃ.","এতিয়াই গুচি যাওঁ।"
"68","I can wake up early.","mai xonkāle xār pāb pāro.","মই সোনকালে সাৰ পাব পাৰো।"
"69","She must go to work.","tāi kāmlai yābai lāgib.","তাই কামলৈ যাবই লাগিব।"
"70","He should eat breakfast.","teoṃ brekphāxṭa khāb lāge.","তেওঁ ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাব লাগে।"
"71","We need to leave now.","āmi etiyāi yāb lāgib.","আমি এতিয়াই যাব লাগিব।"
"72","You may stay home today.","āji hayto gharte thākibā.","আজি হয়তো ঘৰতে থাকিবা।"
"73","Can you help me?","mok xahāy karib pāribāne?","মোক সহায় কৰিব পাৰিবানে?"
"74","I cannot find my keys.","mor cābi bicāri powā nāi.","মোৰ চাবি বিচাৰি পোৱা নাই।"
"75","Breakfast is prepared by my mother.","brekphāxṭa mār dbārā praxtut karā hay.","ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট মাৰ দ্বাৰা প্ৰস্তুত কৰা হয়।"
"76","The house was cleaned yesterday.","kāli gharṭo cāphā karā hay.","কালি ঘৰটো চাফা কৰা হয়।"
"77","Dinner will be cooked by him.","rātir āhār teoṃ rāndhib.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ তেওঁ ৰান্ধিব।"
"78","If I wake up early, I will exercise.","xonkāle xār pāle byāyām karim.","সোনকালে সাৰ পালে ব্যায়াম কৰিম।"
"79","If she had time, she would cook breakfast.","xamay pāle tāi brekphāxṭa rāndhib.","সময় পালে তাই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট ৰান্ধিব।"
"80","If I had woken up earlier, I would not have been late.","āgte xār powā ha’le deri naha’lheṃten","আগতে সাৰ পোৱা হ’লে দেৰি নহ’লহেঁতেন৷"
"81","I wake up and I eat breakfast.","xār pāi brekphāxṭa khāoṃ.","সাৰ পাই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাওঁ।"
"82","He works but she studies.","xi kām kare kintu tāi paṛhe.","সি কাম কৰে কিন্তু তাই পঢ়ে।"
"83","I eat breakfast because I am hungry.","bhok lāgiche bābei brekphāxṭa khāoṃ.","ভোক লাগিছে বাবেই ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট খাওঁ।"
"84","She brushes her teeth before she leaves.","tāi yowār āgte dāṃt brāch kare.","তাই যোৱাৰ আগতে দাঁত ব্ৰাছ কৰে।"
"85","Although I am tired, I go to work.","yadio mai bhāgri paricho, tathāpio mai kāmlai yāoṃ.","যদিও মই ভাগৰি পৰিছো, তথাপিও মই কামলৈ যাওঁ।"
"86","This is my house.","eikhan mor ghar.","এইখন মোৰ ঘৰ।"
"87","That is her car.","xeikhnei tāir gāṛī.","সেইখনেই তাইৰ গাড়ী।"
"88","I dress myself.","mai nijke xāj-pochāk pindho.","মই নিজকে সাজ-পোছাক পিন্ধো।"
"89","He shaves himself every morning.","pratidine rātipuwā nijke culi kāṭi lay.","প্ৰতিদিনে ৰাতিপুৱা নিজকে চুলি কাটি লয়।"
"90","I wake up earlier than my brother.","bhāitkai āgtei xār pāoṃ.","ভাইতকৈ আগতেই সাৰ পাওঁ।"
"91","She is the fastest runner in our family.","āmār pariyālar āṭāitkai begī daurbid tāi.","আমাৰ পৰিয়ালৰ আটাইতকৈ বেগী দৌৰবিদ তাই।"
"92","Today is busier than yesterday.","kālir tulnāt āji adhik byaxta.","কালিৰ তুলনাত আজি অধিক ব্যস্ত।"
"93","There is a park near my house.","mor gharar ocarte ekhan pārka āche.","মোৰ ঘৰৰ ওচৰতে এখন পাৰ্ক আছে।"
"94","There are many people on the street.","rājapthat bahu mānuh.","ৰাজপথত বহু মানুহ।"
"95","Everyone goes to work.","xaklowe kāmlai yāy.","সকলোৱে কামলৈ যায়।"
"96","Nobody stays home during the week.","xaptāhṭot konowei gharat nāthāke.","সপ্তাহটোত কোনোৱেই ঘৰত নাথাকে।"
"97","Some people wake up very early.","kichumān mānuh khub xonkāle xār pāy.","কিছুমান মানুহ খুব সোনকালে সাৰ পায়।"
"98","The man who lives next door is a teacher.","kāxte thakā mānuhjan xikxak.","কাষতে থকা মানুহজন শিক্ষক।"
"99","The bus that I take is always late.","mai yowā bāchkhan xadāy deri hay.","মই যোৱা বাছখন সদায় দেৰি হয়।"
"100","The house where I grew up is very old.","mai ḍāṅar howā gharkhan bahut purṇi.","মই ডাঙৰ হোৱা ঘৰখন বহুত পুৰণি।"
"101","I always eat lunch at noon.","mai xadāy duprīyār āhār khāoṃ.","মই সদায় দুপৰীয়াৰ আহাৰ খাওঁ।"
"102","She sometimes walks to work.","tāi ketiyābā khojkāṛhi kāmlai yāy.","তাই কেতিয়াবা খোজকাঢ়ি কামলৈ যায়।"
"103","He never eats dinner late.","xi ketiyāo rātir āhār derikai nākhāy.","সি কেতিয়াও ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ দেৰিকৈ নাখায়।"
"104","We usually go to bed at ten.","āmi xādhāraṇte dah bajāt xui yāoṃ.","আমি সাধাৰণতে দহ বজাত শুই যাওঁ।"
"105","I brush my teeth twice a day.","dinṭot dubārkai dāṃt brāch karo.","দিনটোত দুবাৰকৈ দাঁত ব্ৰাছ কৰো।"
"106","The keys are on the table.","cābibor ṭebular oprat.","চাবিবোৰ টেবুলৰ ওপৰত।"
"107","My phone is in my bag.","mor phonṭo mor begat āche.","মোৰ ফোনটো মোৰ বেগত আছে।"
"108","The car is in front of the house.","gāṛīkhan gharar xanmukhat.","গাড়ীখন ঘৰৰ সন্মুখত।"
"109","The park is behind the school.","bidyālyar pichphāle pārkakhan.","বিদ্যালয়ৰ পিছফালে পাৰ্কখন।"
"110","I sit next to my colleague.","mai mor xahkarmījnar kāxat bahi thākoṃ.","মই মোৰ সহকৰ্মীজনৰ কাষত বহি থাকোঁ।"
"111","He said that he was tired.","teoṃ ka’le ye teoṃ bhāgri pariche.","তেওঁ ক’লে যে তেওঁ ভাগৰি পৰিছে।"
"112","She told me that she would be late.","tāi mok ka’le ye tāir deri ha’b.","তাই মোক ক’লে যে তাইৰ দেৰি হ’ব।"
"113","They asked if I was coming.","mai āhi ācho neki buli xudhile.","মই আহি আছো নেকি বুলি সুধিলে।"
"114","I clean the house on Saturdays.","xanibāre ghar cāphā kari laoṃ.","শনিবাৰে ঘৰ চাফা কৰি লওঁ।"
"115","She does the laundry every week.","tāi prati xaptāhat kāpor dhowār kām kare.","তাই প্ৰতি সপ্তাহত কাপোৰ ধোৱাৰ কাম কৰে।"
"116","He cooks dinner for the family.","teoṃ pariyālar bābe rātir āhār rāndhe.","তেওঁ পৰিয়ালৰ বাবে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধে।"
"117","We watch television in the evening.","āmi xandhiyā ṭelibhichan cāoṃ.","আমি সন্ধিয়া টেলিভিছন চাওঁ।"
"118","They play in the garden after school.","xkular parā āhi bārīt khele.","স্কুলৰ পৰা আহি বাৰীত খেলে।"
"119","I read a book before bed.","xui uṭhār āgte kitāp ekhan paṛhiloṃ.","শুই উঠাৰ আগতে কিতাপ এখন পঢ়িলোঁ।"
"120","She listens to music while cooking.","randhā-bṛhā kari thākoṃte tāi gān xuni thāke.","ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া কৰি থাকোঁতে তাই গান শুনি থাকে।"
"121","He takes the dog for a walk.","kukurṭok phuriblai lai yāy.","কুকুৰটোক ফুৰিবলৈ লৈ যায়।"
"122","I drink coffee every morning.","pratidine puwā kaphi khāoṃ.","প্ৰতিদিনে পুৱা কফি খাওঁ।"
"123","The children play outside.","la’rā-chowālībore bāhirat khele.","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰে বাহিৰত খেলে।"
"124","My mother cooks the best food.","māye uttam khādya rāndhe.","মায়ে উত্তম খাদ্য ৰান্ধে।"
"125","Our neighbor is very friendly.","āmār ocar-cuburīyājan bar bandhutbapūrṇa.","আমাৰ ওচৰ-চুবুৰীয়াজন বৰ বন্ধুত্বপূৰ্ণ।"
"126","I need to buy groceries.","gelāmālar baxtu kinib lāgib.","গেলামালৰ বস্তু কিনিব লাগিব।"
"127","She forgot her umbrella.","tāir chātiṭo pāhri ga’l.","তাইৰ ছাতিটো পাহৰি গ’ল।"
"128","The alarm clock rings at six.","chay bajāt elārma ghaṛīṭo bāji uṭhe.","ছয় বজাত এলাৰ্ম ঘড়ীটো বাজি উঠে।"
"129","I usually take a nap after lunch.","xādhāraṇte duprīyār āhār khowār pichat ṭopni laoṃ.","সাধাৰণতে দুপৰীয়াৰ আহাৰ খোৱাৰ পিছত টোপনি লওঁ।"
"130","He is getting dressed.","xāj-pochāk pindhiche.","সাজ-পোছাক পিন্ধিছে।"
"131","I have been living here for five years.","pāṃc bachar ha’l iyāt baxbāx karā.","পাঁচ বছৰ হ’ল ইয়াত বসবাস কৰা।"
"132","She has just arrived.","tāi mātra āhi pāiche.","তাই মাত্ৰ আহি পাইছে।"
"133","Where are my shoes?","mor jotāyor ka't?","মোৰ জোতাযোৰ ক'ত?"
"134","How many rooms does your house have?","āponār gharat kimān koṭhā āche?","আপোনাৰ ঘৰত কিমান কোঠা আছে?"
"135","I wish I could sleep more.","āru xub pārile bhāl āchil.","আৰু শুব পাৰিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"136","If it rains, I will stay home.","barxuṇ dile gharte thākim.","বৰষুণ দিলে ঘৰতে থাকিম।"
"137","Let me help you.","mai āponāk xahāy kari dioṃ.","মই আপোনাক সহায় কৰি দিওঁ।"
"138","The door was left open.","duwārkhan kholākai thai ga’l.","দুৱাৰখন খোলাকৈ থৈ গ’ল।"
"139","Someone knocked on the door.","konobāi duwārat ṭokar mārile.","কোনোবাই দুৱাৰত টোকৰ মাৰিলে।"
"140","I like rice.","bhāt mor bhāl lāge.","ভাত মোৰ ভাল লাগে।"
"141","She likes spicy food.","tāi ṭeṅā khādya bhāl pāy.","তাই টেঙা খাদ্য ভাল পায়।"
"142","He eats bread every day.","pratidine teoṃ ruṭi khāy.","প্ৰতিদিনে তেওঁ ৰুটি খায়।"
"143","We drink tea in the afternoon.","āmi duprīyā cāh khāoṃ.","আমি দুপৰীয়া চাহ খাওঁ।"
"144","They prefer coffee to tea.","cāhatkai kaphi bechi pachanda kare.","চাহতকৈ কফি বেছি পছন্দ কৰে।"
"145","The soup is hot.","cūpṭo garam hai āche.","চূপটো গৰম হৈ আছে।"
"146","The milk is cold.","gākhīr ṭhāṇḍā.","গাখীৰ ঠাণ্ডা।"
"147","The bread is fresh.","ruṭi xatej.","ৰুটি সতেজ।"
"148","The fruit is sweet.","phal miṭhā.","ফল মিঠা।"
"149","This food tastes delicious.","ei khādyar xowād xuxbādu.","এই খাদ্যৰ সোৱাদ সুস্বাদু।"
"150","I cook dinner every evening.","pratidine xandhiyā rātir āhār rāndho.","প্ৰতিদিনে সন্ধিয়া ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধো।"
"151","She is cooking lunch.","tāi duprīyār āhār rāndhi āche.","তাই দুপৰীয়াৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধি আছে।"
"152","He boils water for tea.","cāhar bābe pānī xijāy.","চাহৰ বাবে পানী সিজায়।"
"153","We bake bread on Sundays.","āmi deobāre ruṭi banāoṃ.","আমি দেওবাৰে ৰুটি বনাওঁ।"
"154","They fry the fish in oil.","māchbor telat bhāji diye.","মাছবোৰ তেলত ভাজি দিয়ে।"
"155","I cut the vegetables.","mai xāk-pācli kāṭiloṃ.","মই শাক-পাচলি কাটিলোঁ।"
"156","She stirs the sauce.","tāi cāṭnikhini larāi diye.","তাই চাটনিখিনি লৰাই দিয়ে।"
"157","He adds salt to the soup.","cūpat nimakh diye.","চূপত নিমখ দিয়ে।"
"158","We mix the ingredients together.","āmi upādānbor ekelge mihlāi laoṃ.","আমি উপাদানবোৰ একেলগে মিহলাই লওঁ।"
"159","The chef prepares the meal.","cephe khādya praxtut kare.","চেফে খাদ্য প্ৰস্তুত কৰে।"
"160","I do not eat meat.","mai māṃx nākhāoṃ.","মই মাংস নাখাওঁ।"
"161","She does not like onions.","tāi piṃyāj bhāl nāpāy.","তাই পিঁয়াজ ভাল নাপায়।"
"162","He never drinks alcohol.","teoṃ ketiyāo mad nākhāy.","তেওঁ কেতিয়াও মদ নাখায়।"
"163","We do not add sugar.","āmi ceni nido.","আমি চেনি নিদো।"
"164","The water is not boiling yet.","pānī etiyāo utli thakā nāi.","পানী এতিয়াও উতলি থকা নাই।"
"165","Do you like fish?","māch bhāl pāy neki?","মাছ ভাল পায় নেকি?"
"166","Does she eat vegetables?","tāi xāk-pācli khāy neki?","তাই শাক-পাচলি খায় নেকি?"
"167","What do you want to eat?","ki khāblai man gaiche?","কি খাবলৈ মন গৈছে?"
"168","How much salt should I add?","kimān nimakh dib lāge?","কিমান নিমখ দিব লাগে?"
"169","Is the food ready?","khādya reḍi haiche neki?","খাদ্য ৰেডি হৈছে নেকি?"
"170","What is this dish called?","ei khādyar nām ki?","এই খাদ্যৰ নাম কি?"
"171","Would you like some more?","āru kichumān bicāribne?","আৰু কিছুমান বিচাৰিবনে?"
"172","Can I have a glass of water?","egilāc pānī khāb pārimne?","এগিলাচ পানী খাব পাৰিমনে?"
"173","I cooked pasta last night.","yowā nixā pāxtā rāndhiloṃ.","যোৱা নিশা পাস্তা ৰান্ধিলোঁ।"
"174","She made a cake for the party.","tāi pārṭir bābe kek eṭā banāle.","তাই পাৰ্টিৰ বাবে কেক এটা বনালে।"
"175","He ate too much.","xi bechikai khāichil.","সি বেছিকৈ খাইছিল।"
"176","We had dinner at a restaurant.","āmi rexṭureṇṭa ekhnat rātir āhār khāloṃ","আমি ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্ট এখনত ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খালোঁ৷"
"177","They brought wine to the dinner.","rātir āhārat mad lai āhichil.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰত মদ লৈ আহিছিল।"
"178","I did not finish my meal.","mai mor āhār xex karā nāchilo.","মই মোৰ আহাৰ শেষ কৰা নাছিলো।"
"179","The soup was too salty.","cūpṭo bar nimkhīyā āchil.","চূপটো বৰ নিমখীয়া আছিল।"
"180","Did you enjoy the food?","khādya bhāl lāgil neki?","খাদ্য ভাল লাগিল নেকি?"
"181","What did she cook?","tāi ki rāndhile?","তাই কি ৰান্ধিলে?"
"182","I will cook dinner tonight.","āji rāti rātir āhār rāndhim.","আজি ৰাতি ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধিম।"
"183","She will bake a cake tomorrow.","kāilai tāi kek eṭā banāb.","কাইলৈ তাই কেক এটা বনাব।"
"184","We will eat at a restaurant.","āmi rexṭureṇṭa ekhnat khām.","আমি ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্ট এখনত খাম।"
"185","Will you try this dish?","ei khādyaṭo cexṭā karibne?","এই খাদ্যটো চেষ্টা কৰিবনে?"
"186","I am going to cook fish.","mai māch rāndhiblai olāicho.","মই মাছ ৰান্ধিবলৈ ওলাইছো।"
"187","She has never tasted this fruit.","tāi ketiyāo ei phalar xowād lowā nāi.","তাই কেতিয়াও এই ফলৰ সোৱাদ লোৱা নাই।"
"188","We have been cooking for two hours.","āmi dughaṇṭā dhari rāndhi ācho.","আমি দুঘণ্টা ধৰি ৰান্ধি আছো।"
"189","The food has gotten cold.","khādya ṭhāṇḍā hai gaiche.","খাদ্য ঠাণ্ডা হৈ গৈছে।"
"190","He had already eaten before we arrived.","āmi ahār āgtei xi khāichil.","আমি অহাৰ আগতেই সি খাইছিল।"
"191","The bread is baked in a wood oven.","kāṭhar abhenat ruṭi xeki lowā hay.","কাঠৰ অভেনত ৰুটি সেকি লোৱা হয়।"
"192","The fish was cooked by my grandmother.","māchṭo āitāi rāndhichil.","মাছটো আইতাই ৰান্ধিছিল।"
"193","The vegetables are washed before cooking.","xāk-pācli randhār āgte dhui lowā hay.","শাক-পাচলি ৰন্ধাৰ আগতে ধুই লোৱা হয়।"
"194","Rice is eaten in many countries.","bahu dexat cāul khowā hay.","বহু দেশত চাউল খোৱা হয়।"
"195","The cake will be finished soon.","kekṭo xonkāle xex ha’b.","কেকটো সোনকালে শেষ হ’ব।"
"196","Stir the sauce slowly.","cāṭnikhini lāhe lāhe larāi dib.","চাটনিখিনি লাহে লাহে লৰাই দিব।"
"197","Do not burn the food!","khādya jbalāi nidib!","খাদ্য জ্বলাই নিদিব!"
"198","Add a little salt.","alap nimakh dib.","অলপ নিমখ দিব।"
"199","Taste this and tell me what you think.","eiṭo xowād laok āru āponār matāmat janāok.","এইটো সোৱাদ লওক আৰু আপোনাৰ মতামত জনাওক।"
"200","Let us order dessert.","ḍejārṭa arḍār dioṃ.","ডেজাৰ্ট অৰ্ডাৰ দিওঁ।"
"201","If you add too much salt, the food will be ruined.","bechikai nimakh dile khādya naxṭa hai yāb.","বেছিকৈ নিমখ দিলে খাদ্য নষ্ট হৈ যাব।"
"202","If I had more time, I would bake a cake.","bechi xamay thākile kek eṭā banāloṃheṃten.","বেছি সময় থাকিলে কেক এটা বনালোঁহেঁতেন।"
"203","If she had bought the ingredients, we would have cooked.","tāi yadi xāmagrī kinileheṃten āmi rāndhiloṃheṃten.","তাই যদি সামগ্ৰী কিনিলেহেঁতেন আমি ৰান্ধিলোঁহেঁতেন।"
"204","You should eat more vegetables.","xāk-pācli bechikai khāb lāge.","শাক-পাচলি বেছিকৈ খাব লাগে।"
"205","We must wash the fruit before eating.","āmi khowār āgte phalbor dhub lāgib.","আমি খোৱাৰ আগতে ফলবোৰ ধুব লাগিব।"
"206","I can cook Italian food.","iṭālīr khādya rāndhib pāro.","ইটালীৰ খাদ্য ৰান্ধিব পাৰো।"
"207","She might bring dessert.","tāi hayto ḍejārṭa lai āhib.","তাই হয়তো ডেজাৰ্ট লৈ আহিব।"
"208","You need to chop the onions.","piṃyājkhini kāṭib lāgib.","পিঁয়াজখিনি কাটিব লাগিব।"
"209","This restaurant is better than that one.","ei rexṭureṇṭakhan xeikhnatkai bhāl.","এই ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্টখন সেইখনতকৈ ভাল।"
"210","Her cooking is the best in the family.","tāir randhā-bṛhā pariyālat xarbottam.","তাইৰ ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া পৰিয়ালত সৰ্বোত্তম।"
"211","This soup is spicier than the last one.","ei cūpṭo xexarṭotkai machlāyukta.","এই চূপটো শেষৰটোতকৈ মছলাযুক্ত।"
"212","The freshest fish is at the morning market.","xatej māch rātipuwār bajārat.","সতেজ মাছ ৰাতিপুৱাৰ বজাৰত।"
"213","I want some water.","alap pānī lāge.","অলপ পানী লাগে।"
"214","There is no sugar left.","ceni bākī nāi.","চেনি বাকী নাই।"
"215","We need a few more eggs.","āmāk āru keiṭāmān kaṇī lāge.","আমাক আৰু কেইটামান কণী লাগে।"
"216","He ate all of the rice.","bhātar xaklobor khāi pelāle.","ভাতৰ সকলোবোৰ খাই পেলালে।"
"217","Is there any milk?","gākhīr āche neki?","গাখীৰ আছে নেকি?"
"218","The dish that she cooked was excellent.","tāi yi khādya rāndhichil xeyā āchil ati uttam.","তাই যি খাদ্য ৰান্ধিছিল সেয়া আছিল অতি উত্তম।"
"219","The restaurant where we ate last night is famous.","yowā nixā āmi khowā rexṭureṇṭakhan bikhyāt.","যোৱা নিশা আমি খোৱা ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্টখন বিখ্যাত।"
"220","The chef who trained in Paris cooks the best food.","perichat praxikxaṇ lowā cephajne xarbottam khādya rāndhe.","পেৰিছত প্ৰশিক্ষণ লোৱা চেফজনে সৰ্বোত্তম খাদ্য ৰান্ধে।"
"221","I eat rice and fish.","bhāt māch khāoṃ.","ভাত মাছ খাওঁ।"
"222","She likes coffee but not tea.","tāi kaphi bhāl pāy kintu cāh bhāl nāpāy.","তাই কফি ভাল পায় কিন্তু চাহ ভাল নাপায়।"
"223","He eats a lot because he is always hungry.","xadāy bhokat thakā bābe xi bahut khāy.","সদায় ভোকত থকা বাবে সি বহুত খায়।"
"224","I will cook dinner or we can order food.","mai rātir āhār rāndhim nah’le āmi khādyar arḍār dib pāro.","মই ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধিম নহ’লে আমি খাদ্যৰ অৰ্ডাৰ দিব পাৰো।"
"225","Although the food was expensive, it was delicious.","khādya byayabhul ha’leo xuxbādu āchil.","খাদ্য ব্যয়বহুল হ’লেও সুস্বাদু আছিল।"
"226","This is my plate.","eikhan mor pleṭ.","এইখন মোৰ প্লেট।"
"227","That glass is yours.","xei gilācṭo tomār.","সেই গিলাচটো তোমাৰ।"
"228","Her recipe is very popular.","tāir recipiṭo bar janapriy.","তাইৰ ৰেচিপিটো বৰ জনপ্ৰিয়।"
"229","Our kitchen is small but clean.","āmār pākgharṭo xaru yadio parixkār.","আমাৰ পাকঘৰটো সৰু যদিও পৰিষ্কাৰ।"
"230","He said that the food was delicious.","khādya xuxbādu buli ka’le.","খাদ্য সুস্বাদু বুলি ক’লে।"
"231","She asked me if I wanted more rice.","tāi mok āru bhāt lāge neki buli xudhile.","তাই মোক আৰু ভাত লাগে নেকি বুলি সুধিলে।"
"232","She cooks quickly.","tāi khardharkai rāndhe.","তাই খৰধৰকৈ ৰান্ধে।"
"233","I eat slowly.","lāhe lāhe khāoṃ.","লাহে লাহে খাওঁ।"
"234","The food is almost ready.","khādya prāy reḍi hai gaiche.","খাদ্য প্ৰায় ৰেডি হৈ গৈছে।"
"235","I usually eat at home.","xādhāraṇte gharte khāoṃ.","সাধাৰণতে ঘৰতে খাওঁ।"
"236","The vegetables need to be washed.","xāk-pācli dhub lāge.","শাক-পাচলি ধুব লাগে।"
"237","I have never eaten sushi.","mai ketiyāo cuci khowā nāi.","মই কেতিয়াও চুচি খোৱা নাই।"
"238","Shall we have some tea?","āmi cāh khām neki?","আমি চাহ খাম নেকি?"
"239","Give me a piece of bread.","mok eṭukurā ruṭi diyā.","মোক এটুকুৰা ৰুটি দিয়া।"
"240","The dinner was cooked by my father.","rātir āhār rāndhichil deutāi.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধিছিল দেউতাই।"
"241","Who cooked this meal?","ei āhār kone rāndhile?","এই আহাৰ কোনে ৰান্ধিলে?"
"242","Neither the soup nor the rice was good.","cūpṭoo bhāl nāchil, bhātṭoo bhāl nāchil.","চূপটোও ভাল নাছিল, ভাতটোও ভাল নাছিল।"
"243","Both the meat and the fish are fresh.","māṃx āru māch duyoṭā xatej.","মাংস আৰু মাছ দুয়োটা সতেজ।"
"244","Not only did she cook, but she also cleaned.","kewal randhā-bṛhāi nahay, cāphāo karichil.","কেৱল ৰন্ধা-বঢ়াই নহয়, চাফাও কৰিছিল।"
"245","I had the chicken while he had the fish.","mor kukurāṭo āchil āru teoṃr māch āchil.","মোৰ কুকুৰাটো আছিল আৰু তেওঁৰ মাছ আছিল।"
"246","I wonder whether the cake is ready.","kekṭo reḍi haiche neki buli bhābo.","কেকটো ৰেডি হৈছে নেকি বুলি ভাবো।"
"247","I travel by plane.","mai bimānere yātrā karo.","মই বিমানেৰে যাত্ৰা কৰো।"
"248","She takes the train to the capital.","tāi relat uṭhi rājdhānīlai yāy.","তাই ৰেলত উঠি ৰাজধানীলৈ যায়।"
"249","He drives a car.","gāṛī calāy.","গাড়ী চলায়।"
"250","We fly to Paris every summer.","āmi prati grīxmakālat perichlai urā māri yāoṃ.","আমি প্ৰতি গ্ৰীষ্মকালত পেৰিছলৈ উৰা মাৰি যাওঁ।"
"251","They ride the bus to school.","bāchat uṭhi xkullai yāy.","বাছত উঠি স্কুললৈ যায়।"
"252","The plane leaves at ten.","dah bajāt bimānkhan rāonā hay.","দহ বজাত বিমানখন ৰাওনা হয়।"
"253","The train arrives in twenty minutes.","bix miniṭat rel āhi pāy.","বিশ মিনিটত ৰেল আহি পায়।"
"254","The bus is always late.","bāchkhan xadāy deri hay.","বাছখন সদায় দেৰি হয়।"
"255","I need a ticket.","mok ṭikaṭ lāge.","মোক টিকট লাগে।"
"256","She bought a one-way ticket.","tāi ekmukhī ṭikaṭ eṭā kinile.","তাই একমুখী টিকট এটা কিনিলে।"
"257","We arrived at the airport early.","āmi xonkāle bimānbandarat upaxthit ha’loṃ","আমি সোনকালে বিমানবন্দৰত উপস্থিত হ’লোঁ৷"
"258","They missed the flight.","bimānkhan mich karile.","বিমানখন মিছ কৰিলে।"
"259","He lost his passport.","teoṃr pāchpa’rṭa herāi ga’l.","তেওঁৰ পাছপ’ৰ্ট হেৰাই গ’ল।"
"260","I packed my suitcase last night.","yowā nixā mor chuṭkechṭo pek kariloṃ.","যোৱা নিশা মোৰ ছুটকেছটো পেক কৰিলোঁ।"
"261","She checked into the hotel.","tāi hoṭelat cek in karile.","তাই হোটেলত চেক ইন কৰিলে।"
"262","We stayed at a small hotel.","āmi ekhan xaru hoṭelat thākiloṃ","আমি এখন সৰু হোটেলত থাকিলোঁ৷"
"263","The room was very comfortable.","koṭhāṭo bar ārāmdāyak āchil.","কোঠাটো বৰ আৰামদায়ক আছিল।"
"264","I did not like the hotel.","hoṭelkhan mor bhāl nālāgil.","হোটেলখন মোৰ ভাল নালাগিল।"
"265","Did you enjoy your trip?","āponār bhramaṇ bhāl lāgil neki?","আপোনাৰ ভ্ৰমণ ভাল লাগিল নেকি?"
"266","Where did you go on vacation?","chuṭīt ka’lai ga’l?","ছুটীত ক’লৈ গ’ল?"
"267","I will travel to Japan next year.","ahā bachar jāpānlai yām.","অহা বছৰ জাপানলৈ যাম।"
"268","She is going to visit her family.","tāi gharar gharlai yāblai olāiche.","তাই ঘৰৰ ঘৰলৈ যাবলৈ ওলাইছে।"
"269","We will take the train.","āmi ṭreinat uṭhi yām.","আমি ট্ৰেইনত উঠি যাম।"
"270","He will not fly because he is afraid.","bhay khāi xi uri nāyāy.","ভয় খাই সি উৰি নাযায়।"
"271","Will they come with us?","āmār lagat āhibne?","আমাৰ লগত আহিবনে?"
"272","When will the plane arrive?","ketiyā āhib bimānkhan?","কেতিয়া আহিব বিমানখন?"
"273","I am waiting for the bus.","bāchar bābe apekxā kari ācho.","বাছৰ বাবে অপেক্ষা কৰি আছো।"
"274","She is packing her bags.","tāi begbor pek kari āche.","তাই বেগবোৰ পেক কৰি আছে।"
"275","They are boarding the plane.","bimānat uṭhiche.","বিমানত উঠিছে।"
"276","We were driving when it started to rain.","āmi gāṛī calāi thākoṃte barxuṇ diblai dharile.","আমি গাড়ী চলাই থাকোঁতে বৰষুণ দিবলৈ ধৰিলে।"
"277","I have been to Italy three times.","mai tinibār iṭālīlai gaicho.","মই তিনিবাৰ ইটালীলৈ গৈছো।"
"278","She has never flown before.","tāi āgte ketiyāo urājāhāj calowā nāi.","তাই আগতে কেতিয়াও উৰাজাহাজ চলোৱা নাই।"
"279","We have already booked the hotel.","āmi itimadhye hoṭel bukiṃ kari thaicho.","আমি ইতিমধ্যে হোটেল বুকিং কৰি থৈছো।"
"280","They had already left when we arrived.","āmi āhi powār xamyat xihaṃt itimadhye guci gaichil.","আমি আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত সিহঁত ইতিমধ্যে গুচি গৈছিল।"
"281","He has been traveling for three months.","tini māh dhari bhramaṇ kari āche.","তিনি মাহ ধৰি ভ্ৰমণ কৰি আছে।"
"282","The ticket was bought online.","anlāinyoge kinā haichil ṭikaṭ.","অনলাইনযোগে কিনা হৈছিল টিকট।"
"283","The flight was canceled.","bimānkhan bātil karā hay.","বিমানখন বাতিল কৰা হয়।"
"284","Passengers are asked to show their passports.","yātrīxaklak pāchpa’rṭa dekhuwāblai kowā haiche.","যাত্ৰীসকলক পাছপ’ৰ্ট দেখুৱাবলৈ কোৱা হৈছে।"
"285","The hotel room has been reserved.","hoṭelar koṭhāṭo rijārbha karā haiche.","হোটেলৰ কোঠাটো ৰিজাৰ্ভ কৰা হৈছে।"
"286","Fasten your seatbelt.","chiṭbelṭa bāndhi laok.","ছিটবেল্ট বান্ধি লওক।"
"287","Do not leave your luggage unattended.","āponār lāgej anādr̥t nārākhib.","আপোনাৰ লাগেজ অনাদৃত নাৰাখিব।"
"288","Please show me your ticket.","anugrah kari āponār ṭikaṭṭo dekhuwāok.","অনুগ্ৰহ কৰি আপোনাৰ টিকটটো দেখুৱাওক।"
"289","If we leave now, we will catch the train.","āmi etiyāi guci ga’le ṭreinkhan dharim.","আমি এতিয়াই গুচি গ’লে ট্ৰেইনখন ধৰিম।"
"290","If I had a car, I would drive there.","mor gāṛī thākile mai tātei gāṛī calāloṃheṃten.","মোৰ গাড়ী থাকিলে মই তাতেই গাড়ী চলালোঁহেঁতেন।"
"291","If they had arrived earlier, they would not have missed the flight.","āgte āhi powā ha’le bimānkhan mich nakrileheṃten.","আগতে আহি পোৱা হ’লে বিমানখন মিছ নকৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"292","You must arrive two hours before the flight.","bimānar dughaṇṭā āgte āhib lāgib.","বিমানৰ দুঘণ্টা আগতে আহিব লাগিব।"
"293","I should book the hotel in advance.","āgtīyākai hoṭel bukiṃ karib lāge.","আগতীয়াকৈ হোটেল বুকিং কৰিব লাগে।"
"294","We can take a taxi to the center.","āmi ṭekxi lai ceṇṭārlai yāb pāro.","আমি টেক্সি লৈ চেণ্টাৰলৈ যাব পাৰো।"
"295","She could not find her gate.","tāir geṭkhan bicāri nāpāle.","তাইৰ গেটখন বিচাৰি নাপালে।"
"296","The train is faster than the bus.","relkhan bāchatkai bechi begere.","ৰেলখন বাছতকৈ বেছি বেগেৰে।"
"297","Flying is the quickest way to travel.","urājāhāj calowāṭowei haiche yātrār āṭāitkai kxīpra upāy.","উৰাজাহাজ চলোৱাটোৱেই হৈছে যাত্ৰাৰ আটাইতকৈ ক্ষীপ্ৰ উপায়।"
"298","This hotel is more expensive than that one.","ei hoṭelkhan xeikhnatkai bechi dāmī.","এই হোটেলখন সেইখনতকৈ বেছি দামী।"
"299","It was the longest flight I have ever taken.","mor bābe yowā āṭāitkai dīghal bimānkhan āchil xeikhan.","মোৰ বাবে যোৱা আটাইতকৈ দীঘল বিমানখন আছিল সেইখন।"
"300","The airport is far from the city.","bimānbandarṭo cahrar parā bahu dūrat.","বিমানবন্দৰটো চহৰৰ পৰা বহু দূৰত।"
"301","The hotel is near the beach.","hoṭelkhan bilar ocarte.","হোটেলখন বিলৰ ওচৰতে।"
"302","Go straight and turn left.","pone pone gai bāoṃphāle ghūri.","পোনে পোনে গৈ বাওঁফালে ঘূৰি।"
"303","The station is across the street.","xṭecanṭo rāxtār xipāre.","ষ্টেচনটো ৰাস্তাৰ সিপাৰে।"
"304","The museum is between the park and the river.","udyān āru nadīr mājat xaṃgrahālayṭo.","উদ্যান আৰু নদীৰ মাজত সংগ্ৰহালয়টো।"
"305","She said that the flight was delayed.","bimānkhan palam howā bulio kay chātrīgrākīye.","বিমানখন পলম হোৱা বুলিও কয় ছাত্ৰীগৰাকীয়ে।"
"306","He told me that the hotel was fully booked.","teoṃ mok ka’le ye hoṭelkhan xampūrṇa bukiṃ hai āche.","তেওঁ মোক ক’লে যে হোটেলখন সম্পূৰ্ণ বুকিং হৈ আছে।"
"307","They asked us where we were going.","āmāk ka’lai yām buli xudhile.","আমাক ক’লৈ যাম বুলি সুধিলে।"
"308","All passengers must board now.","xaklo yātrīye etiyāi uṭhib lāgib.","সকলো যাত্ৰীয়ে এতিয়াই উঠিব লাগিব।"
"309","Some flights were canceled.","kichumān bimān bātil karā ha’l.","কিছুমান বিমান বাতিল কৰা হ’ল।"
"310","No one was at the station.","xṭecnat kono nāchil.","ষ্টেচনত কোনো নাছিল।"
"311","I have not seen any taxis.","kono ṭekxi dekhā nāi.","কোনো টেক্সি দেখা নাই।"
"312","The city where we stayed was beautiful.","āmi thakā caharkhan dhunīyā āchil.","আমি থকা চহৰখন ধুনীয়া আছিল।"
"313","The woman who sat next to me on the plane was very kind.","bimānat mor kāxat bahi thakā mahilāgrākī bar dayālu āchil.","বিমানত মোৰ কাষত বহি থকা মহিলাগৰাকী বৰ দয়ালু আছিল।"
"314","We can take the bus or walk.","āmi bāchat uṭhib pāro bā khojkāṛhi yāb pāro.","আমি বাছত উঠিব পাৰো বা খোজকাঢ়ি যাব পাৰো।"
"315","I want to travel, but I do not have enough money.","bhramaṇ kariblai man yāy, kintu mor hātat paryāpta dhan nāi.","ভ্ৰমণ কৰিবলৈ মন যায়, কিন্তু মোৰ হাতত পৰ্যাপ্ত ধন নাই।"
"316","She travels a lot because she loves exploring new places.","tāi bahut bhramaṇ kare kāraṇ tāi natun natun ṭhāi anbexaṇ kari bhāl pāy.","তাই বহুত ভ্ৰমণ কৰে কাৰণ তাই নতুন নতুন ঠাই অন্বেষণ কৰি ভাল পায়।"
"317","Although the journey was long, we enjoyed it.","yadio yātrāṭo dīghlīyā āchil, tathāpio āmi upbhog kariloṃ.","যদিও যাত্ৰাটো দীঘলীয়া আছিল, তথাপিও আমি উপভোগ কৰিলোঁ।"
"318","How long does the flight take?","bimān yātrāt kimān xamay lāge?","বিমান যাত্ৰাত কিমান সময় লাগে?"
"319","I would like to rent a car.","gāṛī bhāṛā kari la’b bicārim.","গাড়ী ভাড়া কৰি ল’ব বিচাৰিম।"
"320","The taxi driver took us to the wrong address.","ṭekxi cālkajne āmāk bhul ṭhiknālai lai ga’l.","টেক্সি চালকজনে আমাক ভুল ঠিকনালৈ লৈ গ’ল।"
"321","We are lost.","āmi herāi gaicho.","আমি হেৰাই গৈছো।"
"322","Can you tell me how to get to the train station?","rel xṭecanlai kenekai yāb pāri kab pāribne?","ৰেল ষ্টেচনলৈ কেনেকৈ যাব পাৰি কব পাৰিবনে?"
"323","I have never been here before.","mai iyāt āgte ketiyāo yowā nāi.","মই ইয়াত আগতে কেতিয়াও যোৱা নাই।"
"324","Excuse me, where is the nearest bus stop?","kxamā karib, ocrar bāch xṭapṭo ka’t?","ক্ষমা কৰিব, ওচৰৰ বাছ ষ্টপটো ক’ত?"
"325","My mother is a doctor.","mor mā ḍāktar.","মোৰ মা ডাক্তৰ।"
"326","His father works in a factory.","deutāke eṭā kārkhānāt kām kare.","দেউতাকে এটা কাৰখানাত কাম কৰে।"
"327","She has two brothers and one sister.","tāir dujan bhātr̥ āru egrākī bhanīyek āche.","তাইৰ দুজন ভাতৃ আৰু এগৰাকী ভনীয়েক আছে।"
"328","We live with our grandparents.","āmi āmār kakā-āitāhaṃtar lagat thāko.","আমি আমাৰ ককা-আইতাহঁতৰ লগত থাকো।"
"329","Their family is very large.","teoṃlokar pariyālṭo bar ḍāṅar.","তেওঁলোকৰ পৰিয়ালটো বৰ ডাঙৰ।"
"330","The baby is sleeping.","keṃcuwāṭo xui āche.","কেঁচুৱাটো শুই আছে।"
"331","My oldest brother is married.","mor ḍāṅar bhāiṭir biyā haiche.","মোৰ ডাঙৰ ভাইটিৰ বিয়া হৈছে।"
"332","Her younger sister studies at the university.","tāir xaru bhanīyeke bixbabidyālyat paṛhe.","তাইৰ সৰু ভনীয়েকে বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ত পঢ়ে।"
"333","I love my parents.","mai mor mā-deutāk bhāl pāoṃ.","মই মোৰ মা-দেউতাক ভাল পাওঁ।"
"334","He takes care of his grandmother.","āitākar khabar lay xi.","আইতাকৰ খবৰ লয় সি।"
"335","Our house has three bedrooms.","āmār gharat tiniṭā xowā koṭhā.","আমাৰ ঘৰত তিনিটা শোৱা কোঠা।"
"336","The living room is next to the kitchen.","ḍrayiṃ rūmṭo pākaghrar kāxte.","ড্ৰয়িং ৰূমটো পাকঘৰৰ কাষতে।"
"337","There is a garden behind the house.","gharar pichphāle ekhan bārī.","ঘৰৰ পিছফালে এখন বাৰী।"
"338","The bathroom is upstairs.","bāthrumṭo oprat.","বাথৰুমটো ওপৰত।"
"339","We painted the walls last month.","āmi yowā māhat dewālat raṃ kariloṃ.","আমি যোৱা মাহত দেৱালত ৰং কৰিলোঁ।"
"340","She decorated her room.","tāi nijar koṭhāṭo xajāi tulile.","তাই নিজৰ কোঠাটো সজাই তুলিলে।"
"341","The roof needs to be repaired.","cālkhan merāmti karib lāgib.","চালখন মেৰামতি কৰিব লাগিব।"
"342","I want to buy a new sofa.","natun cophā ekhan kinib man gaiche.","নতুন চোফা এখন কিনিব মন গৈছে।"
"343","He built a bookshelf himself.","teoṃ nijei ekhan kitāpar ālmārī xājichil.","তেওঁ নিজেই এখন কিতাপৰ আলমাৰী সাজিছিল।"
"344","The house was built fifty years ago.","pañcāx bachar āgte nirmāṇ karā gharṭo.","পঞ্চাশ বছৰ আগতে নিৰ্মাণ কৰা ঘৰটো।"
"345","I do not have any siblings.","mor kono bhāi-bhnī nāi.","মোৰ কোনো ভাই-ভনী নাই।"
"346","She does not live with her parents.","tāi māk deutākar lagat nāthāke.","তাই মাক দেউতাকৰ লগত নাথাকে।"
"347","He is not married.","teoṃr biyā howā nāi.","তেওঁৰ বিয়া হোৱা নাই।"
"348","We have never moved to another city.","āmi ketiyāo ān caharlai guci yowā nāi.","আমি কেতিয়াও আন চহৰলৈ গুচি যোৱা নাই।"
"349","How many children do you have?","āponār kimān xantān āche?","আপোনাৰ কিমান সন্তান আছে?"
"350","Where does your family live?","āponār pariyālṭo ka’t thāke?","আপোনাৰ পৰিয়ালটো ক’ত থাকে?"
"351","Do you have any pets?","āponār kono pohnīyā jantu āche neki?","আপোনাৰ কোনো পোহনীয়া জন্তু আছে নেকি?"
"352","Is your father at home?","deutā gharat āche neki?","দেউতা ঘৰত আছে নেকি?"
"353","Who is the oldest in your family?","āponār pariyālar āṭāitkai bayxīyāl kon?","আপোনাৰ পৰিয়ালৰ আটাইতকৈ বয়সীয়াল কোন?"
"354","I grew up in a small town.","xaru cahar ekhnat ḍāṅar haicho.","সৰু চহৰ এখনত ডাঙৰ হৈছো।"
"355","My parents met at university.","mor mā-deutā bixbabidyālyat lag haichil.","মোৰ মা-দেউতা বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ত লগ হৈছিল।"
"356","She was born in nineteen ninety.","ūnaix nabbait tāir janma haichil.","ঊনৈশ নব্বৈত তাইৰ জন্ম হৈছিল।"
"357","We moved to this city five years ago.","āmi pāṃc bachar āgte ei caharlai guci āhiloṃ.","আমি পাঁচ বছৰ আগতে এই চহৰলৈ গুচি আহিলোঁ।"
"358","They got married last summer.","yowā grīxmakālat teoṃlokar biyā haichil.","যোৱা গ্ৰীষ্মকালত তেওঁলোকৰ বিয়া হৈছিল।"
"359","My sister will have a baby next month.","ahā māhat mor bhanīyekar xantān ha’b.","অহা মাহত মোৰ ভনীয়েকৰ সন্তান হ’ব।"
"360","We will visit our grandparents this weekend.","ei uikeṇḍat āmi āmār kakā-āitākak lag karim.","এই উইকেণ্ডত আমি আমাৰ ককা-আইতাকক লগ কৰিম।"
"361","He is going to move to a new apartment.","xi natun epārṭameṇṭa eṭālai yāblai olāiche.","সি নতুন এপাৰ্টমেণ্ট এটালৈ যাবলৈ ওলাইছে।"
"362","They will celebrate their anniversary.","teoṃlokar bārxikī udyāpan karib.","তেওঁলোকৰ বাৰ্ষিকী উদযাপন কৰিব।"
"363","If we had a bigger house, we would adopt a dog.","āmār ḍāṅar ghar eṭā thākile kukur eṭā dattak la’loṃheṃten.","আমাৰ ডাঙৰ ঘৰ এটা থাকিলে কুকুৰ এটা দত্তক ল’লোঁহেঁতেন।"
"364","If my grandparents were alive, they would be proud.","mor kakā-āitā jīyāi thākile gauraw karileheṃten.","মোৰ ককা-আইতা জীয়াই থাকিলে গৌৰৱ কৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"365","Children must respect their parents.","xantāne pitr̥-mātr̥k xanmān karib lāgib.","সন্তানে পিতৃ-মাতৃক সন্মান কৰিব লাগিব।"
"366","You should call your mother.","mākak phon karā ucit.","মাকক ফোন কৰা উচিত।"
"367","I can hear the baby crying.","keṃcuwāṭor kāndon xuniblai pāoṃ.","কেঁচুৱাটোৰ কান্দোন শুনিবলৈ পাওঁ।"
"368","Close the door, please.","duwārkhan bandha karak, anugrah kari.","দুৱাৰখন বন্ধ কৰক, অনুগ্ৰহ কৰি।"
"369","Do not make so much noise.","imān kolāhal nakrib.","ইমান কোলাহল নকৰিব।"
"370","Turn off the lights.","lāiṭ bandha kari dib.","লাইট বন্ধ কৰি দিব।"
"371","My house is bigger than yours.","mor gharṭo tomār gharatkai ḍāṅar.","মোৰ ঘৰটো তোমাৰ ঘৰতকৈ ডাঙৰ।"
"372","She is taller than her brother.","bhāyekatkai tāi okh.","ভায়েকতকৈ তাই ওখ।"
"373","He is the youngest child in the family.","pariyālṭor xaru la’rā-chowālī.","পৰিয়ালটোৰ সৰু ল’ৰা-ছোৱালী।"
"374","This is the most beautiful garden I have ever seen.","eikhnei mor dekhā āṭāitkai dhunīyā bāgicākhan.","এইখনেই মোৰ দেখা আটাইতকৈ ধুনীয়া বাগিচাখন।"
"375","The children are raised by their grandparents.","la’rā-chowālīkeiṭāk kakā-āitāke ḍāṅar-dīghal kare.","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীকেইটাক ককা-আইতাকে ডাঙৰ-দীঘল কৰে।"
"376","The dinner table was set by my sister.","ḍinār ṭebulkhan bhanīyeke ceṭ kari thaichil.","ডিনাৰ টেবুলখন ভনীয়েকে চেট কৰি থৈছিল।"
"377","The woman who lives upstairs is my aunt.","oprat thakā mānuhajnī mor pehī.","ওপৰত থকা মানুহজনী মোৰ পেহী।"
"378","The house where I was born is very old.","mor janma howā gharkhan bahut purṇi.","মোৰ জন্ম হোৱা ঘৰখন বহুত পুৰণি।"
"379","The children dress themselves.","la’rā-chowālībore nijke xāj-pochāk pindhe.","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰে নিজকে সাজ-পোছাক পিন্ধে।"
"380","My parents love each other.","mor mā-deutāi ijne xijnak bhāl pāy.","মোৰ মা-দেউতাই ইজনে সিজনক ভাল পায়।"
"381","I miss my family.","mor pariyālṭolai manat pariche.","মোৰ পৰিয়ালটোলৈ মনত পৰিছে।"
"382","She looks like her mother.","tāik mākar dare dekhā yāy.","তাইক মাকৰ দৰে দেখা যায়।"
"383","He inherited the house from his uncle.","kakākar parāi teoṃ gharkhan uttarādhikārī xūtre lābh karichil.","ককাকৰ পৰাই তেওঁ ঘৰখন উত্তৰাধিকাৰী সূত্ৰে লাভ কৰিছিল।"
"384","We named the baby after my grandfather.","āmi mor dādār nāmere keṃcuwāṭor nām rākhiloṃ.","আমি মোৰ দাদাৰ নামেৰে কেঁচুৱাটোৰ নাম ৰাখিলোঁ।"
"385","Everyone in our family speaks two languages.","āmār pariyālar xaklowe duṭā bhāxā kay.","আমাৰ পৰিয়ালৰ সকলোৱে দুটা ভাষা কয়।"
"386","My home is where my family is.","mor gharakhnei mor pariyālṭo.","মোৰ ঘৰখনেই মোৰ পৰিয়ালটো।"
"387","As a child, I used to play in the garden.","xarute mai bārīt khelichilo.","সৰুতে মই বাৰীত খেলিছিলো।"
"388","The neighbors are very kind to us.","ocar-cuburīyāi āmār prati bar dayālu.","ওচৰ-চুবুৰীয়াই আমাৰ প্ৰতি বৰ দয়ালু।"
"389","I work in an office.","mai eṭā aphicat kām karo.","মই এটা অফিচত কাম কৰো।"
"390","She studies mathematics.","tāi gaṇit adhyayan kare.","তাই গণিত অধ্যয়ন কৰে।"
"391","He is a teacher.","teoṃ ejan xikxak.","তেওঁ এজন শিক্ষক।"
"392","We have a meeting at three.","tini bajāt āmār ekhan xabhā āche.","তিনি বজাত আমাৰ এখন সভা আছে।"
"393","They study together every day.","pratidine ekelge paṛhe.","প্ৰতিদিনে একেলগে পঢ়ে।"
"394","The students are in the classroom.","chātra-chātrīxkal xreṇīkoṭhāt.","ছাত্ৰ-ছাত্ৰীসকল শ্ৰেণীকোঠাত।"
"395","My boss is very strict.","mor bach bar kaṭhor.","মোৰ বছ বৰ কঠোৰ।"
"396","She teaches English and French.","iṃrājī āru pharācī paṛhe.","ইংৰাজী আৰু ফৰাচী পঢ়ে।"
"397","He writes reports every week.","prati xaptāhat teoṃ riparṭa likhe.","প্ৰতি সপ্তাহত তেওঁ ৰিপৰ্ট লিখে।"
"398","I have an exam tomorrow.","kāilai mor eṭā parīkxā āche.","কাইলৈ মোৰ এটা পৰীক্ষা আছে।"
"399","I started a new job last month.","yowā māhat natun cākri eṭā ārambha kariloṃ.","যোৱা মাহত নতুন চাকৰি এটা আৰম্ভ কৰিলোঁ।"
"400","She graduated from university in June.","jun māhat bixbabidyālyar parā xnātak ḍigrī lābh kare.","জুন মাহত বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ৰ পৰা স্নাতক ডিগ্ৰী লাভ কৰে।"
"401","He was promoted to manager.","teoṃk menejār padat padonnati diyā hay.","তেওঁক মেনেজাৰ পদত পদোন্নতি দিয়া হয়।"
"402","We finished the project on time.","āmi xamyamte prajekṭaṭo xex kariloṃ.","আমি সময়মতে প্ৰজেক্টটো শেষ কৰিলোঁ।"
"403","They did not pass the test.","parīkxāt uttīrṇa naha’l.","পৰীক্ষাত উত্তীৰ্ণ নহ’ল।"
"404","I will apply for the position.","mai padṭor bābe ābedan karim.","মই পদটোৰ বাবে আবেদন কৰিম।"
"405","She is going to start university.","tāi bixbabidyālay ārambha kariblai olāiche.","তাই বিশ্ববিদ্যালয় আৰম্ভ কৰিবলৈ ওলাইছে।"
"406","He will give a presentation next Monday.","ahā xombāre teoṃ prejeṇṭecan dib.","অহা সোমবাৰে তেওঁ প্ৰেজেণ্টেচন দিব।"
"407","We will hire more employees.","āmi āru karmacārī niyog karim.","আমি আৰু কৰ্মচাৰী নিয়োগ কৰিম।"
"408","Will you take the job?","cākriṭo la’bne?","চাকৰিটো ল’বনে?"
"409","I am studying for an exam.","parīkxār bābe paṛhi āchoṃ.","পৰীক্ষাৰ বাবে পঢ়ি আছোঁ।"
"410","She is working on a new project.","natun prajekṭar kām kari āche tāi.","নতুন প্ৰজেক্টৰ কাম কৰি আছে তাই।"
"411","They are having a meeting right now.","bartamān teoṃlokar ekhan xabhā āche.","বৰ্তমান তেওঁলোকৰ এখন সভা আছে।"
"412","I have worked here for ten years.","mai iyāt dah bachar kām karicho.","মই ইয়াত দহ বছৰ কাম কৰিছো।"
"413","She has already finished her homework.","itimadhye tāir homwarka xex haiche.","ইতিমধ্যে তাইৰ হোমৱৰ্ক শেষ হৈছে।"
"414","He has been studying all day.","goṭei dinṭo paṛhā-xunā kari āche.","গোটেই দিনটো পঢ়া-শুনা কৰি আছে।"
"415","We had already started when the boss arrived.","bach āhi powār lage lage āmi itimadhye ārambha karichiloṃ.","বছ আহি পোৱাৰ লগে লগে আমি ইতিমধ্যে আৰম্ভ কৰিছিলোঁ।"
"416","I do not understand this problem.","ei xamaxyāṭo mai buji powā nāi.","এই সমস্যাটো মই বুজি পোৱা নাই।"
"417","She does not agree with the plan.","parikalpanār lagat tāi ekmat nahay.","পৰিকল্পনাৰ লগত তাই একমত নহয়।"
"418","He cannot come to the meeting.","teoṃ xabhālai āhib nowāre.","তেওঁ সভালৈ আহিব নোৱাৰে।"
"419","What do you do for a living?","āponār jīwikā ki?","আপোনাৰ জীৱিকা কি?"
"420","Where does she work?","tāi ka’t kām kare?","তাই ক’ত কাম কৰে?"
"421","Did you finish the report?","riparṭa xex karile neki?","ৰিপৰ্ট শেষ কৰিলে নেকি?"
"422","How was the exam?","parīkxā kenekuwā āchil?","পৰীক্ষা কেনেকুৱা আছিল?"
"423","Who is your teacher?","āponār xikxak kon?","আপোনাৰ শিক্ষক কোন?"
"424","The homework was assigned yesterday.","kāli gharuwā kāmar dāyitba diyā haichil.","কালি ঘৰুৱা কামৰ দায়িত্ব দিয়া হৈছিল।"
"425","The results will be announced next week.","ahā xaptāhat ghoxṇā karā ha’b phalāphal.","অহা সপ্তাহত ঘোষণা কৰা হ’ব ফলাফল।"
"426","English is taught in many schools.","bahu bidyālyat iṃrājī paṛhowā hay.","বহু বিদ্যালয়ত ইংৰাজী পঢ়োৱা হয়।"
"427","The report must be submitted by Friday.","xukurbārar bhitrat pratibedan dākhil karib lāgib.","শুকুৰবাৰৰ ভিতৰত প্ৰতিবেদন দাখিল কৰিব লাগিব।"
"428","If I study hard, I will pass the exam.","kaxṭa kari paṛhile parīkxā pāch karim.","কষ্ট কৰি পঢ়িলে পৰীক্ষা পাছ কৰিম।"
"429","If she had studied more, she would have passed.","bechikai paṛhile tāi pāch karileheṃten.","বেছিকৈ পঢ়িলে তাই পাছ কৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"430","You must finish your homework.","homwarka xex karib lāgib.","হোমৱৰ্ক শেষ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"431","I should study harder.","mai adhik kaxṭa kari paṛhā ucit.","মই অধিক কষ্ট কৰি পঢ়া উচিত।"
"432","She can speak three languages.","tāi tiniṭā bhāxā ka’b pāre.","তাই তিনিটা ভাষা ক’ব পাৰে।"
"433","May I leave early today?","āji xonkāle yāb pārimne?","আজি সোনকালে যাব পাৰিমনে?"
"434","Open your books to page ten.","āponār kitāpbor dah pr̥xṭhālai khulib.","আপোনাৰ কিতাপবোৰ দহ পৃষ্ঠালৈ খুলিব।"
"435","Do not talk during the exam.","parīkxār xamyat kathā nāpātib.","পৰীক্ষাৰ সময়ত কথা নাপাতিব।"
"436","Study the first three chapters.","pratham tiniṭā adhyāy adhyayan karak.","প্ৰথম তিনিটা অধ্যায় অধ্যয়ন কৰক।"
"437","This problem is harder than the last one.","ei xamaxyā xexarṭotkai kaṭhin.","এই সমস্যা শেষৰটোতকৈ কঠিন।"
"438","She is the best student in the class.","tāi klāchar xrexṭha chātrī.","তাই ক্লাছৰ শ্ৰেষ্ঠ ছাত্ৰী।"
"439","Working from home is easier than going to the office.","aphiclai yowātkai gharar parā kām karāṭo xahaj.","অফিচলৈ যোৱাতকৈ ঘৰৰ পৰা কাম কৰাটো সহজ।"
"440","The teacher who helped me is retiring.","mok xahāy karā xikxakjan awxar laiche.","মোক সহায় কৰা শিক্ষকজন অৱসৰ লৈছে।"
"441","The school where I studied has closed.","mai paṛhā xkulkhan bandha hai gaiche.","মই পঢ়া স্কুলখন বন্ধ হৈ গৈছে।"
"442","I work hard and I study at night.","kaxṭa karo āru rāti paṛho.","কষ্ট কৰো আৰু ৰাতি পঢ়ো।"
"443","She is tired but she keeps studying.","tāi bhāgri pariche yadio tāi paṛhā-xunā kariyei thāke.","তাই ভাগৰি পৰিছে যদিও তাই পঢ়া-শুনা কৰিয়েই থাকে।"
"444","He works because he needs money.","ṭakār prayojan howā bābei teoṃ kām kare.","টকাৰ প্ৰয়োজন হোৱা বাবেই তেওঁ কাম কৰে।"
"445","My colleague is on vacation.","mor xahkarmījan chuṭīt āche.","মোৰ সহকৰ্মীজন ছুটীত আছে।"
"446","The deadline is next Friday.","antim tārikh ahā xukurbār.","অন্তিম তাৰিখ অহা শুকুৰবাৰ।"
"447","I have too much work to do.","mor bahut kām āche.","মোৰ বহুত কাম আছে।"
"448","She earned a promotion.","tāi padonnati lābh karile.","তাই পদোন্নতি লাভ কৰিলে।"
"449","The office is closed on weekends.","chuṭir dinat kāryālayṭo bandha thāke.","ছুটিৰ দিনত কাৰ্যালয়টো বন্ধ থাকে।"
"450","I applied for the scholarship.","mai br̥ttir bābe ābedan kariloṃ.","মই বৃত্তিৰ বাবে আবেদন কৰিলোঁ।"
"451","Education is important for everyone.","xikxā xaklore bābe gurutbapūrṇa.","শিক্ষা সকলোৰে বাবে গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ।"
"452","He was fired from his job.","cākrir parā barkhāxta karā haichil.","চাকৰিৰ পৰা বৰ্খাস্ত কৰা হৈছিল।"
"453","I am looking for a new job.","natun cākri bicāri ācho.","নতুন চাকৰি বিচাৰি আছো।"
"454","What time does the class start?","kimān bajāt klāch ārambha hay?","কিমান বজাত ক্লাছ আৰম্ভ হয়?"
"455","The sun is shining.","xūryyaṭo jiliki āche.","সূৰ্য্যটো জিলিকি আছে।"
"456","It is raining.","barxuṇ di āche.","বৰষুণ দি আছে।"
"457","It is very cold today.","āji bar ṭhāṇḍā.","আজি বৰ ঠাণ্ডা।"
"458","The sky is blue.","ākāxkhan nīlā.","আকাশখন নীলা।"
"459","The wind is strong.","pracaṇḍa batāh.","প্ৰচণ্ড বতাহ।"
"460","There are many trees in the forest.","araṇyar bukut ajaxra gach-gachni.","অৰণ্যৰ বুকুত অজস্ৰ গছ-গছনি।"
"461","The river flows to the sea.","nadīkhan xāgarlai bai gaiche.","নদীখন সাগৰলৈ বৈ গৈছে।"
"462","The mountains are covered with snow.","pāhārbor barphere āwri dhariche.","পাহাৰবোৰ বৰফেৰে আৱৰি ধৰিছে।"
"463","The flowers bloom in spring.","baxanta kālat phul phule.","বসন্ত কালত ফুল ফুলে।"
"464","The leaves fall in autumn.","xarat kālat pāt xari pare.","শৰৎ কালত পাত সৰি পৰে।"
"465","It rained all day yesterday.","kāli goṭei dinṭo barxuṇ dile.","কালি গোটেই দিনটো বৰষুণ দিলে।"
"466","The storm destroyed several houses.","dhumuhāt bhāṅi yāy keibāṭāo ghar.","ধুমুহাত ভাঙি যায় কেইবাটাও ঘৰ।"
"467","The lake was frozen last winter.","yowā xītkālat ei hradṭo jamā hai parichil.","যোৱা শীতকালত এই হ্ৰদটো জমা হৈ পৰিছিল।"
"468","We saw a rainbow after the rain.","barxuṇar pichat āmi eṭā rāmdhenu dekhiloṃ.","বৰষুণৰ পিছত আমি এটা ৰামধেনু দেখিলোঁ।"
"469","The temperature dropped below zero.","tāpmātrā xūnyar tallai nāmi āhil.","তাপমাত্ৰা শূন্যৰ তললৈ নামি আহিল।"
"470","It will snow tomorrow.","kāilai baraph parib.","কাইলৈ বৰফ পৰিব।"
"471","The weather will be warm next week.","ahā xaptāhat batar uxṇa ha’b.","অহা সপ্তাহত বতৰ উষ্ণ হ’ব।"
"472","Spring will come soon.","baxanta xonkāle āhib.","বসন্ত সোনকালে আহিব।"
"473","Will it rain this afternoon?","āji duprīyā barxuṇ dib neki?","আজি দুপৰীয়া বৰষুণ দিব নেকি?"
"474","It is not raining.","barxuṇ howā nāi.","বৰষুণ হোৱা নাই।"
"475","The water is not clean.","pānī parixkār nahay.","পানী পৰিষ্কাৰ নহয়।"
"476","There are no clouds in the sky.","ākāxat ḍāwar nāi.","আকাশত ডাৱৰ নাই।"
"477","What is the weather like today?","ājir batar kenekuwā?","আজিৰ বতৰ কেনেকুৱা?"
"478","Is it going to rain?","barxuṇ ha’b neki?","বৰষুণ হ’ব নেকি?"
"479","How cold is it outside?","bāhirat kimān ṭhāṇḍā?","বাহিৰত কিমান ঠাণ্ডা?"
"480","Where does this river lead?","ei nadīkhne ka’lai lai yāy?","এই নদীখনে ক’লৈ লৈ যায়?"
"481","Have you ever seen a volcano?","āpuni ketiyābā āgneygiri dekhichene?","আপুনি কেতিয়াবা আগ্নেয়গিৰি দেখিছেনে?"
"482","You should bring an umbrella.","chāti eṭā lai āhib lāge.","ছাতি এটা লৈ আহিব লাগে।"
"483","It might rain later.","pichat barxuṇ ha’b pāre.","পিছত বৰষুণ হ’ব পাৰে।"
"484","We must protect the environment.","āmi pariwex rakxā karib lāgib.","আমি পৰিৱেশ ৰক্ষা কৰিব লাগিব।"
"485","Animals cannot survive without water.","pānī abihne jantu jīyāi thākib nowāre.","পানী অবিহনে জন্তু জীয়াই থাকিব নোৱাৰে।"
"486","The forest was destroyed by fire.","agnikāṇḍat naxṭa ha’l banāñcalkhan.","অগ্নিকাণ্ডত নষ্ট হ’ল বনাঞ্চলখন।"
"487","The island is surrounded by water.","dbīpṭo pānīre āguri āche.","দ্বীপটো পানীৰে আগুৰি আছে।"
"488","Many animals are endangered.","bahuto jīw-jantu biluptaprāy.","বহুতো জীৱ-জন্তু বিলুপ্তপ্ৰায়।"
"489","Summer is hotter than spring.","baxantatkai grīxmakāl bechi garam.","বসন্ততকৈ গ্ৰীষ্মকাল বেছি গৰম।"
"490","This is the tallest tree in the park.","eikhan udyānar āṭāitkai okh gach.","এইখন উদ্যানৰ আটাইতকৈ ওখ গছ।"
"491","The ocean is deeper than any lake.","yikono hradatkaio gabhīr xāgar.","যিকোনো হ্ৰদতকৈও গভীৰ সাগৰ।"
"492","Mount Everest is the highest mountain in the world.","māuṇṭa ebhārexṭa bixbar xarbocca parbat.","মাউণ্ট এভাৰেষ্ট বিশ্বৰ সৰ্বোচ্চ পৰ্বত।"
"493","If it rains, we will stay inside.","barxuṇ dile āmi bhitrat thākim.","বৰষুণ দিলে আমি ভিতৰত থাকিম।"
"494","If the ice melts, the sea level will rise.","baraph galile xāgrar jalpr̥xṭha br̥ddhi pāb.","বৰফ গলিলে সাগৰৰ জলপৃষ্ঠ বৃদ্ধি পাব।"
"495","If we do not protect nature, many species will disappear.","prakr̥tik rakxā nakrile bahu prajāti nohowā hai yāb.","প্ৰকৃতিক ৰক্ষা নকৰিলে বহু প্ৰজাতি নোহোৱা হৈ যাব।"
"496","The sun sets and the moon rises.","xūryya axta yāy āru candra uday hay.","সূৰ্য্য অস্ত যায় আৰু চন্দ্ৰ উদয় হয়।"
"497","It is cold but sunny.","ṭhāṇḍā yadio ra’d.","ঠাণ্ডা যদিও ৰ’দ।"
"498","I like summer because the days are long.","dinbor dīghlīyā howār bābe grīxmakāl mor bhāl lāge.","দিনবোৰ দীঘলীয়া হোৱাৰ বাবে গ্ৰীষ্মকাল মোৰ ভাল লাগে।"
"499","The mountain that we climbed was very high.","āmi bagāi yowā pāhārṭo bar okh āchil.","আমি বগাই যোৱা পাহাৰটো বৰ ওখ আছিল।"
"500","The river where we swam was crystal clear.","āmi xāṃturi thakā nadīkhan xphaṭik xbarūp āchil.","আমি সাঁতুৰি থকা নদীখন স্ফটিক স্বৰূপ আছিল।"
"501","The air smells fresh after the rain.","barxuṇar pichat batāhar xatej gondha.","বৰষুণৰ পিছত বতাহৰ সতেজ গোন্ধ।"
"502","Birds sing in the morning.","rātipuwā carāibore gān gāy.","ৰাতিপুৱা চৰাইবোৰে গান গায়।"
"503","The garden is full of flowers.","bārīkhan phulere bhari āche.","বাৰীখন ফুলেৰে ভৰি আছে।"
"504","A strong earthquake shook the city.","prabal bhūmikampai kaṃpāi tulile caharkhan.","প্ৰবল ভূমিকম্পই কঁপাই তুলিলে চহৰখন।"
"505","The sunset was beautiful.","xūryāxtaṭo bar dhunīyā āchil.","সূৰ্যাস্তটো বৰ ধুনীয়া আছিল।"
"506","Snow covers the ground in winter.","xītkālat barphere māṭi ābri rākhe.","শীতকালত বৰফেৰে মাটি আবৰি ৰাখে।"
"507","The weather has been very hot this week.","ei xaptāhat batar ati garam hai pariche.","এই সপ্তাহত বতৰ অতি গৰম হৈ পৰিছে।"
"508","It hardly ever snows here.","iyāt baraph prāyei nahay.","ইয়াত বৰফ প্ৰায়েই নহয়।"
"509","Lightning struck the old tree.","buṛhā gachjopāt bijulī paril.","বুঢ়া গছজোপাত বিজুলী পৰিল।"
"510","The tide comes in twice a day.","dinṭot dubārkai jowār āhe.","দিনটোত দুবাৰকৈ জোৱাৰ আহে।"
"511","I have a headache.","mor mūrar bix haiche.","মোৰ মূৰৰ বিষ হৈছে।"
"512","She is sick.","tāir axukh.","তাইৰ অসুখ।"
"513","He broke his arm.","tār bāhu bhāṅi ga’l.","তাৰ বাহু ভাঙি গ’ল।"
"514","My stomach hurts.","mor peṭṭo bixāiche.","মোৰ পেটটো বিষাইছে।"
"515","The doctor examined the patient.","cikitxake rogījnak parīkxā karile.","চিকিৎসকে ৰোগীজনক পৰীক্ষা কৰিলে।"
"516","We need to go to the hospital.","āmi haxpitāllai yāb lāgib.","আমি হস্পিতাললৈ যাব লাগিব।"
"517","They gave him medicine.","auxadh dile.","ঔষধ দিলে।"
"518","I feel much better today.","āji mor bahut bhāl lāgiche.","আজি মোৰ বহুত ভাল লাগিছে।"
"519","She takes medicine every day.","tāi pratidine auxadh khāy.","তাই প্ৰতিদিনে ঔষধ খায়।"
"520","He cannot see without his glasses.","caxmāyor abihne teoṃ dekhā nāpāy.","চশমাযোৰ অবিহনে তেওঁ দেখা নাপায়।"
"521","She has long hair.","tāir culi dīghal.","তাইৰ চুলি দীঘল।"
"522","His eyes are brown.","caku duṭā bādāmī raṅar.","চকু দুটা বাদামী ৰঙৰ।"
"523","My hands are cold.","mor hāt dukhan ṭhāṇḍā hai pariche.","মোৰ হাত দুখন ঠাণ্ডা হৈ পৰিছে।"
"524","The child cut his finger.","xixuṭiye āṅuli kāṭile.","শিশুটিয়ে আঙুলি কাটিলে।"
"525","I can hear with my ears.","kāṇere xunib pāricho.","কাণেৰে শুনিব পাৰিছো।"
"526","I do not feel well.","mor manṭo bhāl nahay.","মোৰ মনটো ভাল নহয়।"
"527","She is not allergic to anything.","tāir kono baxtur elārjī nāi.","তাইৰ কোনো বস্তুৰ এলাৰ্জী নাই।"
"528","He did not take his medicine.","teoṃ nijar auxadh xewan karā nāchil.","তেওঁ নিজৰ ঔষধ সেৱন কৰা নাছিল।"
"529","How do you feel?","kene lāgiche?","কেনে লাগিছে?"
"530","Does your head hurt?","mūrat bix hay neki?","মূৰত বিষ হয় নেকি?"
"531","What happened to your hand?","tomār hātakhnar ki ha’l?","তোমাৰ হাতখনৰ কি হ’ল?"
"532","Have you been to the doctor?","ḍāktarar ocarlai gaiche neki?","ডাক্তৰৰ ওচৰলৈ গৈছে নেকি?"
"533","When should I take the medicine?","auxadh ketiyā khāb lāge?","ঔষধ কেতিয়া খাব লাগে?"
"534","I will see the doctor tomorrow.","kāilai ḍāktarar ocarlai yām.","কাইলৈ ডাক্তৰৰ ওচৰলৈ যাম।"
"535","She will recover soon.","tāi xonkāle xuxtha hai uṭhib.","তাই সোনকালে সুস্থ হৈ উঠিব।"
"536","The operation will be performed next week.","ahā xaptāhat xampanna karā ha’b ei apārecan.","অহা সপ্তাহত সম্পন্ন কৰা হ’ব এই অপাৰেচন।"
"537","I have had a cold for three days.","tinidin dhari mor cardi haiche.","তিনিদিন ধৰি মোৰ চৰ্দি হৈছে।"
"538","She has never been to the hospital.","tāi ketiyāo haxpitāllai yowā nāi.","তাই কেতিয়াও হস্পিতাললৈ যোৱা নাই।"
"539","He has already recovered.","itimadhye teoṃ xuxtha hai uṭhiche.","ইতিমধ্যে তেওঁ সুস্থ হৈ উঠিছে।"
"540","The patient was treated by a specialist.","rogījnak bixexajñar dbārā cikitxā pradān karā hay.","ৰোগীজনক বিশেষজ্ঞৰ দ্বাৰা চিকিৎসা প্ৰদান কৰা হয়।"
"541","The medicine must be taken twice a day.","ei auxadh dine dubārkai khāb lāgib.","এই ঔষধ দিনে দুবাৰকৈ খাব লাগিব।"
"542","You should rest.","jirṇi lowā ucit.","জিৰণি লোৱা উচিত।"
"543","I must take care of my health.","mai mor xbāxthyar yatna la’b lāgib.","মই মোৰ স্বাস্থ্যৰ যত্ন ল’ব লাগিব।"
"544","She can walk again after the surgery.","axtropcārar pichat tāi ākau khoj kāṛhib pāre.","অস্ত্ৰোপচাৰৰ পিছত তাই আকৌ খোজ কাঢ়িব পাৰে।"
"545","He might need an operation.","teoṃr apārecnar prayojan ha’b pāre.","তেওঁৰ অপাৰেচনৰ প্ৰয়োজন হ’ব পাৰে।"
"546","Drink plenty of water.","pracur parimāṇe pānī khāb lāge.","প্ৰচুৰ পৰিমাণে পানী খাব লাগে।"
"547","Do not eat too much sugar.","bechikai ceni khāb nālāge.","বেছিকৈ চেনি খাব নালাগে।"
"548","Take this medicine after meals.","ei auxadh khādya khowār pichat grahaṇ karak.","এই ঔষধ খাদ্য খোৱাৰ পিছত গ্ৰহণ কৰক।"
"549","If you exercise regularly, you will be healthier.","niymitbhāwe byāyām karile āpuni xuxtha hai uṭhib.","নিয়মিতভাৱে ব্যায়াম কৰিলে আপুনি সুস্থ হৈ উঠিব।"
"550","If I had slept more, I would not be so tired.","bechikai xui thakā ha’le mai imān bhāgruwā nah’loheṃten.","বেছিকৈ শুই থকা হ’লে মই ইমান ভাগৰুৱা নহ’লোহেঁতেন।"
"551","I feel better than yesterday.","kālir tulnāt mor bhāl lāgiche.","কালিৰ তুলনাত মোৰ ভাল লাগিছে।"
"552","She is stronger than she looks.","dekhātkai tāi xaktixālī.","দেখাতকৈ তাই শক্তিশালী।"
"553","This is the worst pain I have ever felt.","eiṭowei mor anubhaw karā āṭāitkai beyā kaxṭa.","এইটোৱেই মোৰ অনুভৱ কৰা আটাইতকৈ বেয়া কষ্ট।"
"554","Exercise is good for your health.","byāyām āponār xbāxthyar bābe upkārī.","ব্যায়াম আপোনাৰ স্বাস্থ্যৰ বাবে উপকাৰী।"
"555","Smoking is bad for your lungs.","dhūmpān āponār hāoṃphāoṃr bābe beyā.","ধূমপান আপোনাৰ হাওঁফাওঁৰ বাবে বেয়া।"
"556","The nurse checked my blood pressure.","nārce mor raktacāp parīkxā karile.","নাৰ্চে মোৰ ৰক্তচাপ পৰীক্ষা কৰিলে।"
"557","I need to get more sleep.","mor āru ṭopni ahār prayojan.","মোৰ আৰু টোপনি অহাৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"558","Wash your hands before eating.","khowār āgte hāt dhub lāge.","খোৱাৰ আগতে হাত ধুব লাগে।"
"559","She was born with a heart condition.","janmate tāir hr̥drog haichil.","জন্মতে তাইৰ হৃদৰোগ হৈছিল।"
"560","He hurt himself while playing.","kheli thākoṃte xi nijke āghāt dichil.","খেলি থাকোঁতে সি নিজকে আঘাত দিছিল।"
"561","My back has been hurting all week.","goṭei xaptāhṭo mor piṭhikhan bixāi āche.","গোটেই সপ্তাহটো মোৰ পিঠিখন বিষাই আছে।"
"562","The pain went away after I took the medicine.","auxadh khowār pichat bixṭo guci ga’l.","ঔষধ খোৱাৰ পিছত বিষটো গুচি গ’ল।"
"563","She fainted because she had not eaten.","tāi khowā nāi bābei ajñān hai paril.","তাই খোৱা নাই বাবেই অজ্ঞান হৈ পৰিল।"
"564","I want to buy a new shirt.","natun cārṭa eṭā kinib man gaiche.","নতুন চাৰ্ট এটা কিনিব মন গৈছে।"
"565","She goes shopping every Saturday.","prati xanibāre tāi bajār kariblai yāy.","প্ৰতি শনিবাৰে তাই বজাৰ কৰিবলৈ যায়।"
"566","He paid for the groceries.","gelāmālar kharac teoṃ dile.","গেলামালৰ খৰচ তেওঁ দিলে।"
"567","We need more money.","āmāk āru ṭakā lāge.","আমাক আৰু টকা লাগে।"
"568","They sell fruit at the market.","bajārat phalmūl bikrī kare.","বজাৰত ফলমূল বিক্ৰী কৰে।"
"569","How much does this cost?","iyār bābe kimān kharac hay?","ইয়াৰ বাবে কিমান খৰচ হয়?"
"570","This is too expensive.","eiṭo bar byayabhul.","এইটো বৰ ব্যয়বহুল।"
"571","That dress is very beautiful.","xei pochākṭo bar dhunīyā.","সেই পোছাকটো বৰ ধুনীয়া।"
"572","The store is open until nine.","xṭa’rkhan na bajālaike kholā thāke.","ষ্ট’ৰখন ন বজালৈকে খোলা থাকে।"
"573","I found a great deal.","bahut bhāl pāichiloṃ.","বহুত ভাল পাইছিলোঁ।"
"574","She bought three pairs of shoes.","tāi tiniyor jotā kinile.","তাই তিনিযোৰ জোতা কিনিলে।"
"575","He spent all his money.","teoṃr xaklo ṭakā kharac karile.","তেওঁৰ সকলো টকা খৰচ কৰিলে।"
"576","We saved enough money for the trip.","āmi bhramṇar bābe yathexṭa ṭakā jamā kariloṃ.","আমি ভ্ৰমণৰ বাবে যথেষ্ট টকা জমা কৰিলোঁ।"
"577","I did not buy anything.","mai eko kinā nāchilo.","মই একো কিনা নাছিলো।"
"578","They returned the broken item.","bhaṅā baxtuṭo ghūrāi dile.","ভঙা বস্তুটো ঘূৰাই দিলে।"
"579","I will go shopping tomorrow.","kāilai bajār kariblai yām.","কাইলৈ বজাৰ কৰিবলৈ যাম।"
"580","She is going to buy a gift for her friend.","tāi bandhur bābe uphār kiniblai olāiche.","তাই বন্ধুৰ বাবে উপহাৰ কিনিবলৈ ওলাইছে।"
"581","The price will go up next month.","ahā māhat br̥ddhi pāb dām.","অহা মাহত বৃদ্ধি পাব দাম।"
"582","Will you pay by card or cash?","kārḍa bā nagad dhanere dhan parixodh karib?","কাৰ্ড বা নগদ ধনেৰে ধন পৰিশোধ কৰিব?"
"583","I am looking for a birthday present.","janmadinar uphār bicāricho.","জন্মদিনৰ উপহাৰ বিচাৰিছো।"
"584","She is trying on a dress.","tāi ḍrech eṭā pindhi cexṭā kari āche.","তাই ড্ৰেছ এটা পিন্ধি চেষ্টা কৰি আছে।"
"585","I have already spent too much.","itimadhye mai bahut kharac karicho.","ইতিমধ্যে মই বহুত খৰচ কৰিছো।"
"586","She has not decided what to buy.","ki kinib tāi ṭhik karā nāi.","কি কিনিব তাই ঠিক কৰা নাই।"
"587","We have been saving for months.","āmi keibāmāho dhari xañcay kari ācho.","আমি কেইবামাহো ধৰি সঞ্চয় কৰি আছো।"
"588","The item was sold out.","baxtuṭo bikrī hai ga’l.","বস্তুটো বিক্ৰী হৈ গ’ল।"
"589","Credit cards are accepted here.","iyāt kreḍiṭ kārḍa grahaṇ karā hay.","ইয়াত ক্ৰেডিট কাৰ্ড গ্ৰহণ কৰা হয়।"
"590","The price has been reduced.","mūlya hrāx karā haiche.","মূল্য হ্ৰাস কৰা হৈছে।"
"591","You can pay at the counter.","kāuṇṭārat dhan dib pārib.","কাউণ্টাৰত ধন দিব পাৰিব।"
"592","I cannot afford this.","eikhini mor xāmarthya nāi.","এইখিনি মোৰ সামৰ্থ্য নাই।"
"593","She should save more money.","tāi āru ṭakā xañcay karib lāge.","তাই আৰু টকা সঞ্চয় কৰিব লাগে।"
"594","May I try this on?","eiṭo cexṭā karib pārimne?","এইটো চেষ্টা কৰিব পাৰিমনে?"
"595","Give me the receipt, please.","racidkhan diyakcon.","ৰচিদখন দিয়কচোন।"
"596","Do not forget your change.","āponār pariwartan pāhri nāyāb.","আপোনাৰ পৰিৱৰ্তন পাহৰি নাযাব।"
"597","If it were cheaper, I would buy it.","xaxtā ha’le kiniloṃheṃten.","সস্তা হ’লে কিনিলোঁহেঁতেন।"
"598","If I had enough money, I would buy a car.","mor hātat paryāpta ṭakā thākile gāṛī ekhan kiniloṃheṃten.","মোৰ হাতত পৰ্যাপ্ত টকা থাকিলে গাড়ী এখন কিনিলোঁহেঁতেন।"
"599","This store is cheaper than that one.","ei dokānkhan xeikhnatkai xaxtā.","এই দোকানখন সেইখনতকৈ সস্তা।"
"600","The black shoes are more expensive than the brown ones.","bādāmī jotātkai ka’lā jotāyorar dām bechi.","বাদামী জোতাতকৈ ক’লা জোতাযোৰৰ দাম বেছি।"
"601","She always finds the best prices.","tāi xadāy uttam dām bicāri pāy.","তাই সদায় উত্তম দাম বিচাৰি পায়।"
"602","I bought a few things.","dui eṭā baxtu kiniloṃ.","দুই এটা বস্তু কিনিলোঁ।"
"603","She did not buy any clothes.","tāi kono kāpor kinā nāchil.","তাই কোনো কাপোৰ কিনা নাছিল।"
"604","Everything in this store is on sale.","ei xṭa’rakhnar xaklo baxtu bikrī karā haiche.","এই ষ্ট’ৰখনৰ সকলো বস্তু বিক্ৰী কৰা হৈছে।"
"605","None of the items were available.","kono baxtuwei powā naga’l.","কোনো বস্তুৱেই পোৱা নগ’ল।"
"606","The store where I bought this is now closed.","mai eiṭo kinā dokānkhan etiyā bandha hai gaiche.","মই এইটো কিনা দোকানখন এতিয়া বন্ধ হৈ গৈছে।"
"607","The shirt that he is wearing was a gift.","pindhi thakā cārṭaṭo āchil uphār.","পিন্ধি থকা চাৰ্টটো আছিল উপহাৰ।"
"608","She bargained for a lower price.","tāi kam dāmat dardām karile.","তাই কম দামত দৰদাম কৰিলে।"
"609","The cashier gave me the wrong change.","keciyāre mok bhul ceñja dile.","কেচিয়াৰে মোক ভুল চেঞ্জ দিলে।"
"610","I always compare prices before buying.","kinār āgte xadāy dām tulnā kari laoṃ.","কিনাৰ আগতে সদায় দাম তুলনা কৰি লওঁ।"
"611","He owes me fifty dollars.","mor ocrat pañcāx ṭakā r̥ṇī.","মোৰ ওচৰত পঞ্চাশ টকা ঋণী।"
"612","We split the bill.","āmi bilkhan bhāg kariloṃ.","আমি বিলখন ভাগ কৰিলোঁ।"
"613","I prefer quality over quantity.","mai parimāṇatkai guṇgat mānhe bechi pachanda karo.","মই পৰিমাণতকৈ গুণগত মানহে বেছি পছন্দ কৰো।"
"614","Money does not grow on trees.","gachat ṭakā gaji nāthāke.","গছত টকা গজি নাথাকে।"
"615","I am happy.","mai xukhī.","মই সুখী।"
"616","She is sad.","tāi dukhī.","তাই দুখী।"
"617","He is angry.","khaṃ uṭhiche tār.","খং উঠিছে তাৰ।"
"618","We are excited.","āmi uttejit hai paricho.","আমি উত্তেজিত হৈ পৰিছো।"
"619","They are afraid of the dark.","āndhārlai bhay kare.","আন্ধাৰলৈ ভয় কৰে।"
"620","I feel lonely.","aklaxrīyā anubhaw hay.","অকলশৰীয়া অনুভৱ হয়।"
"621","She looks worried.","tāik cintit yen lāgiche.","তাইক চিন্তিত যেন লাগিছে।"
"622","He seems tired.","teoṃ yen bhāgruwā hai pariche.","তেওঁ যেন ভাগৰুৱা হৈ পৰিছে।"
"623","The news made me happy.","khabarṭowe mok xukhī kari tulile.","খবৰটোৱে মোক সুখী কৰি তুলিলে।"
"624","The movie was very sad.","cinemākhan bar dukh lāgichil.","চিনেমাখন বৰ দুখ লাগিছিল।"
"625","I love her.","mai tāik bhāl pāoṃ.","মই তাইক ভাল পাওঁ।"
"626","She misses him.","tāi tāk mich kare.","তাই তাক মিছ কৰে।"
"627","He trusts his best friend.","teoṃ nijar bexṭa phreṇḍak bixbāx kare.","তেওঁ নিজৰ বেষ্ট ফ্ৰেণ্ডক বিশ্বাস কৰে।"
"628","We care about each other.","āmi ijne xijnar prati yatna laoṃ.","আমি ইজনে সিজনৰ প্ৰতি যত্ন লওঁ।"
"629","They are getting married.","biyā pātiche.","বিয়া পাতিছে।"
"630","I met her at a party.","pārṭi eṭāt lag pāichilo tāik.","পাৰ্টি এটাত লগ পাইছিলো তাইক।"
"631","She fell in love with him.","tāi tār premat paril.","তাই তাৰ প্ৰেমত পৰিল।"
"632","He asked her to marry him.","xi tāik biyā diblai ka’le.","সি তাইক বিয়া দিবলৈ ক’লে।"
"633","We argued about something silly.","āmi kibā eṭā bokāmay kathāt tarka kariloṃ.","আমি কিবা এটা বোকাময় কথাত তৰ্ক কৰিলোঁ।"
"634","They broke up last year.","yowā bachar duyore mājat brekaāp haichil.","যোৱা বছৰ দুয়োৰে মাজত ব্ৰেকআপ হৈছিল।"
"635","I am not jealous.","mor īrxā nāi.","মোৰ ঈৰ্ষা নাই।"
"636","She does not love him anymore.","tāi āru tāk bhāl nāpāy.","তাই আৰু তাক ভাল নাপায়।"
"637","He never gets angry.","xi ketiyāo khaṃ uṭhā nāi.","সি কেতিয়াও খং উঠা নাই।"
"638","We are not fighting.","āmi yuṃj diyā nāi.","আমি যুঁজ দিয়া নাই।"
"639","Are you happy?","āpuni xukhī neki?","আপুনি সুখী নেকি?"
"640","Why is she crying?","tāi kiy kāndiche?","তাই কিয় কান্দিছে?"
"641","Do you love me?","āpuni mok bhāl pāy ne?","আপুনি মোক ভাল পায় নে?"
"642","How long have you been together?","kimān dinar parā ekelge āche?","কিমান দিনৰ পৰা একেলগে আছে?"
"643","What makes you happy?","kihar bābe āpuni xukhī hay?","কিহৰ বাবে আপুনি সুখী হয়?"
"644","Who is your best friend?","āponār priy bandhu kon?","আপোনাৰ প্ৰিয় বন্ধু কোন?"
"645","I was very happy yesterday.","kāli bar xukhī haichiloṃ.","কালি বৰ সুখী হৈছিলোঁ।"
"646","She cried when she heard the news.","khabarṭo xuni tāi kāndi uṭhil.","খবৰটো শুনি তাই কান্দি উঠিল।"
"647","He apologized for what he said.","teoṃ kowā kathākhinir bābe kxamā khujile.","তেওঁ কোৱা কথাখিনিৰ বাবে ক্ষমা খুজিলে।"
"648","We forgave each other.","āmi ijne xijnak kxamā kari diloṃ.","আমি ইজনে সিজনক ক্ষমা কৰি দিলোঁ।"
"649","They were once very close.","examyat duyo bahut ghanixṭha āchil.","এসময়ত দুয়ো বহুত ঘনিষ্ঠ আছিল।"
"650","I will always love you.","mai tomāk xadāy bhāl pām.","মই তোমাক সদায় ভাল পাম।"
"651","She will be happy when she hears the news.","khabarṭo xuni tāi xukhī ha’b.","খবৰটো শুনি তাই সুখী হ’ব।"
"652","We will work things out.","āmi kathābor ṭhik karim.","আমি কথাবোৰ ঠিক কৰিম।"
"653","I have known her for many years.","mai tāik bahu bachar dhari cini pāicho.","মই তাইক বহু বছৰ ধৰি চিনি পাইছো।"
"654","She has always been kind.","tāi xadāy dayālu hai āhiche.","তাই সদায় দয়ালু হৈ আহিছে।"
"655","They have been friends since childhood.","xarure parā duyore bandhu.","সৰুৰে পৰা দুয়োৰে বন্ধু।"
"656","If you apologize, she will forgive you.","kxamā khujile tāi āponāk kxamā karib.","ক্ষমা খুজিলে তাই আপোনাক ক্ষমা কৰিব।"
"657","If I were not so shy, I would talk to her.","imān lāj nakrile tāir lagat kathā pātiloheṃten.","ইমান লাজ নকৰিলে তাইৰ লগত কথা পাতিলোহেঁতেন।"
"658","If he had told the truth, she would not have been angry.","xi xaṃcā kathā kowā ha’le tāir khaṃ uṭhā nāchil.","সি সঁচা কথা কোৱা হ’লে তাইৰ খং উঠা নাছিল।"
"659","You should be more patient.","āpuni adhik dhairyya dharib lāge.","আপুনি অধিক ধৈৰ্য্য ধৰিব লাগে।"
"660","I cannot stop thinking about her.","tāir kathā bhābi mai bandha karib nowāro.","তাইৰ কথা ভাবি মই বন্ধ কৰিব নোৱাৰো।"
"661","She must learn to forgive.","tāi kxamā kariblai xikib lāgib.","তাই ক্ষমা কৰিবলৈ শিকিব লাগিব।"
"662","He was hurt by her words.","tāir kathāt xi āghāt pāichil.","তাইৰ কথাত সি আঘাত পাইছিল।"
"663","Their friendship is admired by everyone.","teoṃlokar bandhutbak xaklowe praxaṃxā kare.","তেওঁলোকৰ বন্ধুত্বক সকলোৱে প্ৰশংসা কৰে।"
"664","Do not be afraid.","bhay nakrib.","ভয় নকৰিব।"
"665","Be honest with me.","mor lagat xat haok.","মোৰ লগত সৎ হওক।"
"666","Tell me how you feel.","kene lāgiche kowā.","কেনে লাগিছে কোৱা।"
"667","She is kinder than he is.","tāi xitkaio dayālu.","তাই সিতকৈও দয়ালু।"
"668","This is the happiest day of my life.","eiṭowei mor jīwnar āṭāitkai xukhar din.","এইটোৱেই মোৰ জীৱনৰ আটাইতকৈ সুখৰ দিন।"
"669","The person who makes me laugh the most is my sister.","mok āṭāitkai bechi hāṃhiblai diyā mānuhjan mor bhanīyek.","মোক আটাইতকৈ বেছি হাঁহিবলৈ দিয়া মানুহজন মোৰ ভনীয়েক।"
"670","The gift that she gave me means a lot.","tāi mok yi uphār dile tār artha bahut.","তাই মোক যি উপহাৰ দিলে তাৰ অৰ্থ বহুত।"
"671","Jealousy destroys relationships.","īrxāi xamparka dhbaṃx kare.","ঈৰ্ষাই সম্পৰ্ক ধ্বংস কৰে।"
"672","He expressed his feelings openly.","teoṃ nijar anubhawbor muklikai prakāx karile.","তেওঁ নিজৰ অনুভৱবোৰ মুকলিকৈ প্ৰকাশ কৰিলে।"
"673","I appreciate everything you do.","āponār xaklo kāmar mai xalāg laoṃ.","আপোনাৰ সকলো কামৰ মই শলাগ লওঁ।"
"674","She hugged her child tightly.","tāi nijar xantānak jorkai xāwṭi dharile.","তাই নিজৰ সন্তানক জোৰকৈ সাৱটি ধৰিলে।"
"675","Friendship is more important than money.","ṭakātkai bandhutba bechi gurutbapūrṇa.","টকাতকৈ বন্ধুত্ব বেছি গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ।"
"676","Love is patient and kind.","prem dhairyya āru dayālu.","প্ৰেম ধৈৰ্য্য আৰু দয়ালু।"
"677","I wish I could see you more often.","tomāk āru xaghnāi dekhā pāle bhāl āchil.","তোমাক আৰু সঘনাই দেখা পালে ভাল আছিল।"
"678","They reconciled after a long argument.","dīrghadinīyā tarka-bitarkar antat duyore mājat milan ghaṭil.","দীৰ্ঘদিনীয়া তৰ্ক-বিতৰ্কৰ অন্তত দুয়োৰে মাজত মিলন ঘটিল।"
"679","He regrets what he did.","teoṃ yi karile tār bābe anuxocnā kare.","তেওঁ যি কৰিলে তাৰ বাবে অনুশোচনা কৰে।"
"680","She confided in her closest friend.","tāi nijar ghanixṭha bandhur āgat nijar kathā ka’le.","তাই নিজৰ ঘনিষ্ঠ বন্ধুৰ আগত নিজৰ কথা ক’লে।"
"681","I play soccer on weekends.","mai chuṭir dinat phuṭbal khelo.","মই ছুটিৰ দিনত ফুটবল খেলো।"
"682","She reads a book every night.","tāi prati nixā kitāp ekhan paṛhe.","তাই প্ৰতি নিশা কিতাপ এখন পঢ়ে।"
"683","He watches movies at home.","gharte cinemā cāy.","ঘৰতে চিনেমা চায়।"
"684","We go to the park on Sundays.","āmi deobāre pārkalai yāoṃ.","আমি দেওবাৰে পাৰ্কলৈ যাওঁ।"
"685","They listen to music together.","duyo ekelge gān xune.","দুয়ো একেলগে গান শুনে।"
"686","I like to paint.","mai chabi āṃki bhāl pāoṃ.","মই ছবি আঁকি ভাল পাওঁ।"
"687","She enjoys dancing.","tāi nāci bhāl pāy.","তাই নাচি ভাল পায়।"
"688","He is good at chess.","teoṃ dabā khelat bhāl.","তেওঁ দবা খেলত ভাল।"
"689","We went to a concert last night.","yowā nixā āmi eṭā kancārṭalai gaichilo.","যোৱা নিশা আমি এটা কনচাৰ্টলৈ গৈছিলো।"
"690","The movie was very entertaining.","cinemākhan bar manorañjandāyak āchil.","চিনেমাখন বৰ মনোৰঞ্জনদায়ক আছিল।"
"691","I am reading a very interesting book.","bar āmodajnak kitāp ekhan paṛhi āchoṃ.","বৰ আমোদজনক কিতাপ এখন পঢ়ি আছোঁ।"
"692","She is learning to play the guitar.","tāi gīṭār bajāblai xikiche.","তাই গীটাৰ বজাবলৈ শিকিছে।"
"693","He is playing video games.","bhiḍia’ gem kheli āche.","ভিডিঅ’ গেম খেলি আছে।"
"694","We are watching a documentary.","āmi ekhan tathyacitra cāi ācho.","আমি এখন তথ্যচিত্ৰ চাই আছো।"
"695","I played tennis yesterday.","kāli ṭenich khelichilo.","কালি টেনিছ খেলিছিলো।"
"696","She sang a beautiful song.","tāi eṭi xundar gīt gāichil.","তাই এটি সুন্দৰ গীত গাইছিল।"
"697","He won the game.","khelakhnat teoṃ jaylābh kare.","খেলখনত তেওঁ জয়লাভ কৰে।"
"698","We visited the museum last week.","yowā xaptāhat āmi xaṃgrahālayṭo cāloṃ.","যোৱা সপ্তাহত আমি সংগ্ৰহালয়টো চালোঁ।"
"699","They performed a play at the theater.","bhrāmyamāṇ thiyeṭārat nāṭak pradarxan kare.","ভ্ৰাম্যমাণ থিয়েটাৰত নাটক প্ৰদৰ্শন কৰে।"
"700","I did not enjoy the show.","xba’ṭo mor bhāl nālāgil.","শ্ব’টো মোৰ ভাল নালাগিল।"
"701","I will go swimming tomorrow.","kāilai mai xāṃturiblai yām.","কাইলৈ মই সাঁতুৰিবলৈ যাম।"
"702","She will join the art class.","tāi kalā xreṇīt yogdān karib.","তাই কলা শ্ৰেণীত যোগদান কৰিব।"
"703","We will have a party this Saturday.","ei xanibāre āmār pārṭi ha’b.","এই শনিবাৰে আমাৰ পাৰ্টি হ’ব।"
"704","Will you come to the game?","khellai āhibāne?","খেললৈ আহিবানে?"
"705","I have seen that movie before.","xei cinemākhan mai āgteo cāicho.","সেই চিনেমাখন মই আগতেও চাইছো।"
"706","She has read all his books.","tār xaklo kitāp tāi paṛhiche.","তাৰ সকলো কিতাপ তাই পঢ়িছে।"
"707","We have never been to a live concert.","āmi ketiyāo lāibh kancārṭalai yowā nāi.","আমি কেতিয়াও লাইভ কনচাৰ্টলৈ যোৱা নাই।"
"708","He had already won two tournaments before he turned twenty.","bix bachar xampūrṇa howār āgtei teoṃ dukhan pratiyogitāt jaylābh karichil.","বিশ বছৰ সম্পূৰ্ণ হোৱাৰ আগতেই তেওঁ দুখন প্ৰতিযোগিতাত জয়লাভ কৰিছিল।"
"709","The song was written by a famous musician.","gītṭi racnā karichil ejan bikhyāt xaṃgīt xilpīye.","গীতটি ৰচনা কৰিছিল এজন বিখ্যাত সংগীত শিল্পীয়ে।"
"710","The game was won by the home team.","khelakhnat gharuwā dalṭowe jaylābh kare.","খেলখনত ঘৰুৱা দলটোৱে জয়লাভ কৰে।"
"711","The festival is held every year.","prati bachre ei utxaw anuxṭhit hay.","প্ৰতি বছৰে এই উৎসৱ অনুষ্ঠিত হয়।"
"712","A new movie is being filmed in our city.","āmār cahrat ekhan natun cinemār citragrahaṇ cali āche.","আমাৰ চহৰত এখন নতুন চিনেমাৰ চিত্ৰগ্ৰহণ চলি আছে।"
"713","I can swim.","mai xāṃturib pāro.","মই সাঁতুৰিব পাৰো।"
"714","She can play three instruments.","tāi tiniṭā bādyayantra bajāb pāre.","তাই তিনিটা বাদ্যযন্ত্ৰ বজাব পাৰে।"
"715","You should try this game.","āpuni ei khelkhan cexṭā karā ucit.","আপুনি এই খেলখন চেষ্টা কৰা উচিত।"
"716","We could go to the beach.","āmi billai yāb pāriloṃ.","আমি বিললৈ যাব পাৰিলোঁ।"

"718","Let us play a game.","āhak āmi eṭā khel khelo.","আহক আমি এটা খেল খেলো।"

"720","If the weather is good, we will go hiking.","batar bhāl ha’le āmi hāikiṃ kariblai yām.","বতৰ ভাল হ’লে আমি হাইকিং কৰিবলৈ যাম।"
"721","If I had free time, I would learn to paint.","ājri xamay pāle mai chabi āṃkiblai xikiloheṃten.","আজৰি সময় পালে মই ছবি আঁকিবলৈ শিকিলোহেঁতেন।"
"722","If she had practiced more, she would have won.","tāi yadi bechi anuxīlan karileheṃten tente tāi jayī ha’lheṃten.","তাই যদি বেছি অনুশীলন কৰিলেহেঁতেন তেন্তে তাই জয়ী হ’লহেঁতেন।"
"723","I do not like horror movies.","bhayānak cinemā mor bhāl nālāge.","ভয়ানক চিনেমা মোৰ ভাল নালাগে।"

"727","Have you ever played this sport?","āpuni ketiyābā ei khel khelichene?","আপুনি কেতিয়াবা এই খেল খেলিছেনে?"

"730","Did you enjoy the concert?","kancārṭaṭo bhāl lāgil neki?","কনচাৰ্টটো ভাল লাগিল নেকি?"
"731","This book is more interesting than that one.","xeikhnatkai ei kitāpkhan adhik ākarxaṇīy.","সেইখনতকৈ এই কিতাপখন অধিক আকৰ্ষণীয়।"
"732","He is the best player on the team.","teoṃ dalar xrexṭha kheluwai.","তেওঁ দলৰ শ্ৰেষ্ঠ খেলুৱৈ।"
"733","Swimming is more relaxing than running.","daurātkai xāṃtorṭowe adhik xithiltā diye.","দৌৰাতকৈ সাঁতোৰটোৱে অধিক শিথিলতা দিয়ে।"
"734","The actor who played the lead role was brilliant.","mukhya caritrat abhinay karā abhinetājan āchil medhāwī.","মুখ্য চৰিত্ৰত অভিনয় কৰা অভিনেতাজন আছিল মেধাৱী।"
"735","The song that I heard on the radio is stuck in my head.","reḍia’t xunā gānṭo mor mūrat ābaddha hai āche.","ৰেডিঅ’ত শুনা গানটো মোৰ মূৰত আবদ্ধ হৈ আছে।"
"736","I like both reading and writing.","paṛhā-xunā duyoṭāi mor bhāl lāge.","পঢ়া-শুনা দুয়োটাই মোৰ ভাল লাগে।"
"737","She sings well, but she cannot dance.","tāi bhālkai gān gāy, kintu nācib nowāre.","তাই ভালকৈ গান গায়, কিন্তু নাচিব নোৱাৰে।"
"738","He plays guitar because it relaxes him.","teoṃ gīṭār bajāy kāraṇ i teoṃk xithil kare.","তেওঁ গীটাৰ বজায় কাৰণ ই তেওঁক শিথিল কৰে।"
"739","We either go to the movies or stay home.","āmi hay cinemā cāblai yāoṃ nahay gharte thākoṃ.","আমি হয় চিনেমা চাবলৈ যাওঁ নহয় ঘৰতে থাকোঁ।"
"740","Hobbies help you relax.","cakhbore āponāk jirṇi lowāt xahāy kare.","চখবোৰে আপোনাক জিৰণি লোৱাত সহায় কৰে।"
"741","She drew a picture of the sunset.","tāi xūryāxtar chabi ekhan āṃkile.","তাই সূৰ্যাস্তৰ ছবি এখন আঁকিলে।"
"742","The audience clapped loudly.","darxake jorere hāt cāpri bajāle.","দৰ্শকে জোৰেৰে হাত চাপৰি বজালে।"
"743","I used to play the piano as a child.","xarute piyāno bajāichilo.","সৰুতে পিয়ানো বজাইছিলো।"
"744","He taught himself to play the guitar.","teoṃ nijke gīṭār bajāblai xikāichil.","তেওঁ নিজকে গীটাৰ বজাবলৈ শিকাইছিল।"
"745","The park is a great place to exercise.","udyānakhnat byāyām kariblai bhāl ṭhāi.","উদ্যানখনত ব্যায়াম কৰিবলৈ ভাল ঠাই।"
"746","We spent the whole afternoon at the beach.","āmi goṭei duprīyāṭo bilat kaṭāloṃ.","আমি গোটেই দুপৰীয়াটো বিলত কটালোঁ।"
"747","Music brings people together.","xaṃgīte mānuhak ekatrit kare.","সংগীতে মানুহক একত্ৰিত কৰে।"
"748","I downloaded a new app.","natun ep eṭā ḍāunloḍ kariloṃ.","নতুন এপ এটা ডাউনলোড কৰিলোঁ।"
"749","She recommended a great restaurant.","tāi ekhan ḍāṅar rexṭureṇṭar parāmarxa dile.","তাই এখন ডাঙৰ ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্টৰ পৰামৰ্শ দিলে।"
"750","The exhibition opens next month.","ahā māhat mukli ha’b ei pradarxanī.","অহা মাহত মুকলি হ’ব এই প্ৰদৰ্শনী।"
"751","I have been playing chess since I was five.","pāṃc bachar bayxar parāi dabā kheli āhicho.","পাঁচ বছৰ বয়সৰ পৰাই দবা খেলি আহিছো।"

"754","Did they clean the house?","ghar cāphā karile neki?","ঘৰ চাফা কৰিলে নেকি?"
"755","The dishes were washed by my sister.","bācan-bartanbor bhanīyeke dhuichil.","বাচন-বৰ্তনবোৰ ভনীয়েকে ধুইছিল।"
"756","The beds will be made before noon.","duprīyār āgte bicnābor banowā ha’b.","দুপৰীয়াৰ আগতে বিচনাবোৰ বনোৱা হ’ব।"
"757","You will take out the trash.","āwarjanābor uliyāi dibā.","আৱৰ্জনাবোৰ উলিয়াই দিবা।"
"758","Were you sleeping?","xui āchilā neki?","শুই আছিলা নেকি?"
