"760","Have they eaten dinner yet?","etiyāo xihaṃte rātir āhār khāiche neki?","এতিয়াও সিহঁতে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খাইছে নেকি?"
"761","You should not stay up too late.","bechi derilaike xui thakā ucit nahay.","বেছি দেৰিলৈকে শুই থকা উচিত নহয়।"
"762","We could hear the rain from inside.","bhitrar parā barxuṇar xabda āmi xunib pārichilo.","ভিতৰৰ পৰা বৰষুণৰ শব্দ আমি শুনিব পাৰিছিলো।"
"763","If you go to bed late, you will be tired.","derikai xublai ga’le bhāgruwā hai yāb.","দেৰিকৈ শুবলৈ গ’লে ভাগৰুৱা হৈ যাব।"
"764","Do not leave the lights on.","lāiṭ jbalāi rākhib nālāge.","লাইট জ্বলাই ৰাখিব নালাগে।"
"765","I make my bed every morning.","pratidine rātipuwā mor bicnākhan banāoṃ.","প্ৰতিদিনে ৰাতিপুৱা মোৰ বিচনাখন বনাওঁ।"
"766","The laundry has been done.","kāpor dhowār kām xex hai gaiche.","কাপোৰ ধোৱাৰ কাম শেষ হৈ গৈছে।"
"767","Nobody answered the phone.","koneo phonṭo ricibh nakrile.","কোনেও ফোনটো ৰিচিভ নকৰিলে।"
"768","Either you clean up or I will.","hay tumi cāphā karā nahay mai karim.","হয় তুমি চাফা কৰা নহয় মই কৰিম।"
"769","Whose shoes are these?","eibor kār jotā?","এইবোৰ কাৰ জোতা?"
"770","These are not mine.","eibor mor nahay.","এইবোৰ মোৰ নহয়।"
"771","Each of us has a task.","āmār pratyekre eṭā kām āche.","আমাৰ প্ৰত্যেকৰে এটা কাম আছে।"
"772","All of the rooms have been cleaned.","xaklo koṭhā cāphā karā haiche.","সকলো কোঠা চাফা কৰা হৈছে।"
"773","I myself cook dinner on Fridays.","mai nijei xukurbāre rātir āhār rāndho.","মই নিজেই শুকুৰবাৰে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ ৰান্ধো।"
"774","You cooked a wonderful meal.","āpuni eṭā ācrit khādya rāndhile.","আপুনি এটা আচৰিত খাদ্য ৰান্ধিলে।"
"775","We are eating lunch.","āmi duprīyār āhār khāi ācho.","আমি দুপৰীয়াৰ আহাৰ খাই আছো।"
"776","Were they cooking when you arrived?","āpuni āhi powār xamyat xihaṃte randhā-bṛhā kari āchil neki?","আপুনি আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত সিহঁতে ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া কৰি আছিল নেকি?"
"777","Will the food be ready by seven?","xāt bajār bhitrat khādya xāju ha’bne?","সাত বজাৰ ভিতৰত খাদ্য সাজু হ’বনে?"
"778","The cake is being baked right now.","kekṭo etiyāi bek karā haiche.","কেকটো এতিয়াই বেক কৰা হৈছে।"
"779","You should not eat so fast.","imān kharkai khowā ucit nahay.","ইমান খৰকৈ খোৱা উচিত নহয়।"
"780","If she had added more salt, it would have tasted better.","tāi yadi āru nimakh dileheṃten tente tār xowād bhāl ha’lheṃten.","তাই যদি আৰু নিমখ দিলেহেঁতেন তেন্তে তাৰ সোৱাদ ভাল হ’লহেঁতেন।"
"781","I ate neither the soup nor the salad.","mai cūpṭoo nākhāloṃ, cālāḍṭoo nākhāloṃ.","মই চূপটোও নাখালোঁ, চালাডটোও নাখালোঁ।"
"782","Each plate has a different design.","pratikhan pleṭar ḍijāin beleg beleg.","প্ৰতিখন প্লেটৰ ডিজাইন বেলেগ বেলেগ।"
"783","These apples are sweeter than those.","ei āpelbor xeiboratkai miṭhā.","এই আপেলবোৰ সেইবোৰতকৈ মিঠা।"
"784","What kind of food do they eat?","kenedharṇar khādya khāy?","কেনেধৰণৰ খাদ্য খায়?"
"785","We ourselves grew these vegetables.","āmi nijei ei xāk-pāclibor kheti karichiloṃ.","আমি নিজেই এই শাক-পাচলিবোৰ খেতি কৰিছিলোঁ।"
"786","Whose plate is this?","eikhan kār pleṭ?","এইখন কাৰ প্লেট?"
"787","It smells wonderful in here.","iyār bhitrat iyār gondhaṭo ācrit dharṇar.","ইয়াৰ ভিতৰত ইয়াৰ গোন্ধটো আচৰিত ধৰণৰ।"
"788","The more you cook, the better you get.","yimānei rāndhib ximānei bhāl ha’b.","যিমানেই ৰান্ধিব সিমানেই ভাল হ’ব।"
"789","I would rather have tea than coffee.","kaphitkai cāh khāi bhāl pām.","কফিতকৈ চাহ খাই ভাল পাম।"
"790","Somebody left the oven on.","konobāi abhenṭo jbalāi thai ga’l.","কোনোবাই অভেনটো জ্বলাই থৈ গ’ল।"
"791","Has anyone tried this dessert?","ei ḍejārṭaṭo konobāi cexṭā karichene?","এই ডেজাৰ্টটো কোনোবাই চেষ্টা কৰিছেনে?"
"792","Nothing tastes better than homemade bread.","gharte banowā ruṭitkai bechi xowād ekowei nāi.","ঘৰতে বনোৱা ৰুটিতকৈ বেছি সোৱাদ একোৱেই নাই।"
"793","You had better not eat that.","xeiṭo nokhowāi bhāl āchil.","সেইটো নোখোৱাই ভাল আছিল।"
"794","The recipe calls for two cups of flour.","recipit dukāp āṭā khāb lāge.","ৰেচিপিত দুকাপ আটা খাব লাগে।"
"795","We seldom eat out.","āmi bāhirat khub kameihe khāoṃ.","আমি বাহিৰত খুব কমেইহে খাওঁ।"
"796","I will have cooked by the time you arrive.","tumi āhi powār xamaylaike mai rāndhi thai yām.","তুমি আহি পোৱাৰ সময়লৈকে মই ৰান্ধি থৈ যাম।"
"797","You traveled by yourself.","āpuni nijei bhramaṇ karichil.","আপুনি নিজেই ভ্ৰমণ কৰিছিল।"
"798","We have been traveling for a week.","āmi bhramaṇ kari thakā exaptāh ha’l.","আমি ভ্ৰমণ কৰি থকা এসপ্তাহ হ’ল।"
"799","The passengers were asked to remain seated.","yātrīxaklak bahi thākiblai kowā hay.","যাত্ৰীসকলক বহি থাকিবলৈ কোৱা হয়।"
"800","I will have arrived by noon.","duprīyālaike āhi pāmhi.","দুপৰীয়ালৈকে আহি পামহি।"
"801","Were you waiting long?","bahu xamay apekxā kari āchilā neki?","বহু সময় অপেক্ষা কৰি আছিলা নেকি?"
"802","She herself carried all the bags.","tāi nijei xaklo beg kaṛhiyāi lai phurichil.","তাই নিজেই সকলো বেগ কঢ়িয়াই লৈ ফুৰিছিল।"
"803","Nobody told us about the delay.","palmar kathā koneo kowā nāchil.","পলমৰ কথা কোনেও কোৱা নাছিল।"
"804","Whose suitcase is this?","eiṭo kār chuṭkech?","এইটো কাৰ ছুটকেছ?"
"805","The train had left before we arrived.","āmi ahār āgtei relkhan guci gaichil.","আমি অহাৰ আগতেই ৰেলখন গুচি গৈছিল।"
"806","Each passenger must show identification.","pratijan yātrīye paricay patra dekhuwāb lāgib.","প্ৰতিজন যাত্ৰীয়ে পৰিচয় পত্ৰ দেখুৱাব লাগিব।"
"807","I would rather fly than take the bus.","bāchat urātkai mai urājāhāj calāi bhāl pām.","বাছত উৰাতকৈ মই উৰাজাহাজ চলাই ভাল পাম।"
"808","You must not take photographs here.","iyāt phaṭo tulib nālāge.","ইয়াত ফটো তুলিব নালাগে।"
"809","The road was being repaired.","pathṭo merāmtir kām cali āchil.","পথটো মেৰামতিৰ কাম চলি আছিল।"
"810","Neither the bus nor the train was on time.","bāch bā ṭreinao xamyamte nāchil.","বাছ বা ট্ৰেইনও সময়মতে নাছিল।"
"811","Which platform does the train leave from?","konṭo pleṭpharmar parā relkhan rāonā hay?","কোনটো প্লেটফৰ্মৰ পৰা ৰেলখন ৰাওনা হয়?"
"812","Either we walk or we take a taxi.","hay āmi khoj kāṛhi yāoṃ nahay ṭekxi lai yāoṃ.","হয় আমি খোজ কাঢ়ি যাওঁ নহয় টেক্সি লৈ যাওঁ।"
"813","He had never flown before that trip.","xei bhramṇar āgte teoṃ ketiyāo urājāhāj calowā nāchil.","সেই ভ্ৰমণৰ আগতে তেওঁ কেতিয়াও উৰাজাহাজ চলোৱা নাছিল।"
"814","How far is the airport from here?","iyār parā bimānbandarṭo kimān dūr?","ইয়াৰ পৰা বিমানবন্দৰটো কিমান দূৰ?"
"815","The sooner we leave, the earlier we arrive.","āmi yimān xonkāle yāoṃ ximān xonkāle āhi pāoṃ.","আমি যিমান সোনকালে যাওঁ সিমান সোনকালে আহি পাওঁ।"
"816","I was told to wait here.","mok iyāt rai thākiblai kowā haichil.","মোক ইয়াত ৰৈ থাকিবলৈ কোৱা হৈছিল।"
"817","You have a beautiful family.","tomār eṭā dhunīyā pariyāl āche.","তোমাৰ এটা ধুনীয়া পৰিয়াল আছে।"
"818","They are building a new house.","natun ghar xājiche.","নতুন ঘৰ সাজিছে।"
"819","Were your parents born in this country?","āponār pitr̥-mātr̥r janma ei dexte haichil neki?","আপোনাৰ পিতৃ-মাতৃৰ জন্ম এই দেশতে হৈছিল নেকি?"
"820","I will have lived here for twenty years by then.","tetiyālaike mai iyāt bix bachar bāx karim.","তেতিয়ালৈকে মই ইয়াত বিশ বছৰ বাস কৰিম।"
"821","Nobody in our family smokes.","āmār pariyālar konowei dhūmpān nakre.","আমাৰ পৰিয়ালৰ কোনোৱেই ধূমপান নকৰে।"
"822","Both my parents work.","mor mā-deutā duyojnei kām kare.","মোৰ মা-দেউতা দুয়োজনেই কাম কৰে।"
"823","Neither my brother nor my sister lives nearby.","ocarte mor bhāiṭi bā bhanīyek nāthāke.","ওচৰতে মোৰ ভাইটি বা ভনীয়েক নাথাকে।"
"824","Each child has their own room.","pratiṭo xixur nijaxba koṭhā āche.","প্ৰতিটো শিশুৰ নিজস্ব কোঠা আছে।"
"825","Whose toys are these?","eibor kār khelnā?","এইবোৰ কাৰ খেলনা?"
"826","It was my grandmother who taught me to cook.","āitāiyei mok randhā-bṛhā xikāichil.","আইতাইয়েই মোক ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া শিকাইছিল।"
"827","My parents had already gone to bed when I came home.","gharlai āhoṃte mā-deutā itimadhye xui gaichil.","ঘৰলৈ আহোঁতে মা-দেউতা ইতিমধ্যে শুই গৈছিল।"
"828","We ourselves built this fence.","āmi nijei ei berkhan xājiloṃ.","আমি নিজেই এই বেৰখন সাজিলোঁ।"
"829","He is taller than any of his brothers.","teoṃr yikono bhāyekatkai okh.","তেওঁৰ যিকোনো ভায়েকতকৈ ওখ।"
"830","I am the only child in my family.","mor pariyālat mai ekmātra xantān.","মোৰ পৰিয়ালত মই একমাত্ৰ সন্তান।"
"831","You should spend more time with your family.","pariyālar xaite adhik xamay kaṭāb lāge.","পৰিয়ালৰ সৈতে অধিক সময় কটাব লাগে।"
"832","Had they moved before the baby was born?","keṃcuwāṭo janma howār āgtei xihaṃt xthānāntarit haichil neki?","কেঁচুৱাটো জন্ম হোৱাৰ আগতেই সিহঁত স্থানান্তৰিত হৈছিল নেকি?"
"833","The house is being renovated.","gharṭo xaṃxkār karā haiche.","ঘৰটো সংস্কাৰ কৰা হৈছে।"
"834","I wish my family lived closer.","mor pariyālṭo āru ocrat thākile bhāl āchil.","মোৰ পৰিয়ালটো আৰু ওচৰত থাকিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"835","It is my mother who cooks the best meals.","mor māyei āṭāitkai bhāl āhār rāndhe.","মোৰ মায়েই আটাইতকৈ ভাল আহাৰ ৰান্ধে।"
"836","You need to study harder.","adhik kaxṭa kari paṛhāṭo prayojan.","অধিক কষ্ট কৰি পঢ়াটো প্ৰয়োজন।"
"837","We are being trained by the manager.","āmāk menejāre praxikxaṇ di āche.","আমাক মেনেজাৰে প্ৰশিক্ষণ দি আছে।"
"838","Had she finished the project before the deadline?","xamayxīmār āgtei tāi prajekṭaṭo xex karichil neki?","সময়সীমাৰ আগতেই তাই প্ৰজেক্টটো শেষ কৰিছিল নেকি?"
"839","The exam will have been graded by Monday.","xombārar bhitrat parīkxāt greḍ diyā ha’b.","সোমবাৰৰ ভিতৰত পৰীক্ষাত গ্ৰেড দিয়া হ’ব।"
"840","Neither the teacher nor the students understood.","xikxak bā chātra-chātrīyeo buji nāpāle.","শিক্ষক বা ছাত্ৰ-ছাত্ৰীয়েও বুজি নাপালে।"
"841","Each student must write an essay.","pratijan chātrai eṭā racnā likhib lāgib.","প্ৰতিজন ছাত্ৰই এটা ৰচনা লিখিব লাগিব।"
"842","Whose pen is this?","eiṭo kār kalam?","এইটো কাৰ কলম?"
"843","It was the teacher who explained the problem.","xamaxyāṭo bujāi dichil xikxakajne.","সমস্যাটো বুজাই দিছিল শিক্ষকজনে।"
"844","I myself completed the entire project.","mai nijei xamagra prajekṭaṭo xampūrṇa kariloṃ.","মই নিজেই সমগ্ৰ প্ৰজেক্টটো সম্পূৰ্ণ কৰিলোঁ।"
"845","Nobody passed the exam.","koneo parīkxāt uttīrṇa naha’l.","কোনেও পৰীক্ষাত উত্তীৰ্ণ নহ’ল।"
"846","Were you working when I called?","mai phon karār xamyat āpuni kām kari āchil neki?","মই ফোন কৰাৰ সময়ত আপুনি কাম কৰি আছিল নেকি?"
"847","Both the students and the teacher are responsible.","chātra-chātrī āru xikxak ubhyei dāybaddha.","ছাত্ৰ-ছাত্ৰী আৰু শিক্ষক উভয়েই দায়বদ্ধ।"
"848","You had better finish your work.","kām xex karā bhāl āchil.","কাম শেষ কৰা ভাল আছিল।"
"849","Either she resigns or she is fired.","hay tāi padatyāg kare nahay tāik cākrir parā barkhāxta karā hay.","হয় তাই পদত্যাগ কৰে নহয় তাইক চাকৰিৰ পৰা বৰ্খাস্ত কৰা হয়।"
"850","The harder you study, the higher your grades will be.","yimānei kaxṭa kari paṛhib ximānei greḍ bechi ha’b.","যিমানেই কষ্ট কৰি পঢ়িব সিমানেই গ্ৰেড বেছি হ’ব।"
"851","I would rather work from home.","gharar parāi kām kari bhāl pām.","ঘৰৰ পৰাই কাম কৰি ভাল পাম।"
"852","I have not been given a raise.","mok reij diyā howā nāi.","মোক ৰেইজ দিয়া হোৱা নাই।"
"853","Someone forgot their laptop.","konobāi teoṃlokar lepṭapṭo pāhri ga’l.","কোনোবাই তেওঁলোকৰ লেপটপটো পাহৰি গ’ল।"
"854","Whatever you decide, I will support you.","āpuni yi xiddhānta laok, mai āponāk xamarthan karim.","আপুনি যি সিদ্ধান্ত লওক, মই আপোনাক সমৰ্থন কৰিম।"
"855","She asked whether the meeting had been canceled.","xabhākhan bātil karā haiche neki buli tāi xudhile.","সভাখন বাতিল কৰা হৈছে নেকি বুলি তাই সুধিলে।"
"856","You saw the eclipse.","āpuni dekhichil xūryagrahaṇ.","আপুনি দেখিছিল সূৰ্যগ্ৰহণ।"
"857","We have been watching the storm.","āmi dhumuhāṭo cāi āhicho.","আমি ধুমুহাটো চাই আহিছো।"
"858","Were the animals frightened by the thunder?","bajrapātat jīw-jantubor bhay khāichil neki?","বজ্ৰপাতত জীৱ-জন্তুবোৰ ভয় খাইছিল নেকি?"
"859","The crops will have been harvested by October.","akṭobrar bhitrat xaxya capowā ha’b.","অক্টোবৰৰ ভিতৰত শস্য চপোৱা হ’ব।"
"860","Neither the heat nor the cold bothers me.","garam bā ṭhāṇḍāio mok āmni nakre.","গৰম বা ঠাণ্ডাইও মোক আমনি নকৰে।"
"861","Each season has its own beauty.","pratiṭo r̥tur nijaxba xaundaryya āche.","প্ৰতিটো ঋতুৰ নিজস্ব সৌন্দৰ্য্য আছে।"
"862","Whose land is this?","eikhan kār māṭi?","এইখন কাৰ মাটি?"
"863","It was the earthquake that destroyed the bridge.","bhūmikampai dalaṃkhan dhbaṃx kari pelāle.","ভূমিকম্পই দলংখন ধ্বংস কৰি পেলালে।"
"864","We ourselves planted these trees.","āmi nijei ei gachbor ropaṇ kariloṃ.","আমি নিজেই এই গছবোৰ ৰোপণ কৰিলোঁ।"
"865","Nobody expected the flood.","bānpānīr āxā koneo karā nāchil.","বানপানীৰ আশা কোনেও কৰা নাছিল।"
"866","Both the river and the lake are polluted.","nadī āru bil duyoṭā pradūxit.","নদী আৰু বিল দুয়োটা প্ৰদূষিত।"
"867","Either it rains or it stays dry.","hay barxuṇ hay nahay xukān hai thāke.","হয় বৰষুণ হয় নহয় শুকান হৈ থাকে।"
"868","The longer it rains, the higher the river rises.","barxuṇ yimānei bechi ximānei oparlai uṭhi yāy.","বৰষুণ যিমানেই বেছি সিমানেই ওপৰলৈ উঠি যায়।"
"869","I would rather have sunshine.","mai ra’d powāṭowei bhāl pām.","মই ৰ’দ পোৱাটোৱেই ভাল পাম।"
"870","You had better bring warm clothes.","garam kāpor anāi bhāl āchil.","গৰম কাপোৰ অনাই ভাল আছিল।"
"871","Whatever happens, nature will recover.","yiyei nahaok prakr̥tiye xuxtha hai uṭhib.","যিয়েই নহওক প্ৰকৃতিয়ে সুস্থ হৈ উঠিব।"
"872","The forest has been burning for days.","dine dine jbali āche araṇyakhan.","দিনে দিনে জ্বলি আছে অৰণ্যখন।"
"873","Had the volcano erupted before?","āgte āgneygiriṭo bixphoraṇ haichil neki?","আগতে আগ্নেয়গিৰিটো বিস্ফোৰণ হৈছিল নেকি?"
"874","It is being investigated whether the water is safe.","pānī nirāpad ne nahay tār tadanta cali āche.","পানী নিৰাপদ নে নহয় তাৰ তদন্ত চলি আছে।"
"875","The plants need to be watered.","gach-gachniborak pānī diyār prayojan.","গছ-গছনিবোৰক পানী দিয়াৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"876","Somebody must have seen the bear.","konobāi nixcay bhālukṭo dekhiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় ভালুকটো দেখিছে।"
"877","It seldom snows in this region.","ei añcalat baraph khub kameihe pare.","এই অঞ্চলত বৰফ খুব কমেইহে পৰে।"
"878","I wish it were warmer.","garam ha’le bhāl āchil.","গৰম হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"879","How deep is the lake?","hradṭo kimān da?","হ্ৰদটো কিমান দ?"
"880","The sky turned red at sunset.","xūryāxtar xamyat ākāxkhan raṅā hai paril.","সূৰ্যাস্তৰ সময়ত আকাশখন ৰঙা হৈ পৰিল।"
"881","Flowers are watered by the gardener every morning.","pratidine rātipuwā māliye phular pānī diye.","প্ৰতিদিনে ৰাতিপুৱা মালিয়ে ফুলৰ পানী দিয়ে।"
"882","You look pale.","tomāk xeṃtā dekhā gaiche.","তোমাক শেঁতা দেখা গৈছে।"
"883","We were examined by the specialist.","bixexajñajne āmāk parīkxā karile.","বিশেষজ্ঞজনে আমাক পৰীক্ষা কৰিলে।"
"884","Had he taken his medicine before the attack?","ākramṇar āgte teoṃ nijar auxadh khāichil neki?","আক্ৰমণৰ আগতে তেওঁ নিজৰ ঔষধ খাইছিল নেকি?"
"885","The surgery will have been completed by noon.","duprīyār bhitrat axtropcār xampūrṇa ha’b.","দুপৰীয়াৰ ভিতৰত অস্ত্ৰোপচাৰ সম্পূৰ্ণ হ’ব।"
"886","Neither the doctor nor the nurse was available.","ḍāktar bā nārca konowei powā nāchil.","ডাক্তৰ বা নাৰ্চ কোনোৱেই পোৱা নাছিল।"
"887","Each patient needs individual attention.","pratijan rogīk byaktigat manoyogar prayojan.","প্ৰতিজন ৰোগীক ব্যক্তিগত মনোযোগৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"888","Whose prescription is this?","eiṭo kār prechakripcan?","এইটো কাৰ প্ৰেছক্ৰিপচন?"
"889","It was the infection that caused the fever.","xaṃkramṇei jbarar xr̥xṭi karichil.","সংক্ৰমণেই জ্বৰৰ সৃষ্টি কৰিছিল।"
"890","I myself changed the bandage.","mai nijei beṇḍej xalni kariloṃ.","মই নিজেই বেণ্ডেজ সলনি কৰিলোঁ।"
"891","Nobody heard him call for help.","teoṃ xahāy bicāri mātiblai koneo xunā nāchil.","তেওঁ সহায় বিচাৰি মাতিবলৈ কোনেও শুনা নাছিল।"
"892","Both his arms and his legs were broken.","duyokhan hāt āru bhari dukhan bhāṅi gaichil.","দুয়োখন হাত আৰু ভৰি দুখন ভাঙি গৈছিল।"
"893","Either you rest or you will get worse.","hay jirṇi laok nahay beyā hai yāb.","হয় জিৰণি লওক নহয় বেয়া হৈ যাব।"
"894","The sooner you see a doctor, the better.","yimān xonkāle ḍāktarar ocarlai yāb ximānei bhāl.","যিমান সোনকালে ডাক্তৰৰ ওচৰলৈ যাব সিমানেই ভাল।"
"895","I would rather avoid surgery.","axtropcār erāi calāṭowei bhāl pām.","অস্ত্ৰোপচাৰ এৰাই চলাটোৱেই ভাল পাম।"
"896","You had better take this seriously.","ei kathāṭo āpuni gurutbaxahkāre lowāṭowei bhāl āchil.","এই কথাটো আপুনি গুৰুত্বসহকাৰে লোৱাটোৱেই ভাল আছিল।"
"897","Whatever the diagnosis, we will face it together.","nidān yiyei nahaok kiy, āmi ekelge iyār xanmukhīn ha’m.","নিদান যিয়েই নহওক কিয়, আমি একেলগে ইয়াৰ সন্মুখীন হ’ম।"
"898","The wound is being treated.","ghāṃr cikitxā cali āche.","ঘাঁৰ চিকিৎসা চলি আছে।"
"899","Someone called an ambulance.","konobāi embulenxa mātile.","কোনোবাই এম্বুলেন্স মাতিলে।"
"900","I wish I had gone to the doctor sooner.","xonkāle ḍāktarar ocarlai yowā ha’le bhāl āchil.","সোনকালে ডাক্তৰৰ ওচৰলৈ যোৱা হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"901","How long has she been in the hospital?","kimān din haxpitālat āche tāi?","কিমান দিন হস্পিতালত আছে তাই?"
"902","The patient is being monitored closely.","rogījnar oprat tīkxṇa najar rakhā haiche.","ৰোগীজনৰ ওপৰত তীক্ষ্ণ নজৰ ৰখা হৈছে।"
"903","His condition has improved significantly.","teoṃr awaxthār yathexṭa unnati haiche.","তেওঁৰ অৱস্থাৰ যথেষ্ট উন্নতি হৈছে।"
"904","She is too weak to walk.","tāi khoj kāṛhib nowārākaiye durbal.","তাই খোজ কাঢ়িব নোৱাৰাকৈয়ে দুৰ্বল।"
"905","The doctor asked him to lie down.","ḍāktare tāk xublai ka’le.","ডাক্তৰে তাক শুবলৈ ক’লে।"
"906","I rarely get sick.","mor bemār khub kameihe hay.","মোৰ বেমাৰ খুব কমেইহে হয়।"
"907","Regular exercise keeps you healthy.","niymit byāyāme āponāk xuxtha kari rākhe.","নিয়মিত ব্যায়ামে আপোনাক সুস্থ কৰি ৰাখে।"
"908","You paid too much for that.","tār bābe āpuni bechi ṭakā dichil.","তাৰ বাবে আপুনি বেছি টকা দিছিল।"
"909","We are being overcharged.","āmār parā atirikta cārja lowā haiche.","আমাৰ পৰা অতিৰিক্ত চাৰ্জ লোৱা হৈছে।"
"910","Had they already closed when you arrived?","āpuni ahār xamyat itimadhye bandha hai gaichil neki?","আপুনি অহাৰ সময়ত ইতিমধ্যে বন্ধ হৈ গৈছিল নেকি?"
"911","The order will have been delivered by Friday.","xukurbārar bhitrat arḍār ḍelibhārī ha’b.","শুকুৰবাৰৰ ভিতৰত অৰ্ডাৰ ডেলিভাৰী হ’ব।"
"912","Neither the red dress nor the blue one fits me.","raṅā pochāk bā nīlā pochākṭoo mor lagat khāp nākhāy.","ৰঙা পোছাক বা নীলা পোছাকটোও মোৰ লগত খাপ নাখায়।"
"913","Each item has a different price.","pratiṭo baxtur dām beleg beleg.","প্ৰতিটো বস্তুৰ দাম বেলেগ বেলেগ।"
"914","Whose wallet is this?","eiṭo kār mānibeg?","এইটো কাৰ মানিবেগ?"
"915","It was the manager who helped me.","mok xahāy karā menejārehe.","মোক সহায় কৰা মেনেজাৰেহে।"
"916","I myself wrapped all the gifts.","mai nijei xaklo uphār meriyāi laichilo.","মই নিজেই সকলো উপহাৰ মেৰিয়াই লৈছিলো।"
"917","Nobody bought the old furniture.","purṇi ācbāb koneo kinā nāchil.","পুৰণি আচবাব কোনেও কিনা নাছিল।"
"918","Both the quality and the price are good.","mān āru dām duyoṭā bhāl.","মান আৰু দাম দুয়োটা ভাল।"
"919","Either I buy it now or someone else will.","hay mai etiyāi kinim nahay ān konobāi kinib.","হয় মই এতিয়াই কিনিম নহয় আন কোনোবাই কিনিব।"
"920","The more you buy, the more you save.","yimānei kinib ximānei xañcay karib.","যিমানেই কিনিব সিমানেই সঞ্চয় কৰিব।"
"921","I would rather save the money.","mai ṭakākhini jamā kari rakhāṭowei bhāl pām.","মই টকাখিনি জমা কৰি ৰখাটোৱেই ভাল পাম।"
"922","You had better check your receipt.","āpuni āponār racidkhan parīkxā karāṭo bhāl āchil.","আপুনি আপোনাৰ ৰচিদখন পৰীক্ষা কৰাটো ভাল আছিল।"
"923","Whatever she buys, she always returns it.","yi kinib tāi xadāy ghūrāi diye.","যি কিনিব তাই সদায় ঘূৰাই দিয়ে।"
"924","The product is being recalled.","praḍākṭaṭo punar āhbān karā haiche.","প্ৰডাক্টটো পুনৰ আহ্বান কৰা হৈছে।"
"925","Someone stole my wallet.","konobāi mor mānibegṭo curi kari lai ga’l.","কোনোবাই মোৰ মানিবেগটো চুৰি কৰি লৈ গ’ল।"
"926","I wish it were cheaper.","xaxtā ha’le bhāl āchil.","সস্তা হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"927","How much do we owe?","āmi kimān r̥ṇī?","আমি কিমান ঋণী?"
"928","They themselves made this product.","teoṃloke nijei ei xāmagrīṭo nirmāṇ karichil.","তেওঁলোকে নিজেই এই সামগ্ৰীটো নিৰ্মাণ কৰিছিল।"
"929","Hardly anyone shops there.","tāt prāyei konowe bajār nakre.","তাত প্ৰায়েই কোনোৱে বজাৰ নকৰে।"
"930","You seem upset.","tumi yen biclit haichā.","তুমি যেন বিচলিত হৈছা।"
"931","We are being ignored.","āmāk āokāṇ karā haiche.","আমাক আওকাণ কৰা হৈছে।"
"932","Had they spoken to each other before the meeting?","xabhār āgte ijne xijnar lagat kathā pātichil neki?","সভাৰ আগতে ইজনে সিজনৰ লগত কথা পাতিছিল নেকি?"
"933","The issue will have been resolved by tomorrow.","kāilair bhitrat xamaxyāṭo xamādhān ha’b.","কাইলৈৰ ভিতৰত সমস্যাটো সমাধান হ’ব।"
"934","Neither he nor she wanted to apologize.","xi bā tāio kxamā khujib bicrā nāchil.","সি বা তাইও ক্ষমা খুজিব বিচৰা নাছিল।"
"935","Each person reacted differently.","pratijan byaktiye beleg beleg pratikriyā prakāx karichil.","প্ৰতিজন ব্যক্তিয়ে বেলেগ বেলেগ প্ৰতিক্ৰিয়া প্ৰকাশ কৰিছিল।"
"936","It was she who forgave first.","tāiyei prathme kxamā karichil.","তাইয়েই প্ৰথমে ক্ষমা কৰিছিল।"
"937","I myself do not understand my own feelings.","mai nijeo nijar anubhaw buji nāpāoṃ.","মই নিজেও নিজৰ অনুভৱ বুজি নাপাওঁ।"
"938","Nobody expected the surprise.","ācrit ha’b buli koneo āxā karā nāchil.","আচৰিত হ’ব বুলি কোনেও আশা কৰা নাছিল।"
"939","Both happiness and sadness are part of life.","xukh āru dukh duyoṭā jīwnar aṃg.","সুখ আৰু দুখ দুয়োটা জীৱনৰ অংগ।"
"940","Either we talk or the problem gets worse.","hay āmi kathā pāto nahay xamaxyāṭo āru bechi beyā hai yāy.","হয় আমি কথা পাতো নহয় সমস্যাটো আৰু বেছি বেয়া হৈ যায়।"
"941","The longer you wait, the harder it becomes.","yimānei bechi xamay apekxā karib ximānei kaṭhin hai pare.","যিমানেই বেছি সময় অপেক্ষা কৰিব সিমানেই কঠিন হৈ পৰে।"
"942","I would rather be alone right now.","mai etiyāi akle thakāṭowei bhāl pām.","মই এতিয়াই অকলে থকাটোৱেই ভাল পাম।"
"943","You had better apologize.","āpuni kxamā khujile bhāl āchil.","আপুনি ক্ষমা খুজিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"944","Whatever happens between us, I will remember you.","āmār mājat yiyei nahaok, mai tomāk manat rākhim.","আমাৰ মাজত যিয়েই নহওক, মই তোমাক মনত ৰাখিম।"
"945","She had been crying before I arrived.","mai ahār āgte tāi kāndi āchil.","মই অহাৰ আগতে তাই কান্দি আছিল।"
"946","Someone must have told her.","konobāi nixcay tāik kaiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় তাইক কৈছে।"
"947","I wish we had met sooner.","xonkāle lag powā ha’le bhāl āchil.","সোনকালে লগ পোৱা হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"948","How do you cope with stress?","mānxik cāpar xaite kenekai mokābilā kare?","মানসিক চাপৰ সৈতে কেনেকৈ মোকাবিলা কৰে?"
"949","Hardly anyone noticed his sadness.","teoṃr dukhṭo prāy koneo lakxya karā nāchil.","তেওঁৰ দুখটো প্ৰায় কোনেও লক্ষ্য কৰা নাছিল।"
"950","They themselves decided to separate.","duyo nijei pr̥thak howār xiddhānta laichil.","দুয়ো নিজেই পৃথক হোৱাৰ সিদ্ধান্ত লৈছিল।"
"951","It is believed that love conquers all.","preme xakloke jay kare buli bixbāx karā hay.","প্ৰেমে সকলোকে জয় কৰে বুলি বিশ্বাস কৰা হয়।"
"952","Were you surprised by the news?","khabarṭo pāi āpuni ācrit haichil neki?","খবৰটো পাই আপুনি আচৰিত হৈছিল নেকি?"
"953","You played very well.","āpuni bahut bhāl khelichil.","আপুনি বহুত ভাল খেলিছিল।"
"954","We are being entertained by a magician.","āmāk manorañjan di āche ejan yādukar.","আমাক মনোৰঞ্জন দি আছে এজন যাদুকৰ।"
"955","Had she practiced before the performance?","abhinyar āgte tāi anuxīlan karichil neki?","অভিনয়ৰ আগতে তাই অনুশীলন কৰিছিল নেকি?"
"956","The movie will have started by the time we arrive.","āmi ahār xamaylaike cinemākhan ārambha hai yāb.","আমি অহাৰ সময়লৈকে চিনেমাখন আৰম্ভ হৈ যাব।"
"957","Neither the book nor the movie was interesting.","kitāpkhan bā cinemākhno ākarxaṇīy nāchil.","কিতাপখন বা চিনেমাখনো আকৰ্ষণীয় নাছিল।"
"958","Each player received a medal.","pratijan kheluwaiye eṭākai padak lābh karichil.","প্ৰতিজন খেলুৱৈয়ে এটাকৈ পদক লাভ কৰিছিল।"
"959","Whose guitar is this?","eiṭo kār gīṭār?","এইটো কাৰ গীটাৰ?"
"960","It was the goal that won the match.","xei ga’lṭowei mecakhnat jayī haichil.","সেই গ’লটোৱেই মেচখনত জয়ী হৈছিল।"
"961","I myself composed this song.","ei gītṭo mai nijei racnā kariloṃ.","এই গীতটো মই নিজেই ৰচনা কৰিলোঁ।"
"962","Nobody scored in the first half.","prathmārdhat koneo ga’l nidile.","প্ৰথমাৰ্ধত কোনেও গ’ল নিদিলে।"
"963","Both the actors and the director were praised.","abhinetā āru paricālak duyoke praxaṃxā lābh kare.","অভিনেতা আৰু পৰিচালক দুয়োকে প্ৰশংসা লাভ কৰে।"
"964","Either we win or we learn.","hay āmi jayī haoṃ nahay āmi xiko.","হয় আমি জয়ী হওঁ নহয় আমি শিকো।"
"965","The more you practice, the better you play.","yimānei anuxīlan karib ximānei bhāl khelib.","যিমানেই অনুশীলন কৰিব সিমানেই ভাল খেলিব।"
"966","I would rather watch a comedy.","mai kameḍī ekhan cāi bhāl pām.","মই কমেডী এখন চাই ভাল পাম।"
"967","You had better not miss the show.","xba’ṭo mich nakrāṭowei bhāl āchil.","শ্ব’টো মিছ নকৰাটোৱেই ভাল আছিল।"
"968","Whatever you choose, it will be fun.","yi bāchi laok, majār ha’b.","যি বাছি লওক, মজাৰ হ’ব।"
"969","The show had been sold out for weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari xba’ṭo bikrī hai gaichil.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি শ্ব’টো বিক্ৰী হৈ গৈছিল।"
"970","Someone left their jacket at the theater.","konobāi teoṃlokar jekeṭṭo thiyeṭārat thai gaichil.","কোনোবাই তেওঁলোকৰ জেকেটটো থিয়েটাৰত থৈ গৈছিল।"
"971","I wish I could play an instrument.","bādyayantra bajāb pārile bhāl āchil.","বাদ্যযন্ত্ৰ বজাব পাৰিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"972","How many people attended the festival?","mahotxawat kimānjan lok upaxthit āchil?","মহোৎসৱত কিমানজন লোক উপস্থিত আছিল?"
"973","We ourselves organized the event.","āmi nijei āyojan karichilo anuxṭhānṭo.","আমি নিজেই আয়োজন কৰিছিলো অনুষ্ঠানটো।"
"974","Hardly anyone came to the recital.","ābr̥ttilai prāy konowei nāhil.","আবৃত্তিলৈ প্ৰায় কোনোৱেই নাহিল।"
"975","It is said that he is the most talented musician.","kowā hay ye teoṃ āṭāitkai pratibhāxālī xaṃgītajña.","কোৱা হয় যে তেওঁ আটাইতকৈ প্ৰতিভাশালী সংগীতজ্ঞ।"
"976","Were the children allowed to participate?","la’rā-chowālīk aṃxagrahaṇ kariblai diyā haichil neki?","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীক অংশগ্ৰহণ কৰিবলৈ দিয়া হৈছিল নেকি?"
"977","The crowd cheered as the team entered the field.","dalṭowe khelapthārat prawex karār lage lage rāije ullāx karichil.","দলটোৱে খেলপথাৰত প্ৰৱেশ কৰাৰ লগে লগে ৰাইজে উল্লাস কৰিছিল।"
"978","He is too young to enter this club.","ei klābat prawex kariblai teoṃ bahut xaru.","এই ক্লাবত প্ৰৱেশ কৰিবলৈ তেওঁ বহুত সৰু।"
"979","I speak two languages.","mai duṭā bhāxā kaoṃ.","মই দুটা ভাষা কওঁ।"
"980","She writes letters to her friends.","bandhu-bāndhawīlai ciṭhi likhe.","বন্ধু-বান্ধৱীলৈ চিঠি লিখে।"
"981","He calls his mother every Sunday.","prati deobāre mākak phon kare.","প্ৰতি দেওবাৰে মাকক ফোন কৰে।"
"982","We chat online every evening.","āmi pratidine xandhiyā anlāin āḍḍā māro.","আমি প্ৰতিদিনে সন্ধিয়া অনলাইন আড্ডা মাৰো।"
"983","They greet each other warmly.","ijne xijnak uxma xambhāxaṇ janāy.","ইজনে সিজনক উষ্ম সম্ভাষণ জনায়।"
"984","I sent her an email.","mai tāik imeil eṭā paṭhāloṃ.","মই তাইক ইমেইল এটা পঠালোঁ।"
"985","She received a message from him.","tār parā tāi eṭā mechej pāle.","তাৰ পৰা তাই এটা মেছেজ পালে।"
"986","He introduced himself to everyone.","xaklore lagat cināki karāi dile.","সকলোৰে লগত চিনাকি কৰাই দিলে।"
"987","We have been friends for years.","āmi bachar bachar dhari bandhutba kari āhicho.","আমি বছৰ বছৰ ধৰি বন্ধুত্ব কৰি আহিছো।"
"988","They promised to help us.","āmāk xahāy karār pratixruti dile.","আমাক সহায় কৰাৰ প্ৰতিশ্ৰুতি দিলে।"
"989","I do not understand what he is saying.","teoṃ ki kaiche mai buji powā nāi.","তেওঁ কি কৈছে মই বুজি পোৱা নাই।"
"990","She did not reply to my message.","tāi mor mechejar kono uttar nidile.","তাই মোৰ মেছেজৰ কোনো উত্তৰ নিদিলে।"
"991","He never lies.","teoṃ ketiyāo michā nakay.","তেওঁ কেতিয়াও মিছা নকয়।"
"992","We have not spoken in months.","māhar pichat māh dhari āmi kathā patā nāi.","মাহৰ পিছত মাহ ধৰি আমি কথা পতা নাই।"
"993","Do you speak English?","āpuni iṃrājī kay neki?","আপুনি ইংৰাজী কয় নেকি?"
"994","What did she say?","tāi ki ka’le?","তাই কি ক’লে?"
"995","Who called you?","kone phon karichil tomāk?","কোনে ফোন কৰিছিল তোমাক?"
"996","Can you translate this for me?","mor bābe eiṭo anubād karib pāribne?","মোৰ বাবে এইটো অনুবাদ কৰিব পাৰিবনে?"
"997","How do you say this in your language?","āponār bhāxāt ei kathā kenekai kay?","আপোনাৰ ভাষাত এই কথা কেনেকৈ কয়?"
"998","Have you heard the news?","khabarṭo xunichene?","খবৰটো শুনিছেনে?"
"999","I will call you later.","pichat phon karim.","পিছত ফোন কৰিম।"
"1000","She is going to give a speech.","tāi bhāxaṇ diblai olāiche.","তাই ভাষণ দিবলৈ ওলাইছে।"
"1001","We will discuss it tomorrow.","āmi kāilai ālocnā karim.","আমি কাইলৈ আলোচনা কৰিম।"
"1002","Will they announce the results?","teoṃloke ghoxṇā karibne phalāphal?","তেওঁলোকে ঘোষণা কৰিবনে ফলাফল?"
"1003","I was talking to her when you interrupted.","mai tāir lagat kathā pāti āchilo yetiyā tumi bādhā dilā.","মই তাইৰ লগত কথা পাতি আছিলো যেতিয়া তুমি বাধা দিলা।"
"1004","She is explaining the rules.","tāi niyambor bujāi āche.","তাই নিয়মবোৰ বুজাই আছে।"
"1005","He had already apologized before she arrived.","tāi ahār āgtei xi kxamā khujichil.","তাই অহাৰ আগতেই সি ক্ষমা খুজিছিল।"
"1006","We have already discussed this matter.","ei bixye āmi itimadhye ālocnā karicho.","এই বিষয়ে আমি ইতিমধ্যে আলোচনা কৰিছো।"
"1007","The message was sent by mistake.","bhulte mechejṭo paṭhiowā haichil.","ভুলতে মেছেজটো পঠিওৱা হৈছিল।"
"1008","The speech was heard by thousands.","bhāxaṇṭo hājār hājār loke xunichil.","ভাষণটো হাজাৰ হাজাৰ লোকে শুনিছিল।"
"1009","English is spoken all over the world.","xamagra bixbate iṃrājī kowā hay.","সমগ্ৰ বিশ্বতে ইংৰাজী কোৱা হয়।"
"1010","The news is being reported now.","khabarṭo etiyāi khabar diyā haiche.","খবৰটো এতিয়াই খবৰ দিয়া হৈছে।"
"1011","Speak clearly.","xpaxṭakai kathā kaok.","স্পষ্টকৈ কথা কওক।"
"1012","Do not interrupt me.","mor kathāt bādhā nidibā.","মোৰ কথাত বাধা নিদিবা।"
"1013","Listen to what I am saying.","mai ki kaicho xunā.","মই কি কৈছো শুনা।"
"1014","Let us agree to disagree.","āmi matānaikya kariblai mānti haoṃ.","আমি মতানৈক্য কৰিবলৈ মান্তি হওঁ।"
"1015","You should listen more carefully.","āpuni adhik manoyogere xunā ucit.","আপুনি অধিক মনোযোগেৰে শুনা উচিত।"
"1016","I must tell you something important.","eṭā gurutbapūrṇa kathā ka'b lāgib.","এটা গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ কথা ক'ব লাগিব।"
"1017","She might not come to the meeting.","tāi hayto xabhālai nāhib.","তাই হয়তো সভালৈ নাহিব।"
"1018","He could speak five languages.","teoṃ pāṃcṭā bhāxā ka’b pārichil.","তেওঁ পাঁচটা ভাষা ক’ব পাৰিছিল।"
"1019","If you explain it clearly, everyone will understand.","xpaxṭakai bujāi dile xaklowe bujib.","স্পষ্টকৈ বুজাই দিলে সকলোৱে বুজিব।"
"1020","If I had known your number, I would have called you.","tomār nambarṭo janā ha'le tomāk phon kariloheṃten.","তোমাৰ নম্বৰটো জনা হ'লে তোমাক ফোন কৰিলোহেঁতেন।"
"1021","If she were here, she would agree.","tāi iyāt thākile tāi mānti ha’lheṃten.","তাই ইয়াত থাকিলে তাই মান্তি হ’লহেঁতেন।"
"1022","This language is more difficult than that one.","xei bhāxātkai ei bhāxāṭo adhik kaṭhin.","সেই ভাষাতকৈ এই ভাষাটো অধিক কঠিন।"
"1023","He is the most talkative person I know.","teoṃ mor cināki āṭāitkai kathā kowā mānuh.","তেওঁ মোৰ চিনাকি আটাইতকৈ কথা কোৱা মানুহ।"
"1024","She speaks more fluently than I do.","tāi motkai bechi xāwlīlbhāwe kathā kay.","তাই মোতকৈ বেছি সাৱলীলভাৱে কথা কয়।"
"1025","The meeting that was held yesterday was productive.","kāli yikhan xabhā anuxṭhit haichil xeyā āchil utpādanxīl.","কালি যিখন সভা অনুষ্ঠিত হৈছিল সেয়া আছিল উৎপাদনশীল।"
"1026","I met the woman who translated the book.","kitāpkhan anubād karā mahilāgrākīk lag pāloṃ","কিতাপখন অনুবাদ কৰা মহিলাগৰাকীক লগ পালোঁ৷"
"1027","Both speaking and listening are important.","kathā kowā āru xunā duyoṭā gurutbapūrṇa.","কথা কোৱা আৰু শুনা দুয়োটা গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ।"
"1028","Neither she nor I knew the answer.","uttarṭo tāi bā mai nājāniloṃ.","উত্তৰটো তাই বা মই নাজানিলোঁ।"
"1029","Not only did he apologize, but he also offered to help.","kewal kxamā bicrāi nahay, xahāy karār praxtāwo āgabṛhāichil.","কেৱল ক্ষমা বিচৰাই নহয়, সহায় কৰাৰ প্ৰস্তাৱো আগবঢ়াইছিল।"
"1030","Whatever you say, I will keep it a secret.","yi kab, mai gopan kari rākhim.","যি কব, মই গোপন কৰি ৰাখিম।"
"1031","I would rather text than call.","phon karātkai mechej karāṭowei bhāl pām.","ফোন কৰাতকৈ মেছেজ কৰাটোৱেই ভাল পাম।"
"1032","Hardly anyone understood the lecture.","baktr̥tāṭo prāy konowei buji nāpāle.","বক্তৃতাটো প্ৰায় কোনোৱেই বুজি নাপালে।"
"1033","It is said that he speaks seven languages.","kowā hay ye teoṃ xātṭā bhāxā kay.","কোৱা হয় যে তেওঁ সাতটা ভাষা কয়।"
"1034","Everyone deserves to be heard.","xaklowe xunā powār yogya.","সকলোৱে শুনা পোৱাৰ যোগ্য।"
"1035","Will you lock the door?","duwārat talā mārib neki?","দুৱাৰত তলা মাৰিব নেকি?"
"1036","They used to walk to school.","āgte khojkāṛhi xkullai gaichil.","আগতে খোজকাঢ়ি স্কুললৈ গৈছিল।"
"1037","We had not finished eating when the phone rang.","āmi khāi xex nohowātei phonṭo bāji uṭhil.","আমি খাই শেষ নোহোৱাতেই ফোনটো বাজি উঠিল।"
"1038","The floor is swept every evening.","prati xandhiyā majiyākhan jhāṛu diyā hay.","প্ৰতি সন্ধিয়া মজিয়াখন ঝাড়ু দিয়া হয়।"
"1039","Are the children sleeping?","la’rā-chowālībor xui āche neki?","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰ শুই আছে নেকি?"
"1040","I used to wake up early.","āgte mai xonkāle xār pāichiloṃ.","আগতে মই সোনকালে সাৰ পাইছিলোঁ।"
"1041","She will not forget her keys again.","tāi āru tāir cābibor pāhrib nowāre.","তাই আৰু তাইৰ চাবিবোৰ পাহৰিব নোৱাৰে।"
"1042","Let the children play outside.","la’rā-chowālīborak bāhirat kheliblai diyak.","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰক বাহিৰত খেলিবলৈ দিয়ক।"
"1043","The table is set for dinner.","rātir āhār grahṇar bābe ṭebulkhan ceṭ karā haiche.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ গ্ৰহণৰ বাবে টেবুলখন চেট কৰা হৈছে।"
"1044","We were not ready when they came.","teoṃlok āhile āmi xāju nāchiloṃ.","তেওঁলোক আহিলে আমি সাজু নাছিলোঁ।"
"1045","Whenever it rains, I stay home.","yetiyāi barxuṇ hay tetiyāi gharte thākoṃ.","যেতিয়াই বৰষুণ হয় তেতিয়াই ঘৰতে থাকোঁ।"
"1046","They take turns cleaning the kitchen.","pāl pāti pākghar cāphā kare.","পাল পাতি পাকঘৰ চাফা কৰে।"
"1047","How often do you brush your hair?","kimān xaghnāi culi brāch kare?","কিমান সঘনাই চুলি ব্ৰাছ কৰে?"
"1048","My alarm has not gone off yet.","mor elārma etiyāo bāji uṭhā nāi.","মোৰ এলাৰ্ম এতিয়াও বাজি উঠা নাই।"
"1049","They were making dinner when I got home.","ghar pāoṃte xihaṃte rātir āhār banāi āchil.","ঘৰ পাওঁতে সিহঁতে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ বনাই আছিল।"
"1050","Will she have finished cooking by then?","tetiyālaike tāir randhā-bṛhā xex ha’bne?","তেতিয়ালৈকে তাইৰ ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া শেষ হ’বনে?"
"1051","Do not touch the hot pan.","garam kerāhīt cub nālāge.","গৰম কেৰাহীত চুব নালাগে।"
"1052","The bread is baked fresh every morning.","pratidine puwā xatejkai ruṭi bek karā hay.","প্ৰতিদিনে পুৱা সতেজকৈ ৰুটি বেক কৰা হয়।"
"1053","You used to eat meat.","āgte āpuni māṃx khāichil.","আগতে আপুনি মাংস খাইছিল।"
"1054","We had not eaten all day.","āmi goṭei dinṭo khowā nāchiloṃ.","আমি গোটেই দিনটো খোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1055","He used to work in a bakery.","āgte bekārī ekhnat kām karichil.","আগতে বেকাৰী এখনত কাম কৰিছিল।"
"1056","Are they bringing dessert?","ḍejārṭa lai āhiche neki?","ডেজাৰ্ট লৈ আহিছে নেকি?"
"1057","Pass me the butter, please.","mok mākhan pāch karak, plij.","মোক মাখন পাছ কৰক, প্লিজ।"
"1058","The vegetables had been washed before cooking.","xāk-pāclibor randhā āgte dhui pelowā haichil.","শাক-পাচলিবোৰ ৰন্ধা আগতে ধুই পেলোৱা হৈছিল।"
"1059","How long have the potatoes been boiling?","ālu kimān dinar parā utli āche?","আলু কিমান দিনৰ পৰা উতলি আছে?"
"1060","Let him try a small piece.","teoṃ xaru ṭukurā eṭā cexṭā karak.","তেওঁ সৰু টুকুৰা এটা চেষ্টা কৰক।"
"1061","Whenever we visit, she makes her special cake.","āmi yetiyāi bhramaṇ karo tetiyāi tāi tāir bixex kekṭo banāi diye.","আমি যেতিয়াই ভ্ৰমণ কৰো তেতিয়াই তাই তাইৰ বিশেষ কেকটো বনাই দিয়ে।"
"1062","They used to commute by bicycle.","āgte cāikelere ahā-yowā karichil.","আগতে চাইকেলেৰে অহা-যোৱা কৰিছিল।"
"1063","Will you be taking the early train?","āpuni āgtīyākai re’lat uṭhib neki?","আপুনি আগতীয়াকৈ ৰে’লত উঠিব নেকি?"
"1064","I had not been to Europe before that trip.","xei bhramṇar āgte mai iuroplai yowā nāchiloṃ.","সেই ভ্ৰমণৰ আগতে মই ইউৰোপলৈ যোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1065","Let them board first.","prathme xihaṃtak barḍat uṭhiblai diyak.","প্ৰথমে সিহঁতক বৰ্ডত উঠিবলৈ দিয়ক।"
"1066","The gate is closed at midnight.","mājnixā geṭ bandha hai thāke.","মাজনিশা গেট বন্ধ হৈ থাকে।"
"1067","We were not told about the cancellation.","āmāk bātilar kathā kowā howā nāchil.","আমাক বাতিলৰ কথা কোৱা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1068","He used to drive a truck for a living.","jīwikār bābe ṭrāk calāichil.","জীৱিকাৰ বাবে ট্ৰাক চলাইছিল।"
"1069","Are you enjoying the flight?","bimānkhan upbhog kari āchene?","বিমানখন উপভোগ কৰি আছেনে?"
"1070","Whenever I fly, I get anxious.","yetiyāi urājāhāj calāoṃ, tetiyāi mai udbigna hai paro.","যেতিয়াই উৰাজাহাজ চলাওঁ, তেতিয়াই মই উদ্বিগ্ন হৈ পৰো।"
"1071","How many suitcases may I check?","kimān chuṭkech cek karib pārim?","কিমান ছুটকেছ চেক কৰিব পাৰিম?"
"1072","The tickets have already been purchased.","itimadhye ṭikaṭ kray karā haiche.","ইতিমধ্যে টিকট ক্ৰয় কৰা হৈছে।"
"1073","Do not leave your bags unattended.","begbor anādr̥tbhāwe nārākhib.","বেগবোৰ অনাদৃতভাৱে নাৰাখিব।"
"1074","They used to live next door.","āgte xihaṃtar kāxte thākichil.","আগতে সিহঁতৰ কাষতে থাকিছিল।"
"1075","Will you be visiting your parents this weekend?","ei uikeṇḍat āpuni āponār pitr̥-mātr̥r ocarlai yābne?","এই উইকেণ্ডত আপুনি আপোনাৰ পিতৃ-মাতৃৰ ওচৰলৈ যাবনে?"
"1076","I had not met her husband before the wedding.","biyār āgte tāir xbāmīk lag powā nāchiloṃ.","বিয়াৰ আগতে তাইৰ স্বামীক লগ পোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1077","Let the baby sleep.","keṃcuwāṭok xublai diyak.","কেঁচুৱাটোক শুবলৈ দিয়ক।"
"1078","The lawn is mowed once a week.","xaptāhat ebār lan kāṭi lowā hay.","সপ্তাহত এবাৰ লন কাটি লোৱা হয়।"
"1079","We were not expecting guests.","āmi atithir āxā karā nāchiloṃ.","আমি অতিথিৰ আশা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1080","She used to play with dolls.","tāi putlār lagat khelichil.","তাই পুতলাৰ লগত খেলিছিল।"
"1081","Are your siblings coming for dinner?","āponār bhāi-bhnīxkal rātir āhār khāblai āhiche neki?","আপোনাৰ ভাই-ভনীসকল ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খাবলৈ আহিছে নেকি?"
"1082","Whenever grandma visits, she brings cookies.","āitāi yetiyāi bhramaṇ kare, tāi kukij lai āhe.","আইতাই যেতিয়াই ভ্ৰমণ কৰে, তাই কুকিজ লৈ আহে।"
"1083","How many rooms does the house have?","gharṭot kimān koṭhā āche?","ঘৰটোত কিমান কোঠা আছে?"
"1084","Make yourself at home.","nijke gharar dare kari laok.","নিজকে ঘৰৰ দৰে কৰি লওক।"
"1085","The children had already been picked up.","itimadhye la’rā-chowālīkeiṭāk tuli lowā haichil.","ইতিমধ্যে ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীকেইটাক তুলি লোৱা হৈছিল।"
"1086","Do not slam the door.","duwārkhan ṭheli nidib.","দুৱাৰখন ঠেলি নিদিব।"
"1087","She used to teach mathematics.","āgte tāi gaṇit paṛhāichil.","আগতে তাই গণিত পঢ়াইছিল।"
"1088","Will you have submitted the report by Friday?","xukurbārar bhitrat pratibedan jamā dibne?","শুকুৰবাৰৰ ভিতৰত প্ৰতিবেদন জমা দিবনে?"
"1089","I had not heard about the promotion.","pramocnar kathā xunā nāchiloṃ.","প্ৰমোচনৰ কথা শুনা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1090","Let her explain her idea.","tāik tāir dhārṇāṭo bujāi diyak.","তাইক তাইৰ ধাৰণাটো বুজাই দিয়ক।"
"1091","Homework is assigned every day.","pratidine homwarka diyā hay.","প্ৰতিদিনে হোমৱৰ্ক দিয়া হয়।"
"1092","We were not given enough time.","āmāk paryāpta xamay diyā howā nāchil.","আমাক পৰ্যাপ্ত সময় দিয়া হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1093","He used to work the night shift.","āgte teoṃ nāiṭ xbiphṭat kām karichil.","আগতে তেওঁ নাইট শ্বিফটত কাম কৰিছিল।"
"1094","Are the students paying attention?","chātra-chātrīxakle manoyog dichene?","ছাত্ৰ-ছাত্ৰীসকলে মনোযোগ দিছেনে?"
"1095","Whenever I make a mistake, I learn from it.","yetiyāi bhul karo tetiyāi tār parā xiki laoṃ.","যেতিয়াই ভুল কৰো তেতিয়াই তাৰ পৰা শিকি লওঁ।"
"1096","How many employees does the company have?","kompānīṭor kimānjan karmacārī āche?","কোম্পানীটোৰ কিমানজন কৰ্মচাৰী আছে?"
"1097","Submit your assignment on time.","xamyamte nijar ecāinmeṇṭa jamā diyak.","সময়মতে নিজৰ এচাইনমেণ্ট জমা দিয়ক।"
"1098","All applications must be received by the deadline.","xamayxīmār bhitrat xaklo āwedan lābh karib lāgib.","সময়সীমাৰ ভিতৰত সকলো আৱেদন লাভ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1099","Do not plagiarize.","lekhā-cor nakrib.","লেখা-চোৰ নকৰিব।"
"1100","They used to swim in the river.","āgte nadīt xāṃturichil.","আগতে নদীত সাঁতুৰিছিল।"
"1101","Will it be raining tomorrow?","kāilai barxuṇ dib neki?","কাইলৈ বৰষুণ দিব নেকি?"
"1102","I had never seen such a beautiful sunset.","imān dhunīyā xūryāxta mai ketiyāo dekhā nāchiloṃ.","ইমান ধুনীয়া সূৰ্যাস্ত মই কেতিয়াও দেখা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1103","Let the flowers bloom.","phulbor phuliblai diyak.","ফুলবোৰ ফুলিবলৈ দিয়ক।"
"1104","The park is maintained by volunteers.","xbecchāxewakxakle udyānakhnar rakxaṇābekxaṇ kare.","স্বেচ্ছাসেৱকসকলে উদ্যানখনৰ ৰক্ষণাবেক্ষণ কৰে।"
"1105","We were not warned about the storm.","āmāk dhumuhār bixye xakīyāi diyā howā nāchil.","আমাক ধুমুহাৰ বিষয়ে সকীয়াই দিয়া হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1106","She used to feed the birds every morning.","tāi pratidine puwā carāiborak khuwāichil.","তাই প্ৰতিদিনে পুৱা চৰাইবোৰক খুৱাইছিল।"
"1107","Are the leaves falling already?","itimadhye pātbor xariche neki?","ইতিমধ্যে পাতবোৰ সৰিছে নেকি?"
"1108","Whenever the wind blows, the shutters rattle.","yetiyāi batāh bali thāke tetiyāi xbāṭārbor hulaxthul kare.","যেতিয়াই বতাহ বলি থাকে তেতিয়াই শ্বাটাৰবোৰ হুলস্থুল কৰে।"
"1109","How tall is that tree?","xei gachjopā kimān okh?","সেই গছজোপা কিমান ওখ?"
"1110","Do not pick the wild flowers.","banrīyā phul chiṅib nālāge.","বনৰীয়া ফুল ছিঙিব নালাগে।"
"1111","Several species have been found in this area.","ei añcalat keibāṭāo prajāti powā gaiche.","এই অঞ্চলত কেইবাটাও প্ৰজাতি পোৱা গৈছে।"
"1112","Water the plants twice a day.","gachborak dine dubārkai pānī dib lāge.","গছবোৰক দিনে দুবাৰকৈ পানী দিব লাগে।"
"1113","He used to smoke a pack a day.","dinṭot eṭā pek dhūmpān karichil.","দিনটোত এটা পেক ধূমপান কৰিছিল।"
"1114","Will the test results be ready tomorrow?","kāilai parīkxār phalāphal xāju ha’bne?","কাইলৈ পৰীক্ষাৰ ফলাফল সাজু হ’বনে?"
"1115","I had never broken a bone before.","āgte ketiyāo hāṛ bhāṅi yowā nāchiloṃ.","আগতে কেতিয়াও হাড় ভাঙি যোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1116","Let him rest.","teoṃk jirṇi laok.","তেওঁক জিৰণি লওক।"
"1117","Patients are seen on a first-come basis.","rogīk pratham ahār bhittit dekhā yāy.","ৰোগীক প্ৰথম অহাৰ ভিত্তিত দেখা যায়।"
"1118","We were not informed of the side effects.","pārxbakriyār bixye āmāk janowā howā nāchil.","পাৰ্শ্বক্ৰিয়াৰ বিষয়ে আমাক জনোৱা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1119","She used to run five miles every day.","tāi pratidine pāṃc māil daurichil.","তাই প্ৰতিদিনে পাঁচ মাইল দৌৰিছিল।"
"1120","Are you feeling any better?","āponār kibā bhāl lāgiche neki?","আপোনাৰ কিবা ভাল লাগিছে নেকি?"
"1121","Whenever I eat dairy, my stomach hurts.","yetiyāi ḍeirī khāoṃ tetiyāi peṭat bix hay.","যেতিয়াই ডেইৰী খাওঁ তেতিয়াই পেটত বিষ হয়।"
"1122","How much water should you drink per day?","pratidine kimān pānī khāb lāge?","প্ৰতিদিনে কিমান পানী খাব লাগে?"
"1123","Take this pill twice daily.","ei baṛi dainik dubārkai khāb lāge.","এই বড়ি দৈনিক দুবাৰকৈ খাব লাগে।"
"1124","All patients have been vaccinated.","xaklo rogīke ṭikākraṇ karā haiche.","সকলো ৰোগীকে টিকাকৰণ কৰা হৈছে।"
"1125","Do not scratch the wound.","ghāṃt khoṃc mārib nālāge.","ঘাঁত খোঁচ মাৰিব নালাগে।"
"1126","She used to work at the mall.","āgte tāi malat kām karichil.","আগতে তাই মলত কাম কৰিছিল।"
"1127","Will the price have dropped by summer?","grīxmakālar bhitrat dām kami yābne?","গ্ৰীষ্মকালৰ ভিতৰত দাম কমি যাবনে?"
"1128","I had never spent so much on one item.","eṭā baxtur bābe imān kharac ketiyāo karā nāchiloṃ.","এটা বস্তুৰ বাবে ইমান খৰচ কেতিয়াও কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1129","Let me check the receipt.","racidkhan cek kari laoṃ.","ৰচিদখন চেক কৰি লওঁ।"
"1130","The prices are updated weekly.","dāmaxmūh xāptāhikbhāwe āpḍeṭ karā hay.","দামসমূহ সাপ্তাহিকভাৱে আপডেট কৰা হয়।"
"1131","We were not refunded.","āmāk dhan ghūrāi diyā howā nāchil.","আমাক ধন ঘূৰাই দিয়া হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1132","He used to collect stamps.","āgte teoṃ ḍākṭikaṭ xaṃgrah karichil.","আগতে তেওঁ ডাকটিকট সংগ্ৰহ কৰিছিল।"
"1133","Are they having a sale?","teoṃlokar bikrī haiche neki?","তেওঁলোকৰ বিক্ৰী হৈছে নেকি?"
"1134","Whenever there is a sale, the store gets crowded.","yetiyāi bikrī hay xṭa’rat bhir hay.","যেতিয়াই বিক্ৰী হয় ষ্ট’ৰত ভিৰ হয়।"
"1135","Keep the receipt.","racidkhan rākhak.","ৰচিদখন ৰাখক।"
"1136","All items must be returned within 30 days.","30 dinar bhitrat xaklo xāmagrī ghūrāi dib lāgib.","৩০ দিনৰ ভিতৰত সকলো সামগ্ৰী ঘূৰাই দিব লাগিব।"
"1137","Do not open the package before paying.","dhan diyār āgte pekejṭo khulib nālāge.","ধন দিয়াৰ আগতে পেকেজটো খুলিব নালাগে।"
"1138","He used to be very shy.","āgte teoṃ bar lāj lagā āchil.","আগতে তেওঁ বৰ লাজ লগা আছিল।"
"1139","Will you ever forgive me?","mok ketiyābā kxamā karibne?","মোক কেতিয়াবা ক্ষমা কৰিবনে?"
"1140","I had not realized how much it hurt.","kimān kaxṭa pāichil mai gam powā nāchiloṃ.","কিমান কষ্ট পাইছিল মই গম পোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1141","Let them sort it out themselves.","teoṃloke nijei xamādhān karak.","তেওঁলোকে নিজেই সমাধান কৰক।"
"1142","Compliments are appreciated.","praxaṃxār xalāg lowā hay.","প্ৰশংসাৰ শলাগ লোৱা হয়।"
"1143","We were not told the truth.","āmāk xaṃcā kathā kowā howā nāchil.","আমাক সঁচা কথা কোৱা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1144","She used to cry a lot as a child.","xarute tāi bahut kāndichil.","সৰুতে তাই বহুত কান্দিছিল।"
"1145","Are you still angry?","etiyāo khaṃ uṭhiche neki?","এতিয়াও খং উঠিছে নেকি?"
"1146","Whenever he smiles, I feel better.","yetiyāi xi hāṃhi māre, mor manṭo bhāl lāge.","যেতিয়াই সি হাঁহি মাৰে, মোৰ মনটো ভাল লাগে।"
"1147","How deeply does this affect you?","iyār prabhāw āponār oprat kimān gabhīr?","ইয়াৰ প্ৰভাৱ আপোনাৰ ওপৰত কিমান গভীৰ?"
"1148","Be kind to each other.","ijne xijnar prati dayālu haok.","ইজনে সিজনৰ প্ৰতি দয়ালু হওক।"
"1149","No feelings were hurt.","kono anubhwat āghāt powā nāchil.","কোনো অনুভৱত আঘাত পোৱা নাছিল।"
"1150","Do not blame yourself.","nijke dox nidib.","নিজকে দোষ নিদিব।"
"1151","I wish they would stop arguing.","teoṃloke tarka bandha kari dile bhāl āchil.","তেওঁলোকে তৰ্ক বন্ধ কৰি দিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1152","He used to paint landscapes.","teoṃ āgte leṇḍaxkep āṃkichil.","তেওঁ আগতে লেণ্ডস্কেপ আঁকিছিল।"
"1153","Will you be attending the concert?","kancārṭat aṃxagrahaṇ karibne?","কনচাৰ্টত অংশগ্ৰহণ কৰিবনে?"
"1154","I had not expected such a thrilling ending.","imān romāñcakar anta parib buli mai āxā karā nāchiloṃ.","ইমান ৰোমাঞ্চকৰ অন্ত পৰিব বুলি মই আশা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1155","Let us take a break.","birti laoṃ āhak.","বিৰতি লওঁ আহক।"
"1156","The game is played every Saturday.","prati xanibāre khel anuxṭhit hay.","প্ৰতি শনিবাৰে খেল অনুষ্ঠিত হয়।"
"1157","We were not invited to the party.","āmāk pārṭilai nimantraṇ karā howā nāchil.","আমাক পাৰ্টিলৈ নিমন্ত্ৰণ কৰা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1158","She used to sing in the choir.","tāi āgte kowārat gān gāichil.","তাই আগতে কোৱাৰত গান গাইছিল।"
"1159","Are the tickets still available?","ṭikaṭ etiyāo āche neki?","টিকট এতিয়াও আছে নেকি?"
"1160","Whenever I read, I lose track of time.","yetiyāi paṛho tetiyāi xamyar khabar heruwāi pelāoṃ.","যেতিয়াই পঢ়ো তেতিয়াই সময়ৰ খবৰ হেৰুৱাই পেলাওঁ।"
"1161","How long does the concert last?","kancārṭaṭo kimān dinlai calib?","কনচাৰ্টটো কিমান দিনলৈ চলিব?"
"1162","Enjoy the performance.","abhinay upbhog karak.","অভিনয় উপভোগ কৰক।"
"1163","All seats have been reserved.","xaklo āxan xaṃrakxaṇ karā haiche.","সকলো আসন সংৰক্ষণ কৰা হৈছে।"
"1164","Do not use your phone during the show.","xba’r xamyat āponār phon byawhār nakrib.","শ্ব’ৰ সময়ত আপোনাৰ ফোন ব্যৱহাৰ নকৰিব।"
"1165","He used to write poetry.","āgte teoṃ kabitā likhichil.","আগতে তেওঁ কবিতা লিখিছিল।"
"1166","Will you have read the book by our next meeting?","āmār parwartī xabhār bhitrat āpuni kitāpkhan paṛhibne?","আমাৰ পৰৱৰ্তী সভাৰ ভিতৰত আপুনি কিতাপখন পঢ়িবনে?"
"1167","I had not spoken to her in ages.","yug yug dhari tāir lagat kathā patā nāchiloṃ.","যুগ যুগ ধৰি তাইৰ লগত কথা পতা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1168","Let me finish my sentence.","mor bākyaṭo xex kari dioṃ.","মোৰ বাক্যটো শেষ কৰি দিওঁ।"
"1169","Messages are answered within 24 hours.","24 ghaṇṭār bhitrat bārtār uttar diyā hay.","২৪ ঘণ্টাৰ ভিতৰত বাৰ্তাৰ উত্তৰ দিয়া হয়।"
"1170","We were not consulted before the decision.","xiddhānta lowār āgte āmār lagat parāmarxa karā howā nāchil.","সিদ্ধান্ত লোৱাৰ আগতে আমাৰ লগত পৰামৰ্শ কৰা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1171","She used to read aloud to us.","tāi āmāk jorere paṛhichil.","তাই আমাক জোৰেৰে পঢ়িছিল।"
"1172","Are you listening to me?","mor kathā xuni āchā neki?","মোৰ কথা শুনি আছা নেকি?"
"1173","Whenever we disagree, we try to compromise.","yetiyāi āmār mājat matānaikya hay tetiyāi āmi āpoc kariblai cexṭā karoṃ.","যেতিয়াই আমাৰ মাজত মতানৈক্য হয় তেতিয়াই আমি আপোচ কৰিবলৈ চেষ্টা কৰোঁ।"
"1174","How well do you know her?","tāik kimān bhāladre cini pāy?","তাইক কিমান ভালদৰে চিনি পায়?"

"1176","All complaints will be reviewed.","xaklo abhiyog punrīkxaṇ karā ha’b.","সকলো অভিযোগ পুনৰীক্ষণ কৰা হ’ব।"
"1177","Do not raise your voice.","māt uṭhāb nālāge.","মাত উঠাব নালাগে।"
"1178","There are three bathrooms in this house.","ei gharṭot tiniṭā bāthrum āche.","এই ঘৰটোত তিনিটা বাথৰুম আছে।"
"1179","It takes me thirty minutes to get ready.","xāju ha’blai mor trix miniṭ xamay lāge.","সাজু হ’বলৈ মোৰ ত্ৰিশ মিনিট সময় লাগে।"
"1180","I help her and she helps me.","mai tāik xahāy karo āru tāi mok xahāy kare.","মই তাইক সহায় কৰো আৰু তাই মোক সহায় কৰে।"
"1181","This towel is softer than that one.","ei ṭāwelkhan xei ṭāwelatkai komal.","এই টাৱেলখন সেই টাৱেলতকৈ কোমল।"
"1182","The earliest I can wake up is six.","āṭāitkai xonkāle xār pāb parā xamay chay.","আটাইতকৈ সোনকালে সাৰ পাব পৰা সময় ছয়।"
"1183","I wonder whether he is awake.","bhābichoṃ teoṃ xār pāi āche neki?","ভাবিছোঁ তেওঁ সাৰ পাই আছে নেকি?"
"1184","Someone must have left the water running.","konobāi nixcay pānī calāi thai gaiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় পানী চলাই থৈ গৈছে।"
"1185","There is no milk left.","gākhīr bākī nāi.","গাখীৰ বাকী নাই।"
"1186","It takes about an hour to bake a cake.","kek eṭā bek kariblai prāy eghaṇṭā xamay lāge.","কেক এটা বেক কৰিবলৈ প্ৰায় এঘণ্টা সময় লাগে।"
"1187","She teaches him how to cook.","tāi tāk randhā-bṛhā xikāi diye.","তাই তাক ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া শিকাই দিয়ে।"
"1188","This dish is the spiciest on the menu.","ei khādya menut āṭāitkai ṭeṅā.","এই খাদ্য মেনুত আটাইতকৈ টেঙা।"
"1189","Fry the onions until they are golden.","piṃyājkhini xoṇālī howālaike bhājib.","পিঁয়াজখিনি সোণালী হোৱালৈকে ভাজিব।"
"1190","I wonder if there is any coffee left.","bhābichoṃ kaphi bākī āche neki?","ভাবিছোঁ কফি বাকী আছে নেকি?"
"1191","He made her taste the soup.","xi tāik cūpar xowād la’blai dile.","সি তাইক চূপৰ সোৱাদ ল’বলৈ দিলে।"
"1192","There were no seats available.","āxnar byawaxthā nāchil.","আসনৰ ব্যৱস্থা নাছিল।"

"1194","She let him drive.","tāi tāk gāṛī calāblai dile.","তাই তাক গাড়ী চলাবলৈ দিলে।"
"1195","This road is the narrowest in the region.","ei pathṭo añcalṭor āṭāitkai xaṃkīrṇa.","এই পথটো অঞ্চলটোৰ আটাইতকৈ সংকীৰ্ণ।"
"1196","Stay on the path until you reach the river.","nadī powālaike bāṭte thākak.","নদী পোৱালৈকে বাটতে থাকক।"
"1197","I wonder if the flight is delayed.","bimānkhan palam haiche neki buli bhābo.","বিমানখন পলম হৈছে নেকি বুলি ভাবো।"
"1198","They had each other carry the luggage.","ijne xijnak lāgej kaṛhiyāi lai phurichil.","ইজনে সিজনক লাগেজ কঢ়িয়াই লৈ ফুৰিছিল।"
"1199","There used to be a garden here.","āgte iyāt ekhan bārī āchil.","আগতে ইয়াত এখন বাৰী আছিল।"
"1200","It took them a whole day to move.","xihaṃtak larcar kariblai goṭei dinṭo lāgil.","সিহঁতক লৰচৰ কৰিবলৈ গোটেই দিনটো লাগিল।"
"1201","She made the children clean their rooms.","tāi la’rā-chowālīkeiṭāk koṭhābor cāphā kari dile.","তাই ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীকেইটাক কোঠাবোৰ চাফা কৰি দিলে।"
"1202","My grandmother is the oldest person in our town.","mor āitā āmār cahrar āṭāitkai bayxīyāl mānuh.","মোৰ আইতা আমাৰ চহৰৰ আটাইতকৈ বয়সীয়াল মানুহ।"
"1203","Close the window before you leave.","yowār āgte khirikīkhan bandha karak.","যোৱাৰ আগতে খিৰিকীখন বন্ধ কৰক।"
"1204","I wonder what time my parents will arrive.","bhābichoṃ mā-deutā kimān bajāt āhib.","ভাবিছোঁ মা-দেউতা কিমান বজাত আহিব।"
"1205","There are five people in my family.","mor pariyālat pāṃcjan mānuh āche.","মোৰ পৰিয়ালত পাঁচজন মানুহ আছে।"

"1208","She had him rewrite the entire essay.","tāi tāk goṭei racnākhan punar likhiblai dichil.","তাই তাক গোটেই ৰচনাখন পুনৰ লিখিবলৈ দিছিল।"
"1209","This is the most important exam of the year.","eyā bachrar āṭāitkai gurutbapūrṇa parīkxā.","এয়া বছৰৰ আটাইতকৈ গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ পৰীক্ষা।"
"1210","Finish this before the bell rings.","ghaṇṭā bājibar āgtei eiṭo xex karak.","ঘণ্টা বাজিবৰ আগতেই এইটো শেষ কৰক।"
"1211","I wonder whether the results have been posted.","rijālṭa poxṭa karā haiche neki buli bhābo.","ৰিজাল্ট পোষ্ট কৰা হৈছে নেকি বুলি ভাবো।"
"1212","There have been several complaints.","keibāṭāo abhiyog utthāpan haiche.","কেইবাটাও অভিযোগ উত্থাপন হৈছে।"
"1213","There are mountains in the distance.","dūrait pāhār.","দূৰৈত পাহাৰ।"
"1214","It takes sunlight eight minutes to reach Earth.","xūryar pohar pr̥thiwīlai yāblai āṭh miniṭ xamay lāge.","সূৰ্যৰ পোহৰ পৃথিৱীলৈ যাবলৈ আঠ মিনিট সময় লাগে।"
"1215","The heat made the ice melt.","garmat baraph gali gali ga’l.","গৰমত বৰফ গলি গলি গ’ল।"
"1216","This is the tallest mountain in the country.","ei pāhārkhan dexar āṭāitkai okh pāhār.","এই পাহাৰখন দেশৰ আটাইতকৈ ওখ পাহাৰ।"
"1217","Watch where you step.","ka’t bhari diye cāok.","ক’ত ভৰি দিয়ে চাওক।"
"1218","I wonder if it will rain tonight.","bhābichoṃ āji rāti barxuṇ dib neki?","ভাবিছোঁ আজি ৰাতি বৰষুণ দিব নেকি?"
"1219","There had been a drought for months.","keibāmāho dhari kharāṃ parixthitir xr̥xṭi haichil.","কেইবামাহো ধৰি খৰাং পৰিস্থিতিৰ সৃষ্টি হৈছিল।"
"1220","There is a pharmacy on the corner.","cukat phārmācī ekhan.","চুকত ফাৰ্মাচী এখন।"
"1221","It takes six weeks for a bone to heal.","eṭā hāṛ bhāl ha’blai chay xaptāh xamay lāge.","এটা হাড় ভাল হ’বলৈ ছয় সপ্তাহ সময় লাগে।"
"1222","The noise made my headache worse.","kolāhle mor mūrar bix āru bechi kari tulile.","কোলাহলে মোৰ মূৰৰ বিষ আৰু বেছি কৰি তুলিলে।"
"1223","This is the best hospital in the city.","eikhan mahānagrīr xrexṭha cikitxālay.","এইখন মহানগৰীৰ শ্ৰেষ্ঠ চিকিৎসালয়।"
"1224","Breathe deeply and count to ten.","gabhīrbhāwe uxāh laok āru dahlaike gaṇnā karak.","গভীৰভাৱে উশাহ লওক আৰু দহলৈকে গণনা কৰক।"

"1227","There is a discount on bulk orders.","bālka arḍārat rehāi diyā haiche.","বাল্ক অৰ্ডাৰত ৰেহাই দিয়া হৈছে।"

"1229","The advertisement made me want to buy it.","bijñāpanṭowe mok kiniblai man ga’l.","বিজ্ঞাপনটোৱে মোক কিনিবলৈ মন গ’ল।"

"1233","There were long lines at every checkout.","pratiṭo cekaāuṭte dīghlīyā xārī āchil.","প্ৰতিটো চেকআউটতে দীঘলীয়া শাৰী আছিল।"

"1237","This is the saddest story I have ever heard.","eiṭo mor xunā āṭāitkai dukhar kāhinī.","এইটো মোৰ শুনা আটাইতকৈ দুখৰ কাহিনী।"

"1240","There had been tension between them for weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari duyore mājat uttejnā cali āchil.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি দুয়োৰে মাজত উত্তেজনা চলি আছিল।"
"1241","There is a concert at the park tonight.","āji rāti pārkat kancārṭa āche.","আজি ৰাতি পাৰ্কত কনচাৰ্ট আছে।"
"1242","It took them three years to finish the film.","chabikhan xex kariblai teoṃlokar tini bachar xamay lāgichil.","ছবিখন শেষ কৰিবলৈ তেওঁলোকৰ তিনি বছৰ সময় লাগিছিল।"
"1243","The movie made us laugh.","cinemākhne āmāk hāṃhiblai bādhya karāle.","চিনেমাখনে আমাক হাঁহিবলৈ বাধ্য কৰালে।"
"1244","This is the funniest show I have ever seen.","eiṭo mor dekhā āṭāitkai dhemelīyā xba’.","এইটো মোৰ দেখা আটাইতকৈ ধেমেলীয়া শ্ব’।"
"1245","Try this new game.","ei natun khelkhan cexṭā karak.","এই নতুন খেলখন চেষ্টা কৰক।"
"1246","I wonder what time the show starts.","mai bhābo kimān bajāt xba’ṭo ārambha hay.","মই ভাবো কিমান বজাত শ্ব’টো আৰম্ভ হয়।"
"1247","There have been three sequels so far.","etiyālaike tiniṭā chikuwel haiche.","এতিয়ালৈকে তিনিটা ছিকুৱেল হৈছে।"
"1248","There is a misunderstanding between us.","āmār mājat bhul bujābuji.","আমাৰ মাজত ভুল বুজাবুজি।"
"1249","It took her courage to speak up.","māt mātiblai tāir xāhxar prayojan haichil.","মাত মাতিবলৈ তাইৰ সাহসৰ প্ৰয়োজন হৈছিল।"
"1250","His tone made everyone uncomfortable.","teoṃr xurṭowe xakloke axbaxtit pelāichil.","তেওঁৰ সুৰটোৱে সকলোকে অস্বস্তিত পেলাইছিল।"
"1251","This is the most important conversation we have had.","eiṭowei āmār mājat howā āṭāitkai gurutbapūrṇa kathā-batrā.","এইটোৱেই আমাৰ মাজত হোৱা আটাইতকৈ গুৰুত্বপূৰ্ণ কথা-বতৰা।"
"1252","Repeat after me.","mor pichat punrābr̥tti karak.","মোৰ পিছত পুনৰাবৃত্তি কৰক।"
"1253","I wonder why they have not replied.","mai bhābo teoṃloke kiy uttar diyā nāi.","মই ভাবো তেওঁলোকে কিয় উত্তৰ দিয়া নাই।"
"1254","There used to be more communication between neighbors.","āgte ocar-cuburīyār mājat yogāyog bechi haichil.","আগতে ওচৰ-চুবুৰীয়াৰ মাজত যোগাযোগ বেছি হৈছিল।"
"1255","You forgot to turn off the stove.","caukā bandha kariblai pāhri ga’l.","চৌকা বন্ধ কৰিবলৈ পাহৰি গ’ল।"
"1256","You were reading when the power went out.","āpuni paṛhi āchil yetiyā bidyut bandha hai ga’l.","আপুনি পঢ়ি আছিল যেতিয়া বিদ্যুৎ বন্ধ হৈ গ’ল।"
"1257","You had already left when I arrived.","mai āhi powār xamyat tumi itimadhye guci gaichilā.","মই আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত তুমি ইতিমধ্যে গুচি গৈছিলা।"
"1258","You burned the toast again.","āpuni ākau ṭoxṭaṭo jbalāi dile.","আপুনি আকৌ টোষ্টটো জ্বলাই দিলে।"
"1259","You were chopping vegetables when I called.","mai phon karār xamyat tumi xāk-pācli kāṭi āchilā.","মই ফোন কৰাৰ সময়ত তুমি শাক-পাচলি কাটি আছিলা।"
"1260","You missed the last bus.","xexar bāchkhan mich karile.","শেষৰ বাছখন মিছ কৰিলে।"
"1261","You were packing when I arrived.","mai āhi powār xamyat tumi pekiṃ kari āchilā.","মই আহি পোৱাৰ সময়ত তুমি পেকিং কৰি আছিলা।"
"1262","You were born in a small town.","tomār janma ekhan xaru cahrat.","তোমাৰ জন্ম এখন সৰু চহৰত।"
"1263","You grew up near the ocean.","tumi xāgrar ocarte ḍāṅar haichā.","তুমি সাগৰৰ ওচৰতে ডাঙৰ হৈছা।"
"1264","You forgot your homework at home.","gharat homwarka pāhri ga’l.","ঘৰত হোমৱৰ্ক পাহৰি গ’ল।"
"1265","You were not paying attention.","āpuni gurutba nidichil.","আপুনি গুৰুত্ব নিদিছিল।"
"1266","You saw a shooting star last night.","yowā nixā xbuṭiṃ xṭār ejan dekhichilā.","যোৱা নিশা শ্বুটিং ষ্টাৰ এজন দেখিছিলা।"
"1267","You were hiking when the storm began.","dhumuhā ārambha howār xamyat āpuni hāikiṃ kari āchil.","ধুমুহা আৰম্ভ হোৱাৰ সময়ত আপুনি হাইকিং কৰি আছিল।"
"1268","You fainted during the ceremony.","anuxṭhānar xamyat āpuni ajñān hai paril.","অনুষ্ঠানৰ সময়ত আপুনি অজ্ঞান হৈ পৰিল।"
"1269","You were not feeling well yesterday.","kāli tomār manṭo bhāl nāchil.","কালি তোমাৰ মনটো ভাল নাছিল।"
"1270","You lost your receipt.","āpuni āponār racid heruwāi pelāle.","আপুনি আপোনাৰ ৰচিদ হেৰুৱাই পেলালে।"
"1271","You were browsing when the store closed.","xṭa’r bandha howār xamyat āpuni brāuj kari āchil.","ষ্ট’ৰ বন্ধ হোৱাৰ সময়ত আপুনি ব্ৰাউজ কৰি আছিল।"
"1272","You cried at the ending.","xexat āpuni kāndichil.","শেষত আপুনি কান্দিছিল।"
"1273","You were not listening to me.","tumi mor kathā xunā nāchilā.","তুমি মোৰ কথা শুনা নাছিলা।"
"1274","You won the tournament.","āpuni ṭurṇāmeṇṭat jayī haichil.","আপুনি টুৰ্ণামেণ্টত জয়ী হৈছিল।"
"1275","You were singing along to the music.","āpuni xaṃgītar lagat gān gāi āchil.","আপুনি সংগীতৰ লগত গান গাই আছিল।"
"1276","You interrupted me.","tumi mor kathāt bādhā dilā.","তুমি মোৰ কথাত বাধা দিলা।"
"1277","You were whispering during the speech.","bhāxṇar xamyat āpuni phucphucāi āchil.","ভাষণৰ সময়ত আপুনি ফুচফুচাই আছিল।"
"1278","I will be sleeping by midnight.","mājnixālaike xui thākim.","মাজনিশালৈকে শুই থাকিম।"
"1279","They will clean the garage tomorrow.","kāilai gerej cāphā karib.","কাইলৈ গেৰেজ চাফা কৰিব।"
"1280","We will try a new restaurant tonight.","āmi āji rāti natun rexṭureṇṭa ekhan cexṭā karim.","আমি আজি ৰাতি নতুন ৰেষ্টুৰেণ্ট এখন চেষ্টা কৰিম।"
"1281","She will not eat before the show.","xba’r āgte tāi nākhāb.","শ্ব’ৰ আগতে তাই নাখাব।"
"1282","I will be driving all day.","goṭei dinṭo gāṛī calāi thākim.","গোটেই দিনটো গাড়ী চলাই থাকিম।"
"1283","They will not arrive until midnight.","mājnixālaike āhi nāpāb.","মাজনিশালৈকে আহি নাপাব।"
"1284","We will move to a bigger house next year.","āmi ahā bachar ḍāṅar ghar eṭālai guci yām.","আমি অহা বছৰ ডাঙৰ ঘৰ এটালৈ গুচি যাম।"
"1285","She will be graduating in June.","jun māhat xnātak ḍigrī lābh karib.","জুন মাহত স্নাতক ডিগ্ৰী লাভ কৰিব।"
"1286","They will be interviewing candidates all week.","goṭei xaptāhṭo teoṃloke prārthīr xākxātkār la’b.","গোটেই সপ্তাহটো তেওঁলোকে প্ৰাৰ্থীৰ সাক্ষাৎকাৰ ল’ব।"
"1287","It will snow tonight.","āji rāti baraph parib.","আজি ৰাতি বৰফ পৰিব।"
"1288","The river will flood if the rain continues.","barxuṇ abyāhat thākile nadīkhan plāwit ha’b.","বৰষুণ অব্যাহত থাকিলে নদীখন প্লাৱিত হ’ব।"
"1289","I will see the dentist on Monday.","xombāre danta cikitxakar ocarlai yām.","সোমবাৰে দন্ত চিকিৎসকৰ ওচৰলৈ যাম।"
"1290","She will be recovering for several weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari xuxtha hai uṭhib tāi.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি সুস্থ হৈ উঠিব তাই।"
"1291","We will compare prices first.","āmi prathme dām tulnā karim.","আমি প্ৰথমে দাম তুলনা কৰিম।"
"1292","He will not spend more than fifty dollars.","teoṃ pañcāx ṭakātkai bechi kharac nakre.","তেওঁ পঞ্চাশ টকাতকৈ বেছি খৰচ নকৰে।"
"1293","I will always remember this day.","ei dinṭo mor xadāy manat thākib.","এই দিনটো মোৰ সদায় মনত থাকিব।"
"1294","They will be celebrating their anniversary.","teoṃlokar bārxikī udyāpan karib.","তেওঁলোকৰ বাৰ্ষিকী উদযাপন কৰিব।"
"1295","We will watch the sunset together.","āmi ekelge xūryāxta cām.","আমি একেলগে সূৰ্যাস্ত চাম।"
"1296","She will not miss another game.","tāi āru ekhan khel mich nakrib.","তাই আৰু এখন খেল মিছ নকৰিব।"
"1297","I will tell you everything tomorrow.","kāilai xaklo kathā ka'm.","কাইলৈ সকলো কথা ক'ম।"
"1298","They will be discussing the proposal at the meeting.","baiṭhkat teoṃloke ei praxtāwar oprat ālocnā karib.","বৈঠকত তেওঁলোকে এই প্ৰস্তাৱৰ ওপৰত আলোচনা কৰিব।"
"1299","They cleaned the entire house yesterday.","kāli goṭei gharṭo cāphā kari pelāle.","কালি গোটেই ঘৰটো চাফা কৰি পেলালে।"
"1300","The garbage was taken out by my father.","jābarbor deutāi uliyāi ānichil.","জাবৰবোৰ দেউতাই উলিয়াই আনিছিল।"
"1301","We will not go out tonight.","āji rāti āmi olāi nāyāoṃ.","আজি ৰাতি আমি ওলাই নাযাওঁ।"
"1302","Is the coffee being brewed?","kaphi banowā haiche neki?","কফি বনোৱা হৈছে নেকি?"
"1303","All windows must be shut before you leave.","yowār āgte xaklo khirikī bandha thākib lāgib.","যোৱাৰ আগতে সকলো খিৰিকী বন্ধ থাকিব লাগিব।"
"1304","By this time next year, we will have moved.","ahā bachrar ei xamaylaike āmi xthānāntarit ha’m.","অহা বছৰৰ এই সময়লৈকে আমি স্থানান্তৰিত হ’ম।"
"1305","They did not hear the alarm.","elārma xunā nāpāle.","এলাৰ্ম শুনা নাপালে।"
"1306","Has she finished getting dressed?","tāi xāj-pochāk pindhi xex karile neki?","তাই সাজ-পোছাক পিন্ধি শেষ কৰিলে নেকি?"
"1307","We are always late on Mondays.","xombāre āmi xadāy deri karo.","সোমবাৰে আমি সদায় দেৰি কৰো।"
"1308","You should have locked the door.","duwārat talā lagowā ucit āchil.","দুৱাৰত তলা লগোৱা উচিত আছিল।"
"1309","More chores need to be done.","adhik kām-kāj karib lāgib.","অধিক কাম-কাজ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1310","They have been renovating the kitchen.","pākgharṭo xaṃxkār kari āhiche.","পাকঘৰটো সংস্কাৰ কৰি আহিছে।"
"1311","If we had left earlier, we would not be late.","āgtei yowā ha’le deri naha’lheṃten","আগতেই যোৱা হ’লে দেৰি নহ’লহেঁতেন৷"
"1312","They prepared a feast for the guests.","ālhīxaklar bābe bhoj xāju kari dile.","আলহীসকলৰ বাবে ভোজ সাজু কৰি দিলে।"
"1313","The table was set by the hostess.","ṭebulkhan paricārikāgrākīye xthāpan karichil.","টেবুলখন পৰিচাৰিকাগৰাকীয়ে স্থাপন কৰিছিল।"
"1314","We will not order any appetizers.","āmi kono epeṭāijār arḍār nakro.","আমি কোনো এপেটাইজাৰ অৰ্ডাৰ নকৰো।"
"1315","Is the soup being heated?","cupṭo garam karā haiche neki?","চুপটো গৰম কৰা হৈছে নেকি?"
"1316","All ingredients must be measured carefully.","xaklo upādān bhāladre jukhib lāgib.","সকলো উপাদান ভালদৰে জুখিব লাগিব।"
"1317","By dinner time, the pie will have cooled.","rātir āhār grahṇar xamaylaike pāi ṭhāṇḍā hai yāb.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ গ্ৰহণৰ সময়লৈকে পাই ঠাণ্ডা হৈ যাব।"
"1318","They did not enjoy the meal.","teoṃloke āhār grahaṇ kari bhāl nāpāle.","তেওঁলোকে আহাৰ গ্ৰহণ কৰি ভাল নাপালে।"
"1319","Has the waiter taken our order?","oweṭāre āmār arḍār laiche neki?","ওৱেটাৰে আমাৰ অৰ্ডাৰ লৈছে নেকি?"
"1320","We always have breakfast together.","āmi xadāy ekelge brekphāxṭa karo.","আমি সদায় একেলগে ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট কৰো।"
"1321","You could have added more seasoning.","āpuni āru chijniṃ dib pārileheṃten.","আপুনি আৰু ছিজনিং দিব পাৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"1322","Fresh bread needs to be bought.","xatej ruṭi kinār prayojan.","সতেজ ৰুটি কিনাৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"1323","They have been waiting for a table.","ṭebul ekhnar bābe apekxā kari āhiche.","টেবুল এখনৰ বাবে অপেক্ষা কৰি আহিছে।"
"1324","If I had known you were vegan, I would have cooked differently.","yadi mai jāniloheṃten ye tumi bhegān, tente mai beleg dharṇe randhā-bṛhā kariloṃheṃten.","যদি মই জানিলোহেঁতেন যে তুমি ভেগান, তেন্তে মই বেলেগ ধৰণে ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া কৰিলোঁহেঁতেন।"
"1325","They explored the ancient ruins.","teoṃloke prācīn dhbaṃxāwxexbor anbexaṇ karile.","তেওঁলোকে প্ৰাচীন ধ্বংসাৱশেষবোৰ অন্বেষণ কৰিলে।"
"1326","The luggage was lost by the airline.","bimān xaṃxthāṭowe heruwāiche lāgej.","বিমান সংস্থাটোৱে হেৰুৱাইছে লাগেজ।"
"1327","We will not check any bags.","āmi kono beg parīkxā nakro.","আমি কোনো বেগ পৰীক্ষা নকৰো।"
"1328","Is the plane being refueled?","bimānakhnat indhan bharowā haiche neki?","বিমানখনত ইন্ধন ভৰোৱা হৈছে নেকি?"
"1329","All passports must be presented at the counter.","xaklo pāchpa’rṭa kāuṇṭārat dākhil karib lāgib.","সকলো পাছপ’ৰ্ট কাউণ্টাৰত দাখিল কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1330","By next month, I will have visited ten countries.","ahā māhar bhitrat mai dahkhan dex bhramaṇ karim.","অহা মাহৰ ভিতৰত মই দহখন দেশ ভ্ৰমণ কৰিম।"
"1331","They did not find their gate on time.","xamyamte teoṃlokar geṭ bicāri nāpāle.","সময়মতে তেওঁলোকৰ গেট বিচাৰি নাপালে।"
"1332","Has anyone seen my passport?","mor pāchpa’rṭa konobāi dekhichene?","মোৰ পাছপ’ৰ্ট কোনোবাই দেখিছেনে?"
"1333","We always sit in the back of the plane.","āmi xadāy bimānar pichphāle bahi thākoṃ.","আমি সদায় বিমানৰ পিছফালে বহি থাকোঁ।"
"1334","You could have booked a cheaper flight.","xaxtā bimān ekhan bukiṃ karib pārileheṃten.","সস্তা বিমান এখন বুকিং কৰিব পাৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"1335","Reservations need to be confirmed.","rijārbhacan nixcit karib lāgib.","ৰিজাৰ্ভচন নিশ্চিত কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1336","They have been living abroad for five years.","pāṃc bachar dhari bahiḥrājyat bāx kari āhiche.","পাঁচ বছৰ ধৰি বহিঃৰাজ্যত বাস কৰি আহিছে।"
"1337","If she had arrived earlier, she would have caught the train.","āgte āhi powā ha’le tāi ṭreinkhan dharileheṃten.","আগতে আহি পোৱা হ’লে তাই ট্ৰেইনখন ধৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"1338","They adopted a puppy last spring.","yowā baxantat teoṃloke eṭā kukur powāli dattak laichil.","যোৱা বসন্তত তেওঁলোকে এটা কুকুৰ পোৱালি দত্তক লৈছিল।"
"1339","The garden was designed by an architect.","bāgicākhnar ḍijāin karichil ejan xthaptibide.","বাগিচাখনৰ ডিজাইন কৰিছিল এজন স্থপতিবিদে।"
"1340","We will not repaint the walls this year.","āmi ei bachar dewālat punar raṃ nakro.","আমি এই বছৰ দেৱালত পুনৰ ৰং নকৰো।"
"1341","Is the nursery being decorated?","nārcārīkhan xajāiche neki?","নাৰ্চাৰীখন সজাইছে নেকি?"
"1342","All rooms must be tidied before the guests arrive.","ālhī ahār āgte xaklo koṭhā paripāṭi karib lāgib.","আলহী অহাৰ আগতে সকলো কোঠা পৰিপাটি কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1343","By December, they will have lived here for a decade.","ḍicembar māhlaike teoṃloke iyāt eṭā daxak bāx karib.","ডিচেম্বৰ মাহলৈকে তেওঁলোকে ইয়াত এটা দশক বাস কৰিব।"
"1344","They did not recognize the neighborhood.","cuburīṭok cini nāpāle.","চুবুৰীটোক চিনি নাপালে।"
"1345","Has your brother moved yet?","tomār bhāiṭi etiyāo xthānāntarit haiche neki?","তোমাৰ ভাইটি এতিয়াও স্থানান্তৰিত হৈছে নেকি?"

"1347","You could have told me sooner.","āpuni mok xonkāle ka’b pārileheṃten.","আপুনি মোক সোনকালে ক’ব পাৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"1348","They have been arguing all morning.","goṭei rātipuwā duyore mājat tarka-bitarka.","গোটেই ৰাতিপুৱা দুয়োৰে মাজত তৰ্ক-বিতৰ্ক।"
"1349","If they had saved more, they could have bought a house.","adhik xañcay karā ha’le ghar kinib pārileheṃten.","অধিক সঞ্চয় কৰা হ’লে ঘৰ কিনিব পাৰিলেহেঁতেন।"
"1350","They launched the product last quarter.","yowā tinimāhat teoṃloke ei praḍākṭaṭo mukli karichil.","যোৱা তিনিমাহত তেওঁলোকে এই প্ৰডাক্টটো মুকলি কৰিছিল।"
"1351","The report was written by the intern.","riparṭaṭo likhichil inṭārṇajne.","ৰিপৰ্টটো লিখিছিল ইন্টাৰ্ণজনে।"
"1352","We will not accept late submissions.","palamkai jamā diyā āmi grahaṇ nakro.","পলমকৈ জমা দিয়া আমি গ্ৰহণ নকৰো।"
"1353","Is the software being tested?","caphaṭ werṭo parīkxā karā haiche neki?","চফট্ ৱেৰটো পৰীক্ষা কৰা হৈছে নেকি?"

"1355","By Friday, we will have completed the audit.","xukurbārar bhitrat āmi aḍiṭ xampūrṇa karim.","শুকুৰবাৰৰ ভিতৰত আমি অডিট সম্পূৰ্ণ কৰিম।"
"1356","They did not meet the target.","lakxya pūraṇ karib nowārile.","লক্ষ্য পূৰণ কৰিব নোৱাৰিলে।"
"1357","Has the manager approved the budget?","menejāre bājeṭat anumodan diche neki?","মেনেজাৰে বাজেটত অনুমোদন দিছে নেকি?"

"1360","The data needs to be analyzed.","tathya bixlexaṇ karāṭo prayojan.","তথ্য বিশ্লেষণ কৰাটো প্ৰয়োজন।"
"1361","They have been developing the app for months.","keibāmāho dhari teoṃloke ei epṭo ḍebhelap kari āhiche.","কেইবামাহো ধৰি তেওঁলোকে এই এপটো ডেভেলপ কৰি আহিছে।"
"1362","If he had studied abroad, he would speak more fluently.","bahiḥrājyat paṛhā ha’le teoṃ adhik xāwlīlbhāwe kathā ka’leheṃten.","বহিঃৰাজ্যত পঢ়া হ’লে তেওঁ অধিক সাৱলীলভাৱে কথা ক’লেহেঁতেন।"
"1363","They discovered a new species in the cave.","guhāṭot teoṃloke eṭā natun prajātir prāṇī āwixkār kare.","গুহাটোত তেওঁলোকে এটা নতুন প্ৰজাতিৰ প্ৰাণী আৱিষ্কাৰ কৰে।"
"1364","The trail was blocked by a fallen tree.","xari parā gach ejopā ābaddha kari rākhichil pathṭo.","সৰি পৰা গছ এজোপা আবদ্ধ কৰি ৰাখিছিল পথটো।"

"1367","All endangered species must be preserved.","biluptaprāy xaklo prajāti xaṃrakxaṇ karib lāgib.","বিলুপ্তপ্ৰায় সকলো প্ৰজাতি সংৰক্ষণ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1368","By autumn, the leaves will have changed color.","xaratkāllaike pātar raṃ xalni ha’b.","শৰৎকাললৈকে পাতৰ ৰং সলনি হ’ব।"

"1370","Has the pond frozen yet?","pukhurīṭo etiyāo nithar hai gaiche neki?","পুখুৰীটো এতিয়াও নিথৰ হৈ গৈছে নেকি?"

"1374","They have been studying the migration patterns.","teoṃloke prabrajnar dharaṇ adhyayan kari āhiche.","তেওঁলোকে প্ৰব্ৰজনৰ ধৰণ অধ্যয়ন কৰি আহিছে।"
"1375","If the temperature drops further, the pipes will freeze.","uxṇatā āru kami ga’le pāipbor jamā hai parib.","উষ্ণতা আৰু কমি গ’লে পাইপবোৰ জমা হৈ পৰিব।"

"1385","You should have mentioned your allergies.","āpuni āponār elārjīr kathā ullekh karā ucit āchil.","আপুনি আপোনাৰ এলাৰ্জীৰ কথা উল্লেখ কৰা উচিত আছিল।"

"1388","If she had exercised regularly, she would be healthier now.","niymitbhāwe byāyām karā ha’le etiyā tāi xuxtha hai uṭhilheṃten.","নিয়মিতভাৱে ব্যায়াম কৰা হ’লে এতিয়া তাই সুস্থ হৈ উঠিলহেঁতেন।"
