"1426","They have been rehearsing for months.","keibāmāho dhari ākhrā kari āche.","কেইবামাহো ধৰি আখৰা কৰি আছে।"
"1427","If we had bought tickets earlier, we would have had better seats.","āgte ṭikaṭ kinile āmār āxan bhāl ha’lheṃten","আগতে টিকট কিনিলে আমাৰ আসন ভাল হ’লহেঁতেন৷"
"1428","They announced their engagement at the party.","pārṭit teoṃloke nijar eṃgejmeṇṭar kathā ghoxṇā kare.","পাৰ্টিত তেওঁলোকে নিজৰ এংগেজমেণ্টৰ কথা ঘোষণা কৰে।"
"1429","The proposal was rejected by the committee.","ei praxtāw nākac kare kamiṭīye.","এই প্ৰস্তাৱ নাকচ কৰে কমিটীয়ে।"
"1430","We will not gossip about others.","āmi ānar kathā gujaw nakro.","আমি আনৰ কথা গুজৱ নকৰো।"
"1431","Is the petition being circulated?","ābedankhan pracār karā haiche neki?","আবেদনখন প্ৰচাৰ কৰা হৈছে নেকি?"
"1432","All opinions must be heard.","xaklo matāmat xunib lāgib.","সকলো মতামত শুনিব লাগিব।"
"1433","By tomorrow, I will have sent all the invitations.","kāilailaike mai xaklo nimantraṇī patra paṭhāi dim.","কাইলৈলৈকে মই সকলো নিমন্ত্ৰণী পত্ৰ পঠাই দিম।"
"1434","They did not respond to our invitation.","teoṃloke āmār nimantraṇar kono xaṃhāri nidile.","তেওঁলোকে আমাৰ নিমন্ত্ৰণৰ কোনো সঁহাৰি নিদিলে।"
"1435","Has everyone signed the petition?","xaklowe ābednakhnat xbākxar karichene?","সকলোৱে আবেদনখনত স্বাক্ষৰ কৰিছেনে?"
"1436","We always celebrate birthdays together.","āmi xadāy ekelge janmadin pālan karo.","আমি সদায় একেলগে জন্মদিন পালন কৰো।"
"1437","You should have introduced yourself.","āpuni nijar paricay dib lāgichil.","আপুনি নিজৰ পৰিচয় দিব লাগিছিল।"
"1438","Feedback needs to be given constructively.","gaṭhanmūlakbhāwe matāmat diyār prayojan.","গঠনমূলকভাৱে মতামত দিয়াৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"1439","They have been planning the surprise party.","chāraprāij pārṭir parikalpanā kari āhiche.","ছাৰপ্ৰাইজ পাৰ্টিৰ পৰিকল্পনা কৰি আহিছে।"
"1440","If we had communicated better, none of this would have happened.","āmi yadi bhāladre yogāyog kariloṃheṃten tente eiborar konoṭowei naha’lheṃten.","আমি যদি ভালদৰে যোগাযোগ কৰিলোঁহেঁতেন তেন্তে এইবোৰৰ কোনোটোৱেই নহ’লহেঁতেন।"
"1441","I was brushing my teeth when the doorbell rang.","dāṃt brāch kari thākoṃte duwārar ghaṇṭā bāji uṭhil.","দাঁত ব্ৰাছ কৰি থাকোঁতে দুৱাৰৰ ঘণ্টা বাজি উঠিল।"
"1442","They have not done the laundry in weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari kāpor dhowār kām karā nāi.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি কাপোৰ ধোৱাৰ কাম কৰা নাই।"
"1443","She will be getting ready when you arrive.","tumi āhi powār lage lage tāi xāju hai thākib.","তুমি আহি পোৱাৰ লগে লগে তাই সাজু হৈ থাকিব।"
"1444","We had not expected visitors.","āmi darxanārthīr āxā karā nāchiloṃ.","আমি দৰ্শনাৰ্থীৰ আশা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1445","Are the neighbors making noise again?","cuburīyāi ākau hulaxthul kariche neki?","চুবুৰীয়াই আকৌ হুলস্থুল কৰিছে নেকি?"
"1446","The carpet should be vacuumed weekly.","kārpeṭkhan xāptāhikbhāwe bhekuwām kari la’b lāge.","কাৰ্পেটখন সাপ্তাহিকভাৱে ভেকুৱাম কৰি ল’ব লাগে।"
"1447","You always forget your umbrella.","āpuni xadāy āponār chātiṭo pāhri yāy.","আপুনি সদায় আপোনাৰ ছাতিটো পাহৰি যায়।"
"1448","They used to have a dog.","āgte xihaṃtar eṭā kukur āchil.","আগতে সিহঁতৰ এটা কুকুৰ আছিল।"
"1449","I wonder why the mail has not arrived.","bhāboṃ kiy meil āhi powā nāi.","ভাবোঁ কিয় মেইল আহি পোৱা নাই।"
"1450","Most people prefer warm showers.","bechibhāg mānuhei garam xbāwār pachanda kare.","বেছিভাগ মানুহেই গৰম শ্বাৱাৰ পছন্দ কৰে।"
"1451","She herself ironed the shirts.","tāi nijei cārṭabor ixtrī karile.","তাই নিজেই চাৰ্টবোৰ ইস্ত্ৰী কৰিলে।"
"1452","If it is sunny, we will hang the laundry outside.","ra’d ha’le āmi kāpor dhowā baxtubor bāhirat olomāi dim.","ৰ’দ হ’লে আমি কাপোৰ ধোৱা বস্তুবোৰ বাহিৰত ওলোমাই দিম।"
"1453","Have they taken out the recycling?","teoṃloke punaḥbyawhārar kām uliyāichene?","তেওঁলোকে পুনঃব্যৱহাৰৰ কাম উলিয়াইছেনে?"
"1454","The stairs are swept twice a week.","xaptāhat dubārkai ciribor jhāṛu diyā hay.","সপ্তাহত দুবাৰকৈ চিৰিবোৰ ঝাড়ু দিয়া হয়।"
"1455","He could not find his other sock.","xi tār ānṭo mojā bicāri nāpāle.","সি তাৰ আনটো মোজা বিচাৰি নাপালে।"
"1456","Somebody must have moved my keys.","konobāi nixcay mor cābibor larcar karile.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় মোৰ চাবিবোৰ লৰচৰ কৰিলে।"
"1457","I will not oversleep tomorrow.","kāilai mai atirikta ṭopni nāyāoṃ.","কাইলৈ মই অতিৰিক্ত টোপনি নাযাওঁ।"
"1458","They were arguing about whose turn it was.","kār pāl ha’l buli tarka kari āchil.","কাৰ পাল হ’ল বুলি তৰ্ক কৰি আছিল।"
"1459","I was stirring the sauce when she walked in.","mai cāṭnikhan larāi āchilo yetiyā tāi xomāi āhil.","মই চাটনিখন লৰাই আছিলো যেতিয়া তাই সোমাই আহিল।"
"1460","They have not tried sushi before.","teoṃloke āgte cuci cexṭā karā nāi.","তেওঁলোকে আগতে চুচি চেষ্টা কৰা নাই।"
"1461","She will be serving dinner by the time we arrive.","āmi ahār xamaylaike tāi rātir āhār paribexan karib.","আমি অহাৰ সময়লৈকে তাই ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ পৰিবেশন কৰিব।"
"1462","We had not realized the milk was expired.","gākhīrar myād ukli gaiche āmi gam powā nāchiloṃ.","গাখীৰৰ ম্যাদ উকলি গৈছে আমি গম পোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1463","Are the potatoes boiling yet?","ālu etiyāo utli āche neki?","আলু এতিয়াও উতলি আছে নেকি?"
"1464","The fish should be cooked at a high temperature.","māchkhini adhik uxṇatāt xijib lāge.","মাছখিনি অধিক উষ্ণতাত সিজিব লাগে।"
"1465","You never eat breakfast.","āpuni ketiyāo brekphāxṭa nākhāy.","আপুনি কেতিয়াও ব্ৰেকফাষ্ট নাখায়।"
"1466","He used to bake his own bread.","teoṃ nije ruṭi nijei banāichil.","তেওঁ নিজে ৰুটি নিজেই বনাইছিল।"
"1467","I wonder if the chicken is done.","bhābichoṃ kukurāṭo hai ga’l neki?","ভাবিছোঁ কুকুৰাটো হৈ গ’ল নেকি?"
"1468","Most fruits are rich in vitamins.","bechibhāg phalte pracur parimāṇe bhiṭāmin thāke.","বেছিভাগ ফলতে প্ৰচুৰ পৰিমাণে ভিটামিন থাকে।"
"1469","We ourselves grew the herbs.","āmi nijei banauxdhibor kheti karichiloṃ.","আমি নিজেই বনৌষধিবোৰ খেতি কৰিছিলোঁ।"
"1470","If you overcook the pasta, it becomes mushy.","pāxtākhini atimātrā xijāile i māchi hai pare.","পাস্তাখিনি অতিমাত্ৰা সিজাইলে ই মাছি হৈ পৰে।"
"1471","Have you tried the new bakery?","natun bekārīkhan cexṭā kari cāichene?","নতুন বেকাৰীখন চেষ্টা কৰি চাইছেনে?"
"1472","The dishes should have been washed by now.","etiyālaike bācan-bartanbor dhui pelowā ha’b lāgichil.","এতিয়ালৈকে বাচন-বৰ্তনবোৰ ধুই পেলোৱা হ’ব লাগিছিল।"
"1473","He could not taste the difference.","xi pārthakyar xowād la’b parā nāchil.","সি পাৰ্থক্যৰ সোৱাদ ল’ব পৰা নাছিল।"
"1474","Somebody must have eaten the last cookie.","konobāi nixcay xexar kukijṭo khāiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় শেষৰ কুকিজটো খাইছে।"
"1475","I will not eat any junk food this week.","ei xaptāhat mai kono dharṇar jāṃk phuḍ nākhām.","এই সপ্তাহত মই কোনো ধৰণৰ জাংক ফুড নাখাম।"
"1476","They were debating where to eat.","ka't khāb xei bitarka kari āchil.","ক'ত খাব সেই বিতৰ্ক কৰি আছিল।"
"1477","This pie smells as good as it tastes.","ei pāir gondha yimān bhāl ximānei bhāl.","এই পাইৰ গোন্ধ যিমান ভাল সিমানেই ভাল।"
"1478","He cooks better than anyone I know.","mor cināki mānuhatkai xi bhālkai rāndhe.","মোৰ চিনাকি মানুহতকৈ সি ভালকৈ ৰান্ধে।"
"1479","The more spices you add, the more flavorful the dish becomes.","yimānei bechi machlā dib ximānei khādyar xowādyukta hai pare.","যিমানেই বেছি মছলা দিব সিমানেই খাদ্যৰ সোৱাদযুক্ত হৈ পৰে।"
"1480","These sandwiches were made fresh this morning.","ei ceṇḍuicbor āji puwā xatej kari banowā haichil.","এই চেণ্ডুইচবোৰ আজি পুৱা সতেজ কৰি বনোৱা হৈছিল।"
"1481","I was reading a book on the train.","relat kitāp paṛhi āchilo.","ৰেলত কিতাপ পঢ়ি আছিলো।"
"1482","They have not booked their hotel yet.","teoṃloke etiyāo nijar hoṭel bukiṃ karā nāi.","তেওঁলোকে এতিয়াও নিজৰ হোটেল বুকিং কৰা নাই।"
"1483","She will be landing in about an hour.","prāy eghaṇṭār pichat tāi awatraṇ karib.","প্ৰায় এঘণ্টাৰ পিছত তাই অৱতৰণ কৰিব।"
"1484","We had not expected the delay.","palam ha’b buli āmi āxā karā nāchiloṃ.","পলম হ’ব বুলি আমি আশা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1485","Is the bus coming?","bāch āhi āche neki?","বাছ আহি আছে নেকি?"
"1486","Seatbelts should be worn at all times.","xaklo xamayte chiṭbelṭa pindhib lāge.","সকলো সময়তে ছিটবেল্ট পিন্ধিব লাগে।"
"1487","You always sit by the window.","āpuni xadāy khirikīr kāxat bahi thāke.","আপুনি সদায় খিৰিকীৰ কাষত বহি থাকে।"
"1488","She used to take the subway.","tāi āgte chābmeṭra’t uṭhichil.","তাই আগতে ছাবমেট্ৰ’ত উঠিছিল।"
"1489","I wonder when the next train departs.","pichar relkhan ketiyā rāonā ha’b buli bhābo.","পিছৰ ৰেলখন কেতিয়া ৰাওনা হ’ব বুলি ভাবো।"
"1490","Most tourists prefer guided tours.","bechibhāg paryaṭke gāiḍ ṭyur pachanda kare.","বেছিভাগ পৰ্যটকে গাইড ট্যুৰ পছন্দ কৰে।"
"1491","They themselves navigated through the jungle.","teoṃloke nijei jaṃghlar mājere yātrā karichil.","তেওঁলোকে নিজেই জংঘলৰ মাজেৰে যাত্ৰা কৰিছিল।"
"1492","If the weather is clear, we will go sailing.","batar parixkār ha’le āmi jāhāj calāblai yām.","বতৰ পৰিষ্কাৰ হ’লে আমি জাহাজ চলাবলৈ যাম।"
"1493","Have they checked into the hotel?","hoṭelat cek in karichene?","হোটেলত চেক ইন কৰিছেনে?"
"1494","Tickets should have been purchased in advance.","āgtīyākai ṭikaṭ kray karib lāgichil.","আগতীয়াকৈ টিকট ক্ৰয় কৰিব লাগিছিল।"
"1495","She could not find her boarding pass.","tāi barḍiṃ pāch bicāri nāpāle.","তাই বৰ্ডিং পাছ বিচাৰি নাপালে।"
"1496","Somebody must have taken the wrong suitcase.","konobāi nixcay bhul chuṭkechṭo laiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় ভুল ছুটকেছটো লৈছে।"
"1497","I will not travel during the holidays.","bandhar dinat mai bhramaṇ nakro.","বন্ধৰ দিনত মই ভ্ৰমণ নকৰো।"
"1498","They were discussing which route to take.","konṭo path la’b lāge xei bixye ālocnā kari āchil.","কোনটো পথ ল’ব লাগে সেই বিষয়ে আলোচনা কৰি আছিল।"
"1499","This flight is longer than the previous one.","ei bimānkhan āgrakhnatkai dīghlīyā.","এই বিমানখন আগৰখনতকৈ দীঘলীয়া।"
"1500","She drives more carefully than he does.","teoṃtkai tāi bechi xāwdhāne gāṛī calāy.","তেওঁতকৈ তাই বেছি সাৱধানে গাড়ী চলায়।"
"1501","The sooner you book, the cheaper the fare.","yimān xonkāle bukiṃ karib ximānei bhāṛā kam ha’b.","যিমান সোনকালে বুকিং কৰিব সিমানেই ভাড়া কম হ’ব।"
"1502","This bridge was built over a hundred years ago.","ei dalaṃkhan ex bacharro adhik pūrbe nirmāṇ karā haichil.","এই দলংখন এশ বছৰৰো অধিক পূৰ্বে নিৰ্মাণ কৰা হৈছিল।"
"1503","I was helping my mother in the kitchen.","pākaghrat mākak xahāy kari āchilo.","পাকঘৰত মাকক সহায় কৰি আছিলো।"
"1504","They have not visited us in a long time.","bahudin āmār ocarlai ahā nāi.","বহুদিন আমাৰ ওচৰলৈ অহা নাই।"
"1505","She will be babysitting the twins tonight.","āji rāti tāi yamaj xantān duṭār yatna la’b.","আজি ৰাতি তাই যমজ সন্তান দুটাৰ যত্ন ল’ব।"
"1506","We had not known about the pregnancy.","garbhāwaxthār bixye āmi gam powā nāchiloṃ.","গৰ্ভাৱস্থাৰ বিষয়ে আমি গম পোৱা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1507","Are the kids doing their homework?","la’rā-chowālībore homwarka kari āche neki?","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰে হোমৱৰ্ক কৰি আছে নেকি?"
"1508","The house should be painted every few years.","keibcharmānar mūre mūre gharṭo raṃ karib lāge.","কেইবছৰমানৰ মূৰে মূৰে ঘৰটো ৰং কৰিব লাগে।"
"1509","You always leave your shoes by the door.","āpuni xadāy duwārar kāxat jotāyor eri thai yāy.","আপুনি সদায় দুৱাৰৰ কাষত জোতাযোৰ এৰি থৈ যায়।"
"1510","He used to read to his children every night.","prati nixāi teoṃ nijar la’rā-chowālīk paṛhichil.","প্ৰতি নিশাই তেওঁ নিজৰ ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীক পঢ়িছিল।"
"1511","I wonder how my parents met.","māk-deutāk kenekai lag pāichiloṃ bhābichoṃ.","মাক-দেউতাক কেনেকৈ লগ পাইছিলোঁ ভাবিছোঁ।"
"1512","Most families eat dinner together.","bechibhāg pariyāle ekelge rātir āhār khāy.","বেছিভাগ পৰিয়ালে একেলগে ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খায়।"
"1513","If the roof leaks, we will need to call a plumber.","yadi cālkhan lik hay tente āmi plāmbārak mātib lāgib.","যদি চালখন লিক হয় তেন্তে আমি প্লাম্বাৰক মাতিব লাগিব।"

"1595","The charge should have been reversed by now.","etiyālaike abhiyog ulṭib lāgichil.","এতিয়ালৈকে অভিযোগ উলটিব লাগিছিল।"
"1596","He could not afford the rent.","bhāṛār xāmarthya teoṃr nāchil.","ভাড়াৰ সামৰ্থ্য তেওঁৰ নাছিল।"
"1597","Somebody must have used my credit card.","konobāi nixcay mor kreḍiṭ kārḍa byawhār karichil.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় মোৰ ক্ৰেডিট কাৰ্ড ব্যৱহাৰ কৰিছিল।"
"1598","I will not spend more than my budget allows.","mor bājeṭe anumti diyātkai bechi kharac nakro.","মোৰ বাজেটে অনুমতি দিয়াতকৈ বেছি খৰচ নকৰো।"
"1599","They were arguing over the bill.","bidheykakhnak lai duyore mājat tarka-bitarka haichil.","বিধেয়কখনক লৈ দুয়োৰে মাজত তৰ্ক-বিতৰ্ক হৈছিল।"
"1600","This brand is cheaper than that one.","ei breṇḍaṭo xeiṭotkai xaxtā.","এই ব্ৰেণ্ডটো সেইটোতকৈ সস্তা।"
"1601","She is the most careful shopper I know.","tāi mor cināki āṭāitkai xāwdhān bajār karā mānuhajnī.","তাই মোৰ চিনাকি আটাইতকৈ সাৱধান বজাৰ কৰা মানুহজনী।"
"1602","Several items were recalled due to safety concerns.","xurakxār prati lakxya rākhi keibāṭāo xāmagrī punar āhbān karā hay.","সুৰক্ষাৰ প্ৰতি লক্ষ্য ৰাখি কেইবাটাও সামগ্ৰী পুনৰ আহ্বান কৰা হয়।"
"1603","Most of the cost goes to shipping.","kharcar bechibhāgei xipiṃr bābe yāy.","খৰচৰ বেছিভাগেই শিপিংৰ বাবে যায়।"
"1604","I was thinking about you when you called.","tumi phon karār xamyat mai tomār kathā bhābi āchilo.","তুমি ফোন কৰাৰ সময়ত মই তোমাৰ কথা ভাবি আছিলো।"
"1605","They have not resolved their differences.","teoṃlokar matānaikyar xamādhān howā nāi.","তেওঁলোকৰ মতানৈক্যৰ সমাধান হোৱা নাই।"
"1606","She will be missing him while he is away.","xi āṃtrat thakār xamyat tāi tāk mich karib.","সি আঁতৰত থকাৰ সময়ত তাই তাক মিছ কৰিব।"
"1607","We had not seen each other in years.","bachar bachar dhari āmi ijne xijnak dekhā nāchiloṃ.","বছৰ বছৰ ধৰি আমি ইজনে সিজনক দেখা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1608","Are the children fighting again?","la’rā-chowālībore ākau kājiyā kariche neki?","ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীবোৰে আকৌ কাজিয়া কৰিছে নেকি?"
"1609","Feelings should be expressed openly.","anubhawbor muklikai prakāx karib lāge.","অনুভৱবোৰ মুকলিকৈ প্ৰকাশ কৰিব লাগে।"
"1610","You always worry about everything.","āpuni xadāy xaklo kathāte cintā kare.","আপুনি সদায় সকলো কথাতে চিন্তা কৰে।"
"1611","He used to be afraid of the dark.","āgte teoṃ āndhārlai bhay karichil.","আগতে তেওঁ আন্ধাৰলৈ ভয় কৰিছিল।"
"1612","I wonder why she looked so sad.","bhābichoṃ tāik kiy imān dukhī yen lāgichil.","ভাবিছোঁ তাইক কিয় ইমান দুখী যেন লাগিছিল।"
"1613","Most people want to be loved.","bechibhāg mānuhei bhāl powāṭo bicāre.","বেছিভাগ মানুহেই ভাল পোৱাটো বিচাৰে।"
"1614","If you trust someone, tell them.","kārobāk bixbāx karile kaok.","কাৰোবাক বিশ্বাস কৰিলে কওক।"
"1615","Have you ever been in love?","ketiyābā premat pariche neki?","কেতিয়াবা প্ৰেমত পৰিছে নেকি?"
"1616","Her feelings should have been considered.","tāir anubhawbor bibecnā karā ucit āchil.","তাইৰ অনুভৱবোৰ বিবেচনা কৰা উচিত আছিল।"
"1617","He could not hide his disappointment.","teoṃ nijar hatāxā lukuwāb parā nāchil.","তেওঁ নিজৰ হতাশা লুকুৱাব পৰা নাছিল।"
"1618","Somebody must have spread the rumor.","konobāi nixcay urābātriṭo biypāiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় উৰাবাতৰিটো বিয়পাইছে।"
"1619","I will not give up on us.","āmār ocrat mai hār nāmāno.","আমাৰ ওচৰত মই হাৰ নামানো।"
"1620","They were reminiscing about their childhood.","xaixwar kathā manat pelāi āchil.","শৈশৱৰ কথা মনত পেলাই আছিল।"
"1621","She is more empathetic than most people.","bechibhāg mānuhatkai tāi bechi xahānubhūtixīl.","বেছিভাগ মানুহতকৈ তাই বেছি সহানুভূতিশীল।"
"1622","This is the happiest I have ever been.","eiṭowei mor bābe āṭāitkai xukhī.","এইটোৱেই মোৰ বাবে আটাইতকৈ সুখী।"
"1623","Several relationships were damaged by the scandal.","ei keleṃkārīr phalat keibāṭāo xamparka kxatigraxta hay.","এই কেলেংকাৰীৰ ফলত কেইবাটাও সম্পৰ্ক ক্ষতিগ্ৰস্ত হয়।"
"1624","Most of the blame falls on me.","bechibhāg dox mor oprat pare.","বেছিভাগ দোষ মোৰ ওপৰত পৰে।"
"1625","I was painting when the phone rang.","mai peinṭiṃ kari thākoṃte phonṭo bāji uṭhil.","মই পেইন্টিং কৰি থাকোঁতে ফোনটো বাজি উঠিল।"
"1626","They have not decided where to go.","teoṃloke ka’lai yāb xeiṭo ṭhik karā nāi.","তেওঁলোকে ক’লৈ যাব সেইটো ঠিক কৰা নাই।"
"1627","She will be performing at the festival next week.","ahā xaptāhat ei utxawat anuxṭhān pariwexan karib chānīye.","অহা সপ্তাহত এই উৎসৱত অনুষ্ঠান পৰিৱেশন কৰিব ছানীয়ে।"
"1628","We had not planned for rain.","barxuṇar bābe āmi parikalpanā karā nāchiloṃ.","বৰষুণৰ বাবে আমি পৰিকল্পনা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1629","Are the musicians tuning their instruments?","xaṃgītajñaxakle nijar bādyayantra ṭiun kari āche neki?","সংগীতজ্ঞসকলে নিজৰ বাদ্যযন্ত্ৰ টিউন কৰি আছে নেকি?"
"1630","The rules should be explained before the game.","khelar āgte niyambor bujāb lāge.","খেলৰ আগতে নিয়মবোৰ বুজাব লাগে।"
"1631","You always cheer for the underdog.","āpuni xadāy āṇḍāraḍgar bābe ullāx kare.","আপুনি সদায় আণ্ডাৰডগৰ বাবে উল্লাস কৰে।"
"1632","He used to collect baseball cards.","āgte teoṃ bechablar kārḍa xaṃgrah karichil.","আগতে তেওঁ বেছবলৰ কাৰ্ড সংগ্ৰহ কৰিছিল।"
"1633","I wonder who won the championship.","mai bhābo kone cempiynaxbip lābh karichil.","মই ভাবো কোনে চেম্পিয়নশ্বিপ লাভ কৰিছিল।"
"1634","Most children love playing in the sand.","bechibhāg xixuwe bālit kheli bhāl pāy.","বেছিভাগ শিশুৱে বালিত খেলি ভাল পায়।"
"1635","If the weather holds, we will have a picnic.","batar dhāraṇ karile āmi piknik khām.","বতৰ ধাৰণ কৰিলে আমি পিকনিক খাম।"
"1636","Have you ever been to a music festival?","āpuni ketiyābā xaṃgīt utxawlai gaichene?","আপুনি কেতিয়াবা সংগীত উৎসৱলৈ গৈছেনে?"
"1637","The event should have been advertised better.","anuxṭhānṭor bijñāpan bhālkai dib lāgichil.","অনুষ্ঠানটোৰ বিজ্ঞাপন ভালকৈ দিব লাগিছিল।"
"1638","She could not remember the lyrics.","gītar kathā manat pelāb parā nāchil tāir.","গীতৰ কথা মনত পেলাব পৰা নাছিল তাইৰ।"
"1639","Somebody must have taken my seat.","konobāi nixcay mor āxankhan laiche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় মোৰ আসনখন লৈছে।"
"1640","I will not spoil the ending.","mai xex naxṭa nakro.","মই শেষ নষ্ট নকৰো।"
"1641","They were debating which movie to watch.","konkhan cinemā cāb lāge bitarka kari āchil.","কোনখন চিনেমা চাব লাগে বিতৰ্ক কৰি আছিল।"
"1642","This game is more challenging than I thought.","ei khelkhan mai bhabātkaio adhik pratyāhbānmūlak.","এই খেলখন মই ভবাতকৈও অধিক প্ৰত্যাহ্বানমূলক।"
"1643","She is the most talented dancer in the group.","grupar āṭāitkai pratibhāxālī nr̥tyaxilpī.","গ্ৰুপৰ আটাইতকৈ প্ৰতিভাশালী নৃত্যশিল্পী।"
"1644","Several paintings were damaged during the move.","ei xthānāntarar xamyat keibākhno chabir kxatixādhan hay.","এই স্থানান্তৰৰ সময়ত কেইবাখনো ছবিৰ ক্ষতিসাধন হয়।"
"1645","Most of the audience left before the encore.","enkorar āgtei bechibhāg darxake guci ga’l.","এনকোৰৰ আগতেই বেছিভাগ দৰ্শকে গুচি গ’ল।"
"1646","I was texting her when the battery died.","mai tāik mechej kari āchilo yetiyā beṭārīṭo mari ga’l.","মই তাইক মেছেজ কৰি আছিলো যেতিয়া বেটাৰীটো মৰি গ’ল।"
"1647","They have not answered the question.","teoṃloke praxnar uttar diyā nāi.","তেওঁলোকে প্ৰশ্নৰ উত্তৰ দিয়া নাই।"
"1648","She will be giving a toast at the wedding.","biyāt tāi ṭoxṭa dib.","বিয়াত তাই টোষ্ট দিব।"
"1649","We had not discussed the matter beforehand.","āmi ei bixye āgte ālocnā karā nāchiloṃ.","আমি এই বিষয়ে আগতে আলোচনা কৰা নাছিলোঁ।"
"1650","Are the guests arriving soon?","atithixkal xonkāle āhib neki?","অতিথিসকল সোনকালে আহিব নেকি?"
"1651","Promises should be kept.","pratixruti pālan karib lāge.","প্ৰতিশ্ৰুতি পালন কৰিব লাগে।"
"1652","You always change the subject.","āpuni xadāy bixay xalni kare.","আপুনি সদায় বিষয় সলনি কৰে।"
"1653","He used to stutter as a child.","xarute xi thatmat khāichil.","সৰুতে সি থতমত খাইছিল।"
"1654","I wonder what she meant by that.","bhābichoṃ tāi iyār dbārā ki bujāb bicārichil.","ভাবিছোঁ তাই ইয়াৰ দ্বাৰা কি বুজাব বিচাৰিছিল।"
"1655","Most adults have at least one close friend.","bechibhāg prāptabyaxkar antataḥ ejan ghanixṭha bandhu thāke.","বেছিভাগ প্ৰাপ্তবয়স্কৰ অন্ততঃ এজন ঘনিষ্ঠ বন্ধু থাকে।"
"1656","If you ask politely, people will help.","bhadrabhāwe xudhile mānuhe xahāy karib.","ভদ্ৰভাৱে সুধিলে মানুহে সহায় কৰিব।"
"1657","Have you spoken to her about it?","tāir lagat ei bixye kathā pātichāne?","তাইৰ লগত এই বিষয়ে কথা পাতিছানে?"
"1658","The agreement should have been signed earlier.","cuktikhan āgte xbākxarit ha’b lāgichil.","চুক্তিখন আগতে স্বাক্ষৰিত হ’ব লাগিছিল।"
"1659","He could not find the right words.","teoṃ xaṭhik xabda bicāri nāpāle.","তেওঁ সঠিক শব্দ বিচাৰি নাপালে।"
"1660","Somebody must have overheard us.","konobāi nixcay āmār kathā xuniche.","কোনোবাই নিশ্চয় আমাৰ কথা শুনিছে।"
"1661","I will not break my promise.","mai mor pratixruti bhaṃg nakro.","মই মোৰ প্ৰতিশ্ৰুতি ভংগ নকৰো।"
"1662","They were whispering to each other.","ijne xijnak phucphucāi kai āchil.","ইজনে সিজনক ফুচফুচাই কৈ আছিল।"
"1663","She speaks more convincingly than anyone I know.","mai cināki mānuhatkai tāi bechi patiyan yogyabhāwe kathā kay.","মই চিনাকি মানুহতকৈ তাই বেছি পতিয়ন যোগ্যভাৱে কথা কয়।"
"1664","This is the longest speech I have ever heard.","eiṭo mor xunā āṭāitkai dīghlīyā bhāxaṇ.","এইটো মোৰ শুনা আটাইতকৈ দীঘলীয়া ভাষণ।"
"1665","Several witnesses were interviewed by the police.","keibājno xākxīr xākxātkār laichil ārakxīye.","কেইবাজনো সাক্ষীৰ সাক্ষাৎকাৰ লৈছিল আৰক্ষীয়ে।"
"1666","Most of the information was incorrect.","bechibhāg tathyai bhul āchil.","বেছিভাগ তথ্যই ভুল আছিল।"
"1667","He whispered so that nobody else could hear.","xi phucphucāi ka’le yāte ān konowe xunib nowāre.","সি ফুচফুচাই ক’লে যাতে আন কোনোৱে শুনিব নোৱাৰে।"
"1668","They introduced one another to their families.","ijne xijnak nijar pariyālar lagat cināki karāi dile.","ইজনে সিজনক নিজৰ পৰিয়ালৰ লগত চিনাকি কৰাই দিলে।"
"1669","I had been meaning to call you.","mai tomāk phon karār artha āchiloṃ.","মই তোমাক ফোন কৰাৰ অৰ্থ আছিলোঁ।"
"1670","We need to talk.","āmi kathā patā prayojan.","আমি কথা পতা প্ৰয়োজন।"
"1671","Nobody can force you to agree.","koneo āponāk jor kari mānti ha’b nowāre.","কোনেও আপোনাক জোৰ কৰি মান্তি হ’ব নোৱাৰে।"
"1672","All of them arrived on time.","xaklo xamyamte āhi pālehi.","সকলো সময়মতে আহি পালেহি।"
"1673","A few people disagreed with the plan.","dui ejan loke ei parikalpanār xaite matānaikya prakāx karichil.","দুই এজন লোকে এই পৰিকল্পনাৰ সৈতে মতানৈক্য প্ৰকাশ কৰিছিল।"
"1674","She said that she would think about it.","tāi bhābib buli ka’le.","তাই ভাবিব বুলি ক’লে।"
"1675","He asked whether I agreed.","mai mānti neki buli xudhile.","মই মান্তি নেকি বুলি সুধিলে।"
"1676","I overslept this morning.","āji rātipuwā atimātrā ṭopni āhiloṃ.","আজি ৰাতিপুৱা অতিমাত্ৰা টোপনি আহিলোঁ।"
"1677","We did not have time for breakfast.","āmār brekphāxṭar xamay nāchil.","আমাৰ ব্ৰেকফাষ্টৰ সময় নাছিল।"
"1678","You will need to set two alarms.","āpuni duṭā elārma cheṭ karib lāgib.","আপুনি দুটা এলাৰ্ম ছেট কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1679","We have been living in this apartment for three years.","āmi ei epārṭameṇṭaṭot thakā tini bachar ha’l","আমি এই এপাৰ্টমেণ্টটোত থকা তিনি বছৰ হ’ল৷"
"1680","You used to watch cartoons on Saturday mornings.","xanibāre puwā kārṭun cāichilā.","শনিবাৰে পুৱা কাৰ্টুন চাইছিলা।"
"1681","I am not in the mood to go out.","mor bāhirlai olāi yowār muḍ nāi.","মোৰ বাহিৰলৈ ওলাই যোৱাৰ মুড নাই।"
"1682","Will we have enough hot water?","āmār hātat yathexṭa garam pānī thākibne?","আমাৰ হাতত যথেষ্ট গৰম পানী থাকিবনে?"
"1683","Tell me when you are ready.","ketiyā xāju ha’b kaokcon.","কেতিয়া সাজু হ’ব কওকচোন।"
"1684","I can hear the neighbors arguing.","ocar cuburīyār tarka-bitarka xuniblai pāoṃ.","ওচৰ চুবুৰীয়াৰ তৰ্ক-বিতৰ্ক শুনিবলৈ পাওঁ।"
"1685","You have not watered the plants.","āpuni gach-gachniborak pānī diyā nāi.","আপুনি গছ-গছনিবোৰক পানী দিয়া নাই।"
"1686","I made pasta for the first time.","prathambārar bābe pāxtā banāloṃ.","প্ৰথমবাৰৰ বাবে পাস্তা বনালোঁ।"
"1687","We did not buy enough milk.","āmi paryāpta gākhīr kinā nāchilo.","আমি পৰ্যাপ্ত গাখীৰ কিনা নাছিলো।"
"1688","You will love this dish.","ei khādya āpuni bhāl pāb.","এই খাদ্য আপুনি ভাল পাব।"
"1689","We have been trying to eat more vegetables.","āmi adhik xāk-pācli khāblai cexṭā kari āhicho.","আমি অধিক শাক-পাচলি খাবলৈ চেষ্টা কৰি আহিছো।"
"1690","You used to drink coffee with sugar.","āgte āpuni cenire kaphi khāichil.","আগতে আপুনি চেনিৰে কফি খাইছিল।"
"1691","I am not hungry yet.","mor etiyāo bhok nāi.","মোৰ এতিয়াও ভোক নাই।"
"1692","Will we have enough food for everyone?","āmār xaklore bābe paryāpta khādya thākibne?","আমাৰ সকলোৰে বাবে পৰ্যাপ্ত খাদ্য থাকিবনে?"
"1693","Pour me a glass of water.","mok egilāc pānī ḍhāli diyā.","মোক এগিলাচ পানী ঢালি দিয়া।"
"1694","I can smell something burning.","kibā eṭā jbali thakā gondha pāichoṃ.","কিবা এটা জ্বলি থকা গোন্ধ পাইছোঁ।"
"1695","You have not finished your plate.","tomār pleṭ xex howā nāi.","তোমাৰ প্লেট শেষ হোৱা নাই।"
"1696","I arrived late at the airport.","eyārparṭat derikai āhi pāloṃ","এয়াৰপৰ্টত দেৰিকৈ আহি পালোঁ৷"
"1697","We did not pack enough warm clothes.","āmi paryāpta garam kāpor pek karā nāchilo.","আমি পৰ্যাপ্ত গৰম কাপোৰ পেক কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1698","You will enjoy the view from the top.","oprar parā dr̥xya upbhog karib.","ওপৰৰ পৰা দৃশ্য উপভোগ কৰিব।"
"1699","We have been planning this trip for months.","āmi keibāmāho dhari ei bhramṇar parikalpanā kari āhicho.","আমি কেইবামাহো ধৰি এই ভ্ৰমণৰ পৰিকল্পনা কৰি আহিছো।"
"1700","You used to travel a lot for work.","āgte āpuni kāmar bābe bahut bhramaṇ karichil.","আগতে আপুনি কামৰ বাবে বহুত ভ্ৰমণ কৰিছিল।"
"1701","I am not used to flying.","urājāhāj calowāt mor abhyāx nāi.","উৰাজাহাজ চলোৱাত মোৰ অভ্যাস নাই।"
"1702","Will we arrive before dark?","āmi āndhār howār āgtei āhi pāmne?","আমি আন্ধাৰ হোৱাৰ আগতেই আহি পামনে?"
"1703","Bring your own water.","nijar pānī nije lai āhib.","নিজৰ পানী নিজে লৈ আহিব।"
"1704","I can see the mountains from here.","iyār parā pāhārbor dekhā pāoṃ.","ইয়াৰ পৰা পাহাৰবোৰ দেখা পাওঁ।"
"1705","You have not renewed your passport.","āpuni āponār pāchpa’rṭa nabīkraṇ karā nāi.","আপুনি আপোনাৰ পাছপ’ৰ্ট নবীকৰণ কৰা নাই।"
"1706","I helped my daughter with her homework.","mor chowālījnīr homwarkat xahāy kariloṃ.","মোৰ ছোৱালীজনীৰ হোমৱৰ্কত সহায় কৰিলোঁ।"
"1707","We did not expect them to arrive so early.","imān xonkāle teoṃlok āhib buli āmi bhabā nāchilo.","ইমান সোনকালে তেওঁলোক আহিব বুলি আমি ভবা নাছিলো।"
"1708","You will meet my brother at the dinner.","rātir āhārat mor bhāiṭik lag pāb.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰত মোৰ ভাইটিক লগ পাব।"
"1709","We have been talking about getting a bigger place.","āmi ḍāṅar ṭhāi powār kathā kai āhicho.","আমি ডাঙৰ ঠাই পোৱাৰ কথা কৈ আহিছো।"
"1710","You used to fight with your sister a lot.","āgte bhanīyekar lagat bahut kājiyā karichilā.","আগতে ভনীয়েকৰ লগত বহুত কাজিয়া কৰিছিলা।"
"1711","I am not going to my cousin's wedding.","mai mor khulxālīyekar biyālai yowā nāi.","মই মোৰ খুলশালীয়েকৰ বিয়ালৈ যোৱা নাই।"
"1712","Will we all fit in one car?","āmi xaklowe ekhan gāṛīt xomābne?","আমি সকলোৱে এখন গাড়ীত সোমাবনে?"
"1713","Show me the photo album.","phaṭo elbāmṭo dekhuwāok.","ফটো এলবামটো দেখুৱাওক।"
"1714","You have not called your mother in weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari mākak phon karā nāi.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি মাকক ফোন কৰা নাই।"
"1715","I finished the report last night.","yowā nixā riparṭaṭo xex kariloṃ.","যোৱা নিশা ৰিপৰ্টটো শেষ কৰিলোঁ।"
"1716","We did not reach an agreement.","āmi kono cuktit upnīt howā nāchilo.","আমি কোনো চুক্তিত উপনীত হোৱা নাছিলো।"
"1717","You will receive a confirmation email.","āpuni eṭā nixcitakraṇ imeil lābh karib.","আপুনি এটা নিশ্চিতকৰণ ইমেইল লাভ কৰিব।"
"1718","We have been working on this project for two months.","āmi dumāh dhari ei prakalpaṭor kām kari ācho.","আমি দুমাহ ধৰি এই প্ৰকল্পটোৰ কাম কৰি আছো।"
"1719","You used to take notes by hand.","āgte hātere ṭokā laichilā.","আগতে হাতেৰে টোকা লৈছিলা।"
"1720","I am not satisfied with the results.","phalāphlat mai xantuxṭa nahay.","ফলাফলত মই সন্তুষ্ট নহয়।"
"1721","Will we finish on schedule?","āmi nirdhārit xamyat xex karibne?","আমি নিৰ্ধাৰিত সময়ত শেষ কৰিবনে?"
"1722","Send me the updated version.","āpḍeṭ karā xaṃxkaraṇṭo molai paṭhāok.","আপডেট কৰা সংস্কৰণটো মোলৈ পঠাওক।"
"1723","I can see the potential in this idea.","ei dhārṇāṭor xambhāwnā mai dekhiblai pāoṃ.","এই ধাৰণাটোৰ সম্ভাৱনা মই দেখিবলৈ পাওঁ।"
"1724","You have not submitted the final draft.","āpuni cūṛānta khacrā dākhil karā nāi.","আপুনি চূড়ান্ত খচৰা দাখিল কৰা নাই।"
"1725","I climbed the highest peak in the range.","reñjar āṭāitkai okh xr̥ṃgṭot uṭhiloṃ","ৰেঞ্জৰ আটাইতকৈ ওখ শৃংগটোত উঠিলোঁ৷"
"1726","We did not bring enough sunscreen.","āmi paryāpta parimāṇar chānaxkrīṇ lai ahā nāchilo.","আমি পৰ্যাপ্ত পৰিমাণৰ ছানস্ক্ৰীণ লৈ অহা নাছিলো।"
"1727","You will feel the temperature drop at night.","rāti tāpmātrā kami anubhaw karib.","ৰাতি তাপমাত্ৰা কমি অনুভৱ কৰিব।"
"1728","We have been hiking since sunrise.","āmi xūryya udyar parāi hāikiṃ kari ācho.","আমি সূৰ্য্য উদয়ৰ পৰাই হাইকিং কৰি আছো।"
"1729","You used to be afraid of thunderstorms.","āgte āpuni dhumuhāt bhay karichil.","আগতে আপুনি ধুমুহাত ভয় কৰিছিল।"
"1730","I am not comfortable camping in the wilderness.","prāntarat kempiṃ kari mai ārāmdāyak nahay.","প্ৰান্তৰত কেম্পিং কৰি মই আৰামদায়ক নহয়।"
"1731","Will we see any wildlife?","āmi kono banyaprāṇī dekhā pāmne?","আমি কোনো বন্যপ্ৰাণী দেখা পামনে?"
"1732","Look at that sunset.","xei xūryāxtaṭo cāokcon.","সেই সূৰ্যাস্তটো চাওকচোন।"
"1733","I can feel the humidity rising.","ārdratā bāṛhi ahā anubhaw karib pāricho.","আৰ্দ্ৰতা বাঢ়ি অহা অনুভৱ কৰিব পাৰিছো।"
"1734","You have not seen anything like this before.","āpuni āgte enekuwā eko dekhā nāi.","আপুনি আগতে এনেকুৱা একো দেখা নাই।"
"1735","I twisted my ankle during the game.","khelar xamyat bharir gorohāṭo pelāi diloṃ.","খেলৰ সময়ত ভৰিৰ গোৰোহাটো পেলাই দিলোঁ।"
"1736","We did not notice the warning signs.","xatarkabāṇīr cinbor āmi lakxya karā nāchilo.","সতৰ্কবাণীৰ চিনবোৰ আমি লক্ষ্য কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1737","You will feel much better in the morning.","rātipuwā bahut bhāl lāgib.","ৰাতিপুৱা বহুত ভাল লাগিব।"
"1738","We have been exercising every day this month.","āmi ei māhat pratidine byāyām kari āhicho.","আমি এই মাহত প্ৰতিদিনে ব্যায়াম কৰি আহিছো।"
"1739","You used to be in great shape.","āgte tumi bar cepat āchilā.","আগতে তুমি বৰ চেপত আছিলা।"
"1740","I am not sure if the clinic is open today.","āji klinik kholā āche ne nāi xeyā nixcit nahay.","আজি ক্লিনিক খোলা আছে নে নাই সেয়া নিশ্চিত নহয়।"
"1741","Will we need to see a specialist?","āmi bixexajñar ocarlai yāb lāgibne?","আমি বিশেষজ্ঞৰ ওচৰলৈ যাব লাগিবনে?"
"1742","Show me where it hurts.","ka’t bix hay dekhuwāok.","ক’ত বিষ হয় দেখুৱাওক।"
"1743","I can barely walk on this knee.","ei āṃṭhuṭot mai kaxṭere khoj kāṛhib pāro.","এই আঁঠুটোত মই কষ্টেৰে খোজ কাঢ়িব পাৰো।"
"1744","You have not taken your vitamins today.","āpuni āji āponār bhiṭāmin grahaṇ karā nāi.","আপুনি আজি আপোনাৰ ভিটামিন গ্ৰহণ কৰা নাই।"
"1745","I found a great deal on winter boots.","xītkālīn buṭar oprat bahut bhāl ḍil pāichiloṃ.","শীতকালীন বুটৰ ওপৰত বহুত ভাল ডিল পাইছিলোঁ।"
"1746","We did not save enough for the down payment.","ḍāun pemeṇṭar bābe āmi paryāpta parimāṇe xañcay karā nāchilo.","ডাউন পেমেণ্টৰ বাবে আমি পৰ্যাপ্ত পৰিমাণে সঞ্চয় কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1747","You will not regret this purchase.","ei krayar bābe āpuni ākxep nakrib.","এই ক্ৰয়ৰ বাবে আপুনি আক্ষেপ নকৰিব।"
"1748","We have been saving for a vacation.","āmi chuṭīr bābe xañcay kari āhicho.","আমি ছুটীৰ বাবে সঞ্চয় কৰি আহিছো।"
"1749","You used to buy everything at the market.","āgte āpuni bajārat xaklo kinichil.","আগতে আপুনি বজাৰত সকলো কিনিছিল।"
"1750","I am not willing to pay that price.","xei mūlya diblai mai icchuk nahay.","সেই মূল্য দিবলৈ মই ইচ্ছুক নহয়।"
"1751","Will we get a refund?","āmi dhan ghūrāi pāmne?","আমি ধন ঘূৰাই পামনে?"
"1752","Hand me the catalog.","keṭelagṭo mor hātat tuli diyak.","কেটেলগটো মোৰ হাতত তুলি দিয়ক।"
"1753","I can tell this is handmade.","eiṭo hātere banowā buli ka’b pāricho.","এইটো হাতেৰে বনোৱা বুলি ক’ব পাৰিছো।"
"1754","You have not paid the electricity bill.","āpuni bidyutar bil parixodh karā nāi.","আপুনি বিদ্যুতৰ বিল পৰিশোধ কৰা নাই।"
"1755","I forgave her immediately.","lage lage tāik kxamā kari diloṃ.","লগে লগে তাইক ক্ষমা কৰি দিলোঁ।"
"1756","We did not mean to hurt you.","āmi āponāk āghāt dib bicrā nāchilo.","আমি আপোনাক আঘাত দিব বিচৰা নাছিলো।"
"1757","You will understand when you are older.","ḍāṅar ha’le bujib.","ডাঙৰ হ’লে বুজিব।"
"1758","We have been growing closer over the years.","bachar bachar dhari āmi ghanixṭha hai āhicho.","বছৰ বছৰ ধৰি আমি ঘনিষ্ঠ হৈ আহিছো।"
"1759","You used to make me laugh all the time.","tumi mok xaklo xamayte hāṃhiblai bādhya karāichilā.","তুমি মোক সকলো সময়তে হাঁহিবলৈ বাধ্য কৰাইছিলা।"
"1760","I am not ready to move on.","mai āgbāṛhi yāblai xāju nahay.","মই আগবাঢ়ি যাবলৈ সাজু নহয়।"
"1761","Will we ever agree on this?","ei bixye āmi ketiyābā ekmat ha’mne?","এই বিষয়ে আমি কেতিয়াবা একমত হ’মনে?"
"1762","Forgive me.","mok kxamā karib.","মোক ক্ষমা কৰিব।"
"1763","I can sense that something is wrong.","kibā eṭā bhul haiche buli anubhaw karib pāricho.","কিবা এটা ভুল হৈছে বুলি অনুভৱ কৰিব পাৰিছো।"
"1764","You have not been yourself lately.","xehtīyākai āpuni nijei howā nāi.","শেহতীয়াকৈ আপুনি নিজেই হোৱা নাই।"
"1765","I learned to play chess when I was ten.","dah bachar bayxat mai dabā kheliblai xikichilo.","দহ বছৰ বয়সত মই দবা খেলিবলৈ শিকিছিলো।"
"1766","We did not expect the ending.","āmi xex howār āxā karā nāchilo.","আমি শেষ হোৱাৰ আশা কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1767","You will enjoy the sequel.","chikuwel āponāloke upbhog karib.","ছিকুৱেল আপোনালোকে উপভোগ কৰিব।"
"1768","We have been binge-watching that series all week.","āmi goṭei xaptāhṭo xei dhārābāhikkhan biṃg-wāciṃ kari āhicho.","আমি গোটেই সপ্তাহটো সেই ধাৰাবাহিকখন বিংগ-ৱাচিং কৰি আহিছো।"
"1769","You used to draw pictures of animals.","āgte āpuni jīw-jantur chabi āṃkichil.","আগতে আপুনি জীৱ-জন্তুৰ ছবি আঁকিছিল।"
"1770","I am not interested in sports.","khelar prati mor āgrah nāi.","খেলৰ প্ৰতি মোৰ আগ্ৰহ নাই।"
"1771","Will we get good seats?","āmi bhāl āxan pāmne?","আমি ভাল আসন পামনে?"
"1772","Join us.","āmār lagat yogdān karak.","আমাৰ লগত যোগদান কৰক।"
"1773","I can already tell this movie will be great.","itimadhye ka’b pāricho ei cinemākhan bar bhāl ha’b","ইতিমধ্যে ক’ব পাৰিছো এই চিনেমাখন বৰ ভাল হ’ব৷"
"1774","You have not returned the library books.","āpuni puthibhaṃrālar kitāpbor ghūrāi diyā nāi.","আপুনি পুথিভঁৰালৰ কিতাপবোৰ ঘূৰাই দিয়া নাই।"
"1775","I apologized for the misunderstanding.","bhul bujābujir bābe kxamā khujiloṃ.","ভুল বুজাবুজিৰ বাবে ক্ষমা খুজিলোঁ।"
"1776","We did not realize she was joking.","āmi gam powā nāchilo tāi dhemāli kari āche.","আমি গম পোৱা নাছিলো তাই ধেমালি কৰি আছে।"
"1777","You will regret not telling the truth.","xaṃcā kathā nak’le āpuni anuxocnā karib.","সঁচা কথা নক’লে আপুনি অনুশোচনা কৰিব।"
"1778","We have been friends since kindergarten.","kiṇḍārgārṭenar parāi āmār bandhu.","কিণ্ডাৰগাৰ্টেনৰ পৰাই আমাৰ বন্ধু।"
"1779","You used to write long letters.","āgte āpuni dīghlīyā ciṭhi likhichil.","আগতে আপুনি দীঘলীয়া চিঠি লিখিছিল।"
"1780","I am not comfortable with public speaking.","rājhuwā bhāxṇat mor ārām nāi.","ৰাজহুৱা ভাষণত মোৰ আৰাম নাই।"
"1781","Will we stay in touch?","āmi yogāyogat thākim neki?","আমি যোগাযোগত থাকিম নেকি?"
"1782","Introduce yourself.","nijar paricay diyak.","নিজৰ পৰিচয় দিয়ক।"
"1783","I can see that you are nervous.","mai dekhichoṃ ye tumi nārbhāch haichā.","মই দেখিছোঁ যে তুমি নাৰ্ভাছ হৈছা।"
"1784","You have not responded to my message.","mor bārtār kono xaṃhāri diyā nāi āpuni.","মোৰ বাৰ্তাৰ কোনো সঁহাৰি দিয়া নাই আপুনি।"
"1785","They made fun of him behind his back.","piṭhir pichphāle ṭhāṭṭā-maxkarā karile.","পিঠিৰ পিছফালে ঠাট্টা-মস্কৰা কৰিলে।"
"1786","We promised each other to stay honest.","āmi ijne xijnak xat hai thākiblai pratixruti diloṃ.","আমি ইজনে সিজনক সৎ হৈ থাকিবলৈ প্ৰতিশ্ৰুতি দিলোঁ।"
"1787","I was introduced to her at a party.","tāir lagat mor cināki haichil eṭā pārṭit.","তাইৰ লগত মোৰ চিনাকি হৈছিল এটা পাৰ্টিত।"
"1788","Nobody mentioned the problem.","koneo xamaxyār kathā kowā nāchil.","কোনেও সমস্যাৰ কথা কোৱা নাছিল।"
"1789","Several people volunteered to help.","keibājno loke xbecchāi xahāy āgabṛhāichil.","কেইবাজনো লোকে স্বেচ্ছাই সহায় আগবঢ়াইছিল।"
"1790","A few of us stayed after the event.","āmi dui ejne anuxṭhānar pichat thākiloṃ.","আমি দুই এজনে অনুষ্ঠানৰ পিছত থাকিলোঁ।"
"1791","Both teams congratulated each other.","duyoṭā dale ijne xijnak abhinandan janāy.","দুয়োটা দলে ইজনে সিজনক অভিনন্দন জনায়।"
"1792","Not only did she arrive late, but she also forgot the gift.","derikai āhi powāi nahay, uphārṭoo pāhri ga’l tāi.","দেৰিকৈ আহি পোৱাই নহয়, উপহাৰটোও পাহৰি গ’ল তাই।"
"1793","We both woke up at the same time.","āmi duyo eke xamayte xār pāi uṭhiloṃ.","আমি দুয়ো একে সময়তে সাৰ পাই উঠিলোঁ।"
"1794","I had been waiting for this day.","ei dinṭor bābe mai apekxā kari āchilo.","এই দিনটোৰ বাবে মই অপেক্ষা কৰি আছিলো।"
"1795","Several lights were left on all night.","goṭei rāti keibāṭāo lāiṭ jbalāi rakhā haichil.","গোটেই ৰাতি কেইবাটাও লাইট জ্বলাই ৰখা হৈছিল।"
"1796","You should try a different approach.","āpuni eṭā beleg panthā cexṭā karā ucit.","আপুনি এটা বেলেগ পন্থা চেষ্টা কৰা উচিত।"
"1797","Neither of us remembered the password.","āmār duyore pāchwarḍaṭo manat parā nāchil.","আমাৰ দুয়োৰে পাছৱৰ্ডটো মনত পৰা নাছিল।"
"1798","A few things still need to be organized.","dui eṭā kathā etiyāo xaṃgṭhit karib lāgib.","দুই এটা কথা এতিয়াও সংগঠিত কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1799","I rarely stay up past midnight.","mājnixār pichat mai khub kameihe xui thākoṃ.","মাজনিশাৰ পিছত মই খুব কমেইহে শুই থাকোঁ।"
"1800","They will be moving next weekend.","ahā uikeṇḍat teoṃlok xthānāntarit ha’b.","অহা উইকেণ্ডত তেওঁলোক স্থানান্তৰিত হ’ব।"
"1801","Most mornings, I walk to the station.","bechibhāg rātipuwā khoj kāṛhi xṭecanlai yāoṃ.","বেছিভাগ ৰাতিপুৱা খোজ কাঢ়ি ষ্টেচনলৈ যাওঁ।"
"1802","I did not hear my alarm go off.","mor elārma bāji thakā xunā nāpāloṃ.","মোৰ এলাৰ্ম বাজি থকা শুনা নাপালোঁ।"
"1803","We need to buy a new vacuum.","āmi natun bhekuwām kinib lāgib.","আমি নতুন ভেকুৱাম কিনিব লাগিব।"
"1804","All of our neighbors are very friendly.","āmār xaklo ocar-cuburīyāi bar bandhutbapūrṇa.","আমাৰ সকলো ওচৰ-চুবুৰীয়াই বৰ বন্ধুত্বপূৰ্ণ।"
"1805","You might want to close the curtains.","āpuni hayto pardābor bandha karib bicārib.","আপুনি হয়তো পৰ্দাবোৰ বন্ধ কৰিব বিচাৰিব।"
"1806","She has not taken out the trash yet.","tāi etiyāo āwarjanā uliyāi diyā nāi.","তাই এতিয়াও আৱৰ্জনা উলিয়াই দিয়া নাই।"
"1807","The hallway needs to be swept.","halweṭo jhāṛu diyār prayojan.","হলৱেটো ঝাড়ু দিয়াৰ প্ৰয়োজন।"
"1808","Were we supposed to bring anything?","āmi kibā ānib lāgichil neki?","আমি কিবা আনিব লাগিছিল নেকি?"
"1809","I always double-check the locks before bed.","xowār āgte xadāy talābor dubār parīkxā kari laoṃ.","শোৱাৰ আগতে সদায় তলাবোৰ দুবাৰ পৰীক্ষা কৰি লওঁ।"
"1810","Somebody had been using my toothbrush.","konobāi mor ṭuthabrāch byawhār kari āhichil.","কোনোবাই মোৰ টুথব্ৰাছ ব্যৱহাৰ কৰি আহিছিল।"
"1811","We both ordered the same thing.","āmi duyo ekeṭā baxtur arḍār diloṃ.","আমি দুয়ো একেটা বস্তুৰ অৰ্ডাৰ দিলোঁ।"
"1812","I had been craving chocolate all week.","goṭei xaptāhṭo cakleṭar bābe hāhākār kari āchilo.","গোটেই সপ্তাহটো চকলেটৰ বাবে হাহাকাৰ কৰি আছিলো।"
"1813","Several plates were broken during the party.","pārṭir xamyat keibākhno pleṭ bhāṅi yāy.","পাৰ্টিৰ সময়ত কেইবাখনো প্লেট ভাঙি যায়।"
"1814","You should try the local cuisine.","xthānīy khādyabor cexṭā kari cāb lāge.","স্থানীয় খাদ্যবোৰ চেষ্টা কৰি চাব লাগে।"
"1815","Neither of us liked the appetizer.","āmār duyore epeṭāijārṭo bhāl nālāgil.","আমাৰ দুয়োৰে এপেটাইজাৰটো ভাল নালাগিল।"
"1816","A few ingredients still need to be bought.","etiyāo keiṭāmān upādān kinib lāgib.","এতিয়াও কেইটামান উপাদান কিনিব লাগিব।"
"1817","I seldom cook with olive oil.","mai alibh ailere randhā-bṛhā khub kameihe karo.","মই অলিভ অইলেৰে ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া খুব কমেইহে কৰো।"
"1818","They will be hosting a dinner party this Saturday.","ei xanibāre teoṃloke āyojan karib ḍinār pārṭi.","এই শনিবাৰে তেওঁলোকে আয়োজন কৰিব ডিনাৰ পাৰ্টি।"
"1819","Most people prefer freshly ground coffee.","bechibhāg mānuhei xatejkai pihi lowā kaphi pachanda kare.","বেছিভাগ মানুহেই সতেজকৈ পিহি লোৱা কফি পছন্দ কৰে।"
"1820","I did not realize the oven was still on.","mai gam powā nāchilo ye abhenṭo etiyāo jbali āche.","মই গম পোৱা নাছিলো যে অভেনটো এতিয়াও জ্বলি আছে।"
"1821","We need to order more napkins.","āmi āru nepkin arḍār dib lāgib.","আমি আৰু নেপকিন অৰ্ডাৰ দিব লাগিব।"
"1822","All of the food was homemade.","xaklo khādya gharte banowā āchil.","সকলো খাদ্য ঘৰতে বনোৱা আছিল।"
"1823","You might want to add a pinch of pepper.","āpuni hayto ecikuṭ jalkīyā dib pāre.","আপুনি হয়তো এচিকুট জলকীয়া দিব পাৰে।"
"1824","She has not finished her dessert.","tāir ḍejārṭa xex howā nāi.","তাইৰ ডেজাৰ্ট শেষ হোৱা নাই।"
"1825","The kitchen needs to be cleaned after dinner.","rātir āhār khowār pichat pākgharṭo cāphā karib lāge.","ৰাতিৰ আহাৰ খোৱাৰ পিছত পাকঘৰটো চাফা কৰিব লাগে।"
"1826","Were we charged for the extra side?","atirikta pakxar bābe āmār parā cārja lowā haichil neki?","অতিৰিক্ত পক্ষৰ বাবে আমাৰ পৰা চাৰ্জ লোৱা হৈছিল নেকি?"
"1827","I always taste the soup before serving it.","xadāy cūpṭo paribexnar āgte xowād laoṃ.","সদায় চূপটো পৰিবেশনৰ আগতে সোৱাদ লওঁ।"
"1828","We both forgot our passports at home.","āmi duyo gharte pāchpa’rṭa pāhriloṃ","আমি দুয়ো ঘৰতে পাছপ’ৰ্ট পাহৰিলোঁ৷"
"1829","I had been dreaming of this trip for years.","bachar bachar dhari ei bhramṇar xapon dekhi āchiloṃ.","বছৰ বছৰ ধৰি এই ভ্ৰমণৰ সপোন দেখি আছিলোঁ।"
"1830","Several flights were canceled due to fog.","kuṃwlīr bābe bātil karā haichil keibākhno bimān.","কুঁৱলীৰ বাবে বাতিল কৰা হৈছিল কেইবাখনো বিমান।"
"1831","You should reserve a seat in advance.","āgtīyākai ekhan āxan xaṃrakxaṇ karib lāge.","আগতীয়াকৈ এখন আসন সংৰক্ষণ কৰিব লাগে।"
"1832","Neither of us had a map.","āmār duyore hātat māncitra nāchil.","আমাৰ দুয়োৰে হাতত মানচিত্ৰ নাছিল।"
"1833","A few items still need to be declared.","keiṭāmān baxtu etiyāo ghoxṇā karibalgīyā āche.","কেইটামান বস্তু এতিয়াও ঘোষণা কৰিবলগীয়া আছে।"
"1834","I rarely take the highway.","mai ghāipathṭo khub kameihe laoṃ.","মই ঘাইপথটো খুব কমেইহে লওঁ।"
"1835","They will be staying at a resort for a week.","exaptāh rijarṭa ekhnat thākib.","এসপ্তাহ ৰিজৰ্ট এখনত থাকিব।"
"1836","Most travelers carry a guidebook.","bechibhāg bhramaṇkārīye gāiḍbuk lai phure.","বেছিভাগ ভ্ৰমণকাৰীয়ে গাইডবুক লৈ ফুৰে।"
"1837","I did not expect such heavy traffic.","imān gadhur yān-jaṃṭ mai āxā karā nāchilo.","ইমান গধুৰ যান-জঁট মই আশা কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1838","We need to catch the six o'clock bus.","āmi chay bajār bāchkhan dharib lāgib.","আমি ছয় বজাৰ বাছখন ধৰিব লাগিব।"
"1839","All of the rooms were fully booked.","xaklobor koṭhāi xampūrṇa bukiṃ hai āchil.","সকলোবোৰ কোঠাই সম্পূৰ্ণ বুকিং হৈ আছিল।"
"1840","You might want to download an offline map.","āpuni eṭā aphlāin mep ḍāunloḍ karib bicārib pāre.","আপুনি এটা অফলাইন মেপ ডাউনলোড কৰিব বিচাৰিব পাৰে।"
"1841","She has not packed yet.","tāi etiyāo pek karā nāi.","তাই এতিয়াও পেক কৰা নাই।"
"1842","The luggage needs to be weighed.","lāgejar ojan karib lāgib.","লাগেজৰ ওজন কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1843","Were we overcharged for the taxi?","ṭekxir bābe āmār parā atirikta cārja lowā haichil neki?","টেক্সিৰ বাবে আমাৰ পৰা অতিৰিক্ত চাৰ্জ লোৱা হৈছিল নেকি?"
"1844","We both enjoy spending time with our children.","āmi duyo xantānar lagat xamay kaṭāblai bhāl pāoṃ.","আমি দুয়ো সন্তানৰ লগত সময় কটাবলৈ ভাল পাওঁ।"
"1845","I had been thinking about my childhood.","mor xaixwar kathā bhābi āchiloṃ.","মোৰ শৈশৱৰ কথা ভাবি আছিলোঁ।"
"1846","Several windows were cracked during the storm.","dhumuhār xamyat keibākhno khirikī phāṭi yāy.","ধুমুহাৰ সময়ত কেইবাখনো খিৰিকী ফাটি যায়।"
"1847","You should visit them more often.","āpuni teoṃlokar ocarlai adhik xaghnāi yowā ucit.","আপুনি তেওঁলোকৰ ওচৰলৈ অধিক সঘনাই যোৱা উচিত।"
"1848","Neither of my parents drives.","mor mā-deutā duyore konowei gāṛī calowā nāi.","মোৰ মা-দেউতা দুয়োৰে কোনোৱেই গাড়ী চলোৱা নাই।"
"1849","A few repairs still need to be made.","etiyāo keiṭāmān merāmti karibalgīyā āche.","এতিয়াও কেইটামান মেৰামতি কৰিবলগীয়া আছে।"
"1850","I rarely argue with my spouse.","mor patnīr lagat mai khub kameihe tarka karo.","মোৰ পত্নীৰ লগত মই খুব কমেইহে তৰ্ক কৰো।"
"1851","They will be celebrating their anniversary next month.","ahā māhat teoṃlokar bārxikī udyāpan karib.","অহা মাহত তেওঁলোকৰ বাৰ্ষিকী উদযাপন কৰিব।"
"1852","Most parents want the best for their children.","bechibhāg abhibhāwke nijar xantānar bābe uttam bicāre.","বেছিভাগ অভিভাৱকে নিজৰ সন্তানৰ বাবে উত্তম বিচাৰে।"
"1853","I did not know she had a twin.","mai nājānichilo tāir yamaj xantān āche.","মই নাজানিছিলো তাইৰ যমজ সন্তান আছে।"
"1854","We need to fix the leaking faucet.","āmi lik howā kalṭo ṭhik karib lāgib.","আমি লিক হোৱা কলটো ঠিক কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1855","All of their children attend the same school.","teoṃlokar xaklo la’rā-chowālīye ekekhan bidyālayte paṛhe.","তেওঁলোকৰ সকলো ল’ৰা-ছোৱালীয়ে একেখন বিদ্যালয়তে পঢ়ে।"
"1856","You might want to speak to her privately.","āpuni hayto tāir lagat byaktigatbhāwe kathā pātib bicārib.","আপুনি হয়তো তাইৰ লগত ব্যক্তিগতভাৱে কথা পাতিব বিচাৰিব।"
"1857","We both graduated from the same university.","āmi duyo ekekhan bixbabidyālyar parā xnātak ḍigrī lābh kariloṃ.","আমি দুয়ো একেখন বিশ্ববিদ্যালয়ৰ পৰা স্নাতক ডিগ্ৰী লাভ কৰিলোঁ।"
"1858","I had been considering a career change.","keriyār xalni karār kathā bhābi āchiloṃ.","কেৰিয়াৰ সলনি কৰাৰ কথা ভাবি আছিলোঁ।"
"1859","Several employees were promoted this quarter.","ei traimāxikat keibājno karmacārīk padonnati diyā haichil.","এই ত্ৰৈমাসিকত কেইবাজনো কৰ্মচাৰীক পদোন্নতি দিয়া হৈছিল।"
"1860","You should back up your work regularly.","āpuni niymitbhāwe āponār kāmar bekaāp la’b lāge.","আপুনি নিয়মিতভাৱে আপোনাৰ কামৰ বেকআপ ল’ব লাগে।"
"1861","Neither of the candidates qualified.","duyojan prārthīye yogyatā arjan karā nāchil.","দুয়োজন প্ৰাৰ্থীয়ে যোগ্যতা অৰ্জন কৰা নাছিল।"
"1862","A few mistakes still need to be corrected.","dui eṭā bhul etiyāo xudharṇi karib lāgib.","দুই এটা ভুল এতিয়াও শুধৰণি কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1863","I rarely work on weekends.","chuṭir dinat mai khub kameihe kām karo.","ছুটিৰ দিনত মই খুব কমেইহে কাম কৰো।"
"1864","They will be announcing the results next week.","ahā xaptāhat ghoxṇā karib phalāphal.","অহা সপ্তাহত ঘোষণা কৰিব ফলাফল।"
"1865","Most graduates find jobs within six months.","bechibhāg xnātak chātrai chamāhar bhitarte cākri bicāri pāy.","বেছিভাগ স্নাতক ছাত্ৰই ছমাহৰ ভিতৰতে চাকৰি বিচাৰি পায়।"
"1866","I did not understand the assignment.","mai ecāinmeṇṭaṭo buji nāpāloṃ.","মই এচাইনমেণ্টটো বুজি নাপালোঁ।"
"1867","We need to schedule a follow-up meeting.","āmi ekhan pha’la’-āp miṭiṃr xamay nirdhāraṇ karib lāgib.","আমি এখন ফ’ল’-আপ মিটিংৰ সময় নিৰ্ধাৰণ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1868","All of the applications were reviewed thoroughly.","xaklo ābedan puṃkhānupuṃkhbhāwe paryālocnā karā haichil.","সকলো আবেদন পুংখানুপুংখভাৱে পৰ্যালোচনা কৰা হৈছিল।"
"1869","You might want to reconsider your decision.","āpuni hayto āponār xiddhāntaṭo punar bibecnā karib bicārib.","আপুনি হয়তো আপোনাৰ সিদ্ধান্তটো পুনৰ বিবেচনা কৰিব বিচাৰিব।"
"1870","We both gasped at the view.","āmi duyo dr̥xyaṭo dekhi hāṃhiloṃ.","আমি দুয়ো দৃশ্যটো দেখি হাঁহিলোঁ।"
"1871","I had been watching the clouds build up.","ḍāwarbor jamā howā cāi āchiloṃ.","ডাৱৰবোৰ জমা হোৱা চাই আছিলোঁ।"
"1872","Several trees were uprooted by the storm.","keibākhno gach ubhāli pelāy dhumuhāt.","কেইবাখনো গছ উভালি পেলায় ধুমুহাত।"
"1873","You should wear sunscreen on sunny days.","ra’dar dinat chānaxkrīṇ byawhār karib lāge.","ৰ’দৰ দিনত ছানস্ক্ৰীণ ব্যৱহাৰ কৰিব লাগে।"
"1874","Neither the wind nor the cold stopped them.","batāh bā ṭhāṇḍāio xihaṃtak rakhāb parā nāchil.","বতাহ বা ঠাণ্ডাইও সিহঁতক ৰখাব পৰা নাছিল।"
"1875","A few animals still need to be relocated.","etiyāo keiṭāmān jīw-jantuk xthānāntarit karib lāgib.","এতিয়াও কেইটামান জীৱ-জন্তুক স্থানান্তৰিত কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1876","I rarely see deer in this part of the woods.","hābir ei aṃxat hariṇ khub kameihe dekhā pāoṃ.","হাবিৰ এই অংশত হৰিণ খুব কমেইহে দেখা পাওঁ।"
"1877","They will be planting trees this spring.","ei baxantate teoṃloke gachpuli ropaṇ karib.","এই বসন্ততে তেওঁলোকে গছপুলি ৰোপণ কৰিব।"
"1878","Most rivers flow toward the ocean.","bechibhāg nadī xāgrar phāle bai yāy.","বেছিভাগ নদী সাগৰৰ ফালে বৈ যায়।"
"1879","I did not think it would rain today.","āji barxuṇ ha’b buli bhabā nāchilo.","আজি বৰষুণ হ’ব বুলি ভবা নাছিলো।"
"1880","We need to reduce our carbon footprint.","āmi āmār kārban phuṭapriṇṭa hrāx karib lāgib.","আমি আমাৰ কাৰ্বন ফুটপ্ৰিণ্ট হ্ৰাস কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1881","All of the flowers in the garden have bloomed.","bārīkhnar xaklobor phul phuli uṭhiche.","বাৰীখনৰ সকলোবোৰ ফুল ফুলি উঠিছে।"
"1882","We both caught the flu.","āmār duyore phlu haichil.","আমাৰ দুয়োৰে ফ্লু হৈছিল।"
"1883","I had been feeling dizzy all morning.","goṭei rātipuwā mor mūr ghūrowā yen lāgichil.","গোটেই ৰাতিপুৱা মোৰ মূৰ ঘূৰোৱা যেন লাগিছিল।"
"1884","Several patients were discharged today.","āji keibājno rogīk gharlai yāblai diyā hay.","আজি কেইবাজনো ৰোগীক ঘৰলৈ যাবলৈ দিয়া হয়।"
"1885","You should drink more water.","āpuni adhik pānī khāb lāge.","আপুনি অধিক পানী খাব লাগে।"
"1886","Neither the pills nor the syrup helped.","baṛi bā cirāpeo eko xahāy karā nāchil.","বড়ি বা চিৰাপেও একো সহায় কৰা নাছিল।"
"1887","A few tests still need to be run.","etiyāo keiṭāmān parīkxā calāb lāgib.","এতিয়াও কেইটামান পৰীক্ষা চলাব লাগিব।"
"1888","I rarely get headaches.","mor mūrar bix khub kameihe hay.","মোৰ মূৰৰ বিষ খুব কমেইহে হয়।"
"1889","They will be monitoring her progress closely.","teoṃloke tāir agragti nibiṛbhāwe nirīkxaṇ karib.","তেওঁলোকে তাইৰ অগ্ৰগতি নিবিড়ভাৱে নিৰীক্ষণ কৰিব।"
"1890","Most injuries heal with time.","bechibhāg āghātei xamyar lage lage bhāl hay.","বেছিভাগ আঘাতেই সময়ৰ লগে লগে ভাল হয়।"
"1891","I did not sleep well last night.","yowā nixā mor ṭopni bhāl howā nāchil.","যোৱা নিশা মোৰ টোপনি ভাল হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1892","We need to make an appointment.","āmi epaiṇṭameṇṭa la’b lāgib.","আমি এপইণ্টমেণ্ট ল’ব লাগিব।"
"1893","All of the test results came back normal.","xaklo parīkxār phalāphal xbābhāwik hai āhil.","সকলো পৰীক্ষাৰ ফলাফল স্বাভাৱিক হৈ আহিল।"
"1894","We both agreed on the price.","āmi duyo dāmar kathāt ekmat ha’loṃ","আমি দুয়ো দামৰ কথাত একমত হ’লোঁ৷"
"1895","I had been looking for this item for weeks.","keibā xaptāh dhari ei baxtuṭo bicāri āchiloṃ.","কেইবা সপ্তাহ ধৰি এই বস্তুটো বিচাৰি আছিলোঁ।"
"1896","Several products were removed from the shelves.","xbelphar parā keibāṭāo xāmagrī āṃtrāi pelowā haichil.","শ্বেলফৰ পৰা কেইবাটাও সামগ্ৰী আঁতৰাই পেলোৱা হৈছিল।"
"1897","You should compare different brands.","āpuni bibhinna breṇḍar tulnā karib lāge.","আপুনি বিভিন্ন ব্ৰেণ্ডৰ তুলনা কৰিব লাগে।"
"1898","Neither the color nor the size was right.","raṃ bā ākāro ṭhik nāchil.","ৰং বা আকাৰো ঠিক নাছিল।"
"1899","A few charges still need to be disputed.","keiṭāmān abhiyogar oprat etiyāo bitarkar prayojan āche.","কেইটামান অভিযোগৰ ওপৰত এতিয়াও বিতৰ্কৰ প্ৰয়োজন আছে।"
"1900","I rarely use cash anymore.","mai āru nagad dhan khub kameihe byawhār karo.","মই আৰু নগদ ধন খুব কমেইহে ব্যৱহাৰ কৰো।"
"1901","They will be opening a new location next month.","ahā māhat natun xthān mukli karib.","অহা মাহত নতুন স্থান মুকলি কৰিব।"
"1902","Most customers prefer online shopping.","bechibhāg grāhke anlāin bajār karāṭo pachanda kare.","বেছিভাগ গ্ৰাহকে অনলাইন বজাৰ কৰাটো পছন্দ কৰে।"
"1903","I did not notice the extra charge.","atirikta cārjaṭo mai lakxya karā nāchilo.","অতিৰিক্ত চাৰ্জটো মই লক্ষ্য কৰা নাছিলো।"
"1904","We need to return this before the deadline.","āmi ei xamayxīmār āgtei ghūrāi dib lāgib.","আমি এই সময়সীমাৰ আগতেই ঘূৰাই দিব লাগিব।"
"1905","All of the items on sale were gone.","bikrīr xaklo baxtu nohowā hai ga’l.","বিক্ৰীৰ সকলো বস্তু নোহোৱা হৈ গ’ল।"
"1906","We both cried at the wedding.","āmi duyo biyāt kāndiloṃ.","আমি দুয়ো বিয়াত কান্দিলোঁ।"
"1907","I had been worrying about the results.","phalāphlar bābe cintā kari āchiloṃ.","ফলাফলৰ বাবে চিন্তা কৰি আছিলোঁ।"
"1908","Several friendships were lost over the argument.","ei tarkak lai keibāṭāo bandhutba herāi ga’l.","এই তৰ্কক লৈ কেইবাটাও বন্ধুত্ব হেৰাই গ’ল।"
"1909","You should express your feelings more openly.","āponār anubhawbor adhik muklikai prakāx karā ucit.","আপোনাৰ অনুভৱবোৰ অধিক মুকলিকৈ প্ৰকাশ কৰা উচিত।"
"1910","Neither apology nor explanation was enough.","kxamā bicrā bā bujāi diyāṭowei yathexṭa nāchil.","ক্ষমা বিচৰা বা বুজাই দিয়াটোৱেই যথেষ্ট নাছিল।"
"1911","A few issues still need to be addressed.","keiṭāmān bixay etiyāo xamādhān karib lāgib.","কেইটামান বিষয় এতিয়াও সমাধান কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1912","I rarely hold grudges.","mor kxobh khub kameihe thāke.","মোৰ ক্ষোভ খুব কমেইহে থাকে।"
"1913","They will be visiting us over the holidays.","bandhar dinat āmār ocarlai āhib.","বন্ধৰ দিনত আমাৰ ওচৰলৈ আহিব।"
"1914","Most disagreements can be resolved through communication.","bechibhāg matānaikya yogāyogar jariyte xamādhān karib pāri.","বেছিভাগ মতানৈক্য যোগাযোগৰ জৰিয়তে সমাধান কৰিব পাৰি।"
"1915","I did not mean what I said.","mai kowā kathākhini mai bujāb bicrā nāchilo.","মই কোৱা কথাখিনি মই বুজাব বিচৰা নাছিলো।"
"1916","We need to set boundaries.","āmi xīmā nirdhāraṇ karib lāgib.","আমি সীমা নিৰ্ধাৰণ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1917","All of us felt the same way.","āmār xaklore ekei anubhaw haichil.","আমাৰ সকলোৰে একেই অনুভৱ হৈছিল।"
"1918","We both laughed at the joke.","dhemāliṭot āmi duyo hāṃhi uṭhiloṃ.","ধেমালিটোত আমি দুয়ো হাঁহি উঠিলোঁ।"
"1919","I had been looking forward to this concert.","ei kancārṭaṭor bābe mai āgrahere bāṭ cāi āchilo.","এই কনচাৰ্টটোৰ বাবে মই আগ্ৰহেৰে বাট চাই আছিলো।"
"1920","Several shows were postponed indefinitely.","keibāṭāo xba’ anirdixṭakāllai pichuwāi diyā ha’l.","কেইবাটাও শ্ব’ অনিৰ্দিষ্টকাললৈ পিছুৱাই দিয়া হ’ল।"
"1921","You should try a different genre.","āpuni beleg eṭā dhārā cexṭā karā ucit.","আপুনি বেলেগ এটা ধাৰা চেষ্টা কৰা উচিত।"
"1922","Neither the plot nor the acting was convincing.","kāhinībhāg bā abhinyao patiyan yogya nāchil.","কাহিনীভাগ বা অভিনয়ও পতিয়ন যোগ্য নাছিল।"
"1923","A few scenes still need to be filmed.","dui eṭā dr̥xya etiyāo citragrahaṇ karib lāgib.","দুই এটা দৃশ্য এতিয়াও চিত্ৰগ্ৰহণ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1924","I rarely go to the cinema anymore.","mai āru cinemā cāblai khub kameihe yāoṃ.","মই আৰু চিনেমা চাবলৈ খুব কমেইহে যাওঁ।"
"1925","They will be streaming the event live.","teoṃloke ei anuxṭhānṭo lāibh xṭrīmiṃ karib.","তেওঁলোকে এই অনুষ্ঠানটো লাইভ ষ্ট্ৰীমিং কৰিব।"
"1926","Most critics praised the performance.","bechibhāg xamālocke ei abhinyak praxaṃxā kare.","বেছিভাগ সমালোচকে এই অভিনয়ক প্ৰশংসা কৰে।"
"1927","I did not enjoy the second act.","dbitīy abhinayṭo mor bhāl nālāgil.","দ্বিতীয় অভিনয়টো মোৰ ভাল নালাগিল।"
"1928","We need to reserve tickets early.","āmi xonkāle ṭikaṭ rijārbha karib lāgib.","আমি সোনকালে টিকট ৰিজাৰ্ভ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1929","All of the seats in the front row were taken.","āgxārīr xaklo āxan lowā ha’l.","আগশাৰীৰ সকলো আসন লোৱা হ’ল।"
"1930","We both signed the petition.","āmi duyojne ābednakhnat cahī kariloṃ.","আমি দুয়োজনে আবেদনখনত চহী কৰিলোঁ।"
"1931","I had been meaning to reach out to you.","mai āponār ocarlai hāt āgabṛhowār artha āchiloṃ.","মই আপোনাৰ ওচৰলৈ হাত আগবঢ়োৱাৰ অৰ্থ আছিলোঁ।"
"1932","Several rumors were spread online.","anlāinat biypi paril keibāṭāo urābātri.","অনলাইনত বিয়পি পৰিল কেইবাটাও উৰাবাতৰি।"
"1933","You should speak up if you disagree.","matānaikya thākile māt mātib lāge.","মতানৈক্য থাকিলে মাত মাতিব লাগে।"
"1934","Neither his email nor his call was returned.","teoṃr imeil bā teoṃr phonṭoo uttar diyā naha’l.","তেওঁৰ ইমেইল বা তেওঁৰ ফোনটোও উত্তৰ দিয়া নহ’ল।"
"1935","A few details still need to be clarified.","keiṭāmān xabixex etiyāo xpaxṭa karār prayojan āche.","কেইটামান সবিশেষ এতিয়াও স্পষ্ট কৰাৰ প্ৰয়োজন আছে।"
"1936","I rarely post on social media.","caciyel miḍiyāt khub kameihe poxṭa karo.","চচিয়েল মিডিয়াত খুব কমেইহে পোষ্ট কৰো।"
"1937","They will be launching a new campaign next quarter.","ahā traimāxikat natun abhiyān ārambha karib.","অহা ত্ৰৈমাসিকত নতুন অভিযান আৰম্ভ কৰিব।"
"1938","Most meetings last about an hour.","bechibhāg xabhāi prāy eghaṇṭā xamay cale.","বেছিভাগ সভাই প্ৰায় এঘণ্টা সময় চলে।"
"1939","I did not catch her name.","mai tāir nām dharib parā nāchilo.","মই তাইৰ নাম ধৰিব পৰা নাছিলো।"
"1940","We need to clear up this misunderstanding.","ei bhul bujābuji āmi parixkār karib lāgib.","এই ভুল বুজাবুজি আমি পৰিষ্কাৰ কৰিব লাগিব।"
"1941","All of the invitations have been sent.","xaklo nimantraṇī patra preraṇ karā haiche.","সকলো নিমন্ত্ৰণী পত্ৰ প্ৰেৰণ কৰা হৈছে।"
"1942","Someone knocked on the door while I was napping.","mai ṭopni yowār xamyat konobāi duwārat ṭokar mārile.","মই টোপনি যোৱাৰ সময়ত কোনোবাই দুৱাৰত টোকৰ মাৰিলে।"
"1943","We take turns doing the dishes.","āmi pāl pāti bācan-bartanbor kari thākoṃ.","আমি পাল পাতি বাচন-বৰ্তনবোৰ কৰি থাকোঁ।"
"1944","The mail had not been delivered yet.","meilkhan etiyāo ḍelibhārī howā nāchil.","মেইলখন এতিয়াও ডেলিভাৰী হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1945","What time do you usually get up?","xādhāraṇte kimān bajāt uṭhe?","সাধাৰণতে কিমান বজাত উঠে?"
"1946","I wish I had more free time.","mor āru ājri xamay thākile bhāl āchil.","মোৰ আৰু আজৰি সময় থাকিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1947","We are supposed to recycle these bottles.","āmi ei baṭalbor punar byawhār karār kathā.","আমি এই বটলবোৰ পুনৰ ব্যৱহাৰ কৰাৰ কথা।"
"1948","Someone left the refrigerator door open.","konobāi phrījar duwārkhan kholākai thai ga’l.","কোনোবাই ফ্ৰীজৰ দুৱাৰখন খোলাকৈ থৈ গ’ল।"
"1949","We take turns cooking on weeknights.","āmi xaptāhar nixā pāl pāti randhā-bṛhā karo.","আমি সপ্তাহৰ নিশা পাল পাতি ৰন্ধা-বঢ়া কৰো।"
"1950","The meat had not been thawed in time.","xamyat māṃx gali yowā nāchil.","সময়ত মাংস গলি যোৱা নাছিল।"
"1951","What kind of wine goes with fish?","māchar lagat kenedharṇar mad yāy?","মাছৰ লগত কেনেধৰণৰ মদ যায়?"
"1952","I wish I could eat gluten.","gluṭen khāb pārile bhāl āchil.","গ্লুটেন খাব পাৰিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1953","We are supposed to eat five servings of fruit per day.","āmi pratidine pāṃcṭākai phal khowār kathā.","আমি প্ৰতিদিনে পাঁচটাকৈ ফল খোৱাৰ কথা।"
"1954","Someone cut in line at the gate.","konobāi geṭat xārī kāṭile.","কোনোবাই গেটত শাৰী কাটিলে।"
"1955","We take turns driving on road trips.","āmi roḍ ṭripat pāl pāti gāṛī calāoṃ.","আমি ৰোড ট্ৰিপত পাল পাতি গাড়ী চলাওঁ।"
"1956","The departure had not been announced yet.","praxthānar kathā etiyāo ghoxṇā karā howā nāchil.","প্ৰস্থানৰ কথা এতিয়াও ঘোষণা কৰা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1957","What time does the last train leave?","xexarkhan rel kimān bajāt yātrā kare?","শেষৰখন ৰেল কিমান বজাত যাত্ৰা কৰে?"
"1958","I wish I had brought my camera.","mor kemerāṭo lai ahā ha’le bhāl āchil.","মোৰ কেমেৰাটো লৈ অহা হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"1959","We are supposed to check out by eleven.","āmi eghār bajār bhitrat cek āuṭ karār kathā.","আমি এঘাৰ বজাৰ ভিতৰত চেক আউট কৰাৰ কথা।"
"1960","Someone left a message on the answering machine.","konobāi ānxār mecinat mechej eri thai ga’l.","কোনোবাই আন্সাৰ মেচিনত মেছেজ এৰি থৈ গ’ল।"
"1961","We take turns walking the dog.","āmi pāl pāti kukurṭok khoj kāṛhi phuro.","আমি পাল পাতি কুকুৰটোক খোজ কাঢ়ি ফুৰো।"
"1962","The invitation had not been sent yet.","nimantraṇī patra etiyāo paṭhowā howā nāchil.","নিমন্ত্ৰণী পত্ৰ এতিয়াও পঠোৱা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1963","How old is your youngest child?","āponār xaru xantānar bayax kimān?","আপোনাৰ সৰু সন্তানৰ বয়স কিমান?"
"1964","I wish my siblings lived closer to me.","mor bhāi-bhnīxkal mor ocrat thākile bhāl āchil.","মোৰ ভাই-ভনীসকল মোৰ ওচৰত থাকিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1965","We are supposed to visit grandma this Sunday.","ei deobāre āmi āitākak lag karār kathā.","এই দেওবাৰে আমি আইতাকক লগ কৰাৰ কথা।"
"1966","Someone spilled coffee on the keyboard.","konobāi kibarḍat kaphi ḍhāli dile.","কোনোবাই কিবৰ্ডত কফি ঢালি দিলে।"
"1967","We take turns leading the meetings.","āmi pāl pāti xabhāborar netr̥tba dioṃ.","আমি পাল পাতি সভাবোৰৰ নেতৃত্ব দিওঁ।"
"1968","The invoice had not been approved yet.","cālānkhan etiyāo anumodan howā nāchil.","চালানখন এতিয়াও অনুমোদন হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1969","What qualifications are required for this position?","ei padar bābe ki ki arhatār prayojan?","এই পদৰ বাবে কি কি অৰ্হতাৰ প্ৰয়োজন?"
"1970","I wish I had chosen a different major.","mai yadi beleg eṭā mejar bāchi laichilo tente bhāl āchil.","মই যদি বেলেগ এটা মেজৰ বাছি লৈছিলো তেন্তে ভাল আছিল।"
"1971","We are supposed to submit monthly reports.","āmi māhekīyā pratibedan jamā diyār kathā.","আমি মাহেকীয়া প্ৰতিবেদন জমা দিয়াৰ কথা।"
"1972","Someone reported a bear sighting.","konobāi bhāluk dekhār khabar dile.","কোনোবাই ভালুক দেখাৰ খবৰ দিলে।"
"1973","We take turns carrying the equipment.","āmi pāl pāti xaṃjulibor kaṛhiyāi lai phuro.","আমি পাল পাতি সঁজুলিবোৰ কঢ়িয়াই লৈ ফুৰো।"
"1974","The warning had not been issued in time.","xamyat ei xatarkabāṇī jāri karā howā nāchil.","সময়ত এই সতৰ্কবাণী জাৰি কৰা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1975","What is the temperature outside?","bāhirar uxṇatā kimān?","বাহিৰৰ উষ্ণতা কিমান?"
"1976","I wish it would stop raining.","barxuṇ bandha hai ga'le bhāl āchil.","বৰষুণ বন্ধ হৈ গ'লে ভাল আছিল।"
"1977","We are supposed to stay on the marked trail.","āmi cihnit ṭreilat thakār kathā.","আমি চিহ্নিত ট্ৰেইলত থকাৰ কথা।"
"1978","Someone fainted in the waiting room.","weṭiṃ rumat konobā ajñān hai paril.","ৱেটিং ৰুমত কোনোবা অজ্ঞান হৈ পৰিল।"
"1979","We take turns staying with the patient.","āmi pāl pāti rogīr lagat thākim.","আমি পাল পাতি ৰোগীৰ লগত থাকিম।"
"1980","The diagnosis had not been confirmed yet.","etiyāo nixcit howā nāchil rog nirṇay.","এতিয়াও নিশ্চিত হোৱা নাছিল ৰোগ নিৰ্ণয়।"
"1981","What are the possible side effects?","hr̥dyantrar xambhāwya pārxbakriyā?","হৃদযন্ত্ৰৰ সম্ভাৱ্য পাৰ্শ্বক্ৰিয়া?"
"1982","I wish I had started exercising earlier.","āgtei byāyām kariblai ārambha karā ha’le bhāl āchil.","আগতেই ব্যায়াম কৰিবলৈ আৰম্ভ কৰা হ’লে ভাল আছিল।"
"1983","We are supposed to fast before the blood test.","āmi tejar parīkxār āgat upbāxe rakhār kathā.","আমি তেজৰ পৰীক্ষাৰ আগত উপবাসে ৰখাৰ কথা।"
"1984","Someone left their shopping bags at the counter.","konobāi teoṃlokar xbapiṃ begbor kāuṇṭārat thai gaichil.","কোনোবাই তেওঁলোকৰ শ্বপিং বেগবোৰ কাউণ্টাৰত থৈ গৈছিল।"
"1985","We take turns paying for groceries.","āmi pāl pāti gelāmālar bābe dhan dioṃ.","আমি পাল পাতি গেলামালৰ বাবে ধন দিওঁ।"
"1986","The payment had not been received yet.","etiyāo dhan lābh karā howā nāchil.","এতিয়াও ধন লাভ কৰা হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1987","What is the return policy?","riṭārṇa palici ki?","ৰিটাৰ্ণ পলিচি কি?"
"1988","I wish I had bought it when it was cheaper.","xaxtā ha’le kinile bhāl āchil.","সস্তা হ’লে কিনিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1989","We are supposed to tip at least fifteen percent.","āmi kameo pondhar xatāṃx ṭip diyār kathā.","আমি কমেও পোন্ধৰ শতাংশ টিপ দিয়াৰ কথা।"
"1990","Someone mentioned your name at the party.","konobāi pārṭit tomār nām ullekh karichil.","কোনোবাই পাৰ্টিত তোমাৰ নাম উল্লেখ কৰিছিল।"
"1991","We take turns listening to each other.","āmi pāl pāti ijne xijnar kathā xuni thākoṃ.","আমি পাল পাতি ইজনে সিজনৰ কথা শুনি থাকোঁ।"
"1992","The truth had not been revealed yet.","xatya etiyāo prakāx powā nāchil.","সত্য এতিয়াও প্ৰকাশ পোৱা নাছিল।"
"1993","I wish I could turn back time.","xamay pichlai ghūrāi dib pārile bhāl āchil.","সময় পিছলৈ ঘূৰাই দিব পাৰিলে ভাল আছিল।"
"1994","We are supposed to forgive and forget.","āmi kxamā kari pāhri yowār kathā.","আমি ক্ষমা কৰি পাহৰি যোৱাৰ কথা।"
"1995","Someone left a great review.","konobāi eṭā ḍāṅar ribhiu eri thai ga’l.","কোনোবাই এটা ডাঙৰ ৰিভিউ এৰি থৈ গ’ল।"
"1996","We take turns choosing the movie.","āmi pāl pāti cinemākhan bāchi laoṃ.","আমি পাল পাতি চিনেমাখন বাছি লওঁ।"
"1997","The venue had not been confirmed yet.","ei xthān etiyāo nixcit howā nāchil.","এই স্থান এতিয়াও নিশ্চিত হোৱা নাছিল।"
"1998","What is the dress code for the event?","anuxṭhānṭor xāj-pochāk ki?","অনুষ্ঠানটোৰ সাজ-পোছাক কি?"
"1999","I wish I could play the piano.","piyāno bajāb pārile bhāl āchil.","পিয়ানো বজাব পাৰিলে ভাল আছিল।"
